## Employment Insurance Act {#employment-insurance-act .Title-of-Act}

[S.C.]{.abbr title="Statutes of Canada"} 1996, c. 23
:::

Assented to 1996-06-20

An Act respecting employment insurance in Canada
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: Enacts
Her Majesty, by and with the advice and consent of the Senate and House
of Commons of Canada, enacts as follows:
:::

## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-215680 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6).

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-215683 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this
  Act,

  [[affidavit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[affidavit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an affidavit sworn or
      affirmed before a commissioner of oaths or any other person
      authorized to take affidavits; ([affidavit]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[benefit period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[benefit period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the period described
      in sections 9, 10, 152.1 and 152.11; ([période de
      prestations]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means unemployment benefits
      payable under Part I, VII.1 or
      VIII; ([prestation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[board of referees]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[board of referees]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2012, c.
      19, s. 240\]]{.Repealed}

  [[claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who applies or has
      applied for benefits under this
      Act; ([prestataire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Commission]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Commission]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Canada Employment
      Insurance Commission; ([Commission]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[common-law partner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to an
      individual, means a person who is cohabiting with the individual
      in a conjugal relationship, having so cohabited for a period of at
      least one year; ([conjoint de fait]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[confirmed delivery service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[confirmed delivery service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means certified
      or registered mail or any other delivery service that provides
      proof of delivery; ([service de messagerie]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[documents]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[documents]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes money, securities,
      books, records, letters, telegrams, vouchers, invoices, accounts
      and statements (financial or
      otherwise); ([documents]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[dwelling-house]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[dwelling-house]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the whole or any part
      of a building or structure that is kept or occupied as a permanent
      or temporary residence and includes

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a building within the yard of a dwelling-house
        that is connected to it by a doorway or by a covered and
        enclosed passageway, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a unit that is designed to be mobile and to be
        used as a permanent or temporary residence and that is being
        used as a residence; ([maison d'habitation]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[employee's premium]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[employee's premium]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the premium that
      a person employed in insurable employment is required to pay under
      section 67; ([cotisation ouvrière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[employer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[employer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a person who has been an
      employer and, in respect of remuneration of an individual referred
      to as sponsor or co-ordinator of a project in paragraph 5(1)(e),
      it includes that individual; ([employeur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[employer's premium]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[employer's premium]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the premium that
      an employer of an insured person is required to pay under section
      68; ([cotisation patronale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[employment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[employment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the act of employing or
      the state of being employed; ([emploi]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[employment benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[employment benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2022, c.
      10, s. 387\]]{.Repealed}

  [[employment support measure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[employment support measure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a measure
      established under section 59; ([mesure de soutien à
      l'emploi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[insurable earnings]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[insurable earnings]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the total amount
      of the earnings, as determined in accordance with Part IV, that an
      insured person has from insurable employment; ([rémunération
      assurable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[insurable employment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[insurable employment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning
      assigned by section 5; ([emploi assurable]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[insured person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[insured person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is or
      has been employed in insurable
      employment; ([assuré]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[interruption of earnings]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[interruption of earnings]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      interruption that occurs in the earnings of an insured person or a
      person to whom Part VII.1 applies at any time and in any
      circumstances determined by the regulations; ([arrêt de
      rémunération]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[labour dispute]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[labour dispute]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a dispute between
      employers and employees, or between employees and employees, that
      is connected with the employment or non-employment, or the terms
      or conditions of employment, of any persons; ([conflit
      collectif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[long-tenured worker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[long-tenured worker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2016, c.
      7, s. 207\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of Employment
      and Social Development, except in Parts IV and
      VII; ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[overpayment of benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[overpayment of benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} does not include a
      benefit repayment as described in Part VII; ([versement
      excédentaire de prestations]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means prescribed by the
      regulations or determined in accordance with rules prescribed by
      the regulations; ([Version anglaise seulement]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[provincial law]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[provincial law]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the provisions of any
      Act of the legislature of a province authorizing, or entitling a
      person to, the payment of benefits under a plan established by or
      under that Act; ([loi provinciale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[rate of unemployment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[rate of unemployment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the rate of
      unemployment as determined from time to time in a year; ([taux de
      chômage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[regular benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[regular benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means benefits payable
      under Part I and Part VIII, but does not include special benefits
      or benefits by virtue of section 24 or 25; ([prestations
      régulières]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[special benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[special benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means benefits paid for
      any reason mentioned in subsection 12(3) or
      152.14(1); ([prestations spéciales]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[umpire]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[umpire]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2012, c. 19, s.
      240\]]{.Repealed}

  [[week]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[week]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a period of seven consecutive
      days beginning on and including Sunday, or any other prescribed
      period; ([semaine]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a calendar
      year. ([année]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rates of unemployment produced by
  Statistics Canada

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the use of rates of unemployment produced by
  Statistics Canada is required under this Act or the regulations, the
  Commission shall use those most recently produced at the time it is
  appropriate or necessary for the Commission to make a final
  determination in respect of those rates or involving their use.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic documents and communication

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A document or other communication under this Act or
  the regulations may be in electronic form and a reference in this Act
  or the regulations to a form, record, book, notice, request, demand,
  decision or any other document includes a document in electronic form.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}References to claims for benefits

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this Act and the regulations, references to claims
  for benefits include questions arising in relation to those claims,
  and references to action on a claim include determining questions in
  favour of or adversely to claimants.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Weeks of benefits paid

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 145, the Commission may,
  with the approval of the Governor in Council, make regulations for
  establishing how many weeks of benefits a claimant was paid, in order
  to take into account benefit reductions or deductions in the
  calculation or payment of those benefits.

## [Report]{.HTitleText1} {#h-215730 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commission to assess adjustment

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission shall monitor and assess the impact and effectiveness, for
  individuals, communities and the economy, of the benefits and other
  assistance provided under this Act, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} how the benefits and assistance are utilized by
    employees and employers, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the effect of the benefits and assistance on the
    obligation of claimants to be available for and to seek employment
    and on the efforts of employers to maintain a stable workforce.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission shall report to the Minister on its
  assessment annually no later than March 31 following the end of a
  year. The Commission shall make any additional reports at any other
  times, as the Minister may request.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tabling in Parliament

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall lay each report before Parliament
  within 30 days after receiving it or, if Parliament is not then
  sitting, on any of the first 30 days that either House of Parliament
  is sitting after it is received.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Referral to committee

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Each report shall be referred to such committee of
  the House of Commons as may be designated or established by the House
  for that purpose.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 3
- 2001, c. 5, s. 2
- 2008, c. 28, s. 124
:::

## [Maximum Yearly Insurable Earnings]{.HTitleText1} {#h-215744 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum yearly insurable earnings

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of subsection 14(1.1), section 17, subsection 82(2) and
  sections 95 and 145, the maximum yearly insurable earnings is \$39,000
  until the amount calculated in accordance with subsection (2) for a
  year, before rounding down under subsection (4), exceeds \$39,000, in
  which case the maximum yearly insurable earnings for that year is that
  amount, rounded down under subsection (4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation of amount

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount referred to in subsection (1) is the
  amount equal to 52 times the product obtained by multiplying

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the average for the 12-month period ending on April
    30 in the preceding year of the Average Weekly Earnings for each
    month in that period

  by

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the ratio that the average for the 12-month period
    ending on April 30 in that preceding year of the Average Weekly
    Earnings for each month in that 12-month period bears to the average
    for the 12-month period ending 12 months prior to April 30 of that
    preceding year of the Average Weekly Earnings for each month in that
    12-month period ending 12 months prior to April 30 of that preceding
    year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsequent years

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For years subsequent to the year in which the maximum
  yearly insurable earnings exceeds \$39,000, before rounding down under
  subsection (4), the maximum yearly insurable earnings is the maximum
  yearly insurable earnings for the preceding year, before rounding down
  under that subsection, multiplied by the ratio that the average for
  the 12-month period ending on April 30 in that preceding year of the
  Average Weekly Earnings for each month in that 12-month period bears
  to the average for the 12-month period ending 12 months prior to April
  30 of that preceding year of the Average Weekly Earnings for each
  month in that 12-month period ending 12 months prior to April 30 of
  that preceding year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding down

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the amount calculated in accordance with
  subsection (2) or (3) is not a multiple of one hundred dollars, the
  amount of the maximum yearly insurable earnings is rounded down to the
  nearest multiple of one hundred dollars.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Average Weekly Earnings

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Average Weekly Earnings for a month is the
  average weekly earnings of the Industrial Aggregate in Canada for the
  month as published by Statistics Canada under the authority of the
  [Statistics Act](/eng/acts/S-19).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 4
- 2001, c. 5, s. 3
- 2012, c. 19, s. 603, c. 31, s. 433
- 2013, c. 40, s. 125
:::

Version](section-4-20130307.html "Link to previous version of section 4"){.PITLink}
:::

## [Insurable Employment]{.HTitleText1} {#h-215761 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Types of insurable employment

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection (2), insurable employment is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} employment in Canada by one or more employers,
    under any express or implied contract of service or apprenticeship,
    written or oral, whether the earnings of the employed person are
    received from the employer or some other person and whether the
    earnings are calculated by time or by the piece, or partly by time
    and partly by the piece, or otherwise;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} employment in Canada as described in paragraph (a)
    by Her Majesty in right of Canada;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} service in the Canadian Forces or in a police
    force;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} employment included by regulations made under
    subsection (4) or (5); and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} employment in Canada of an individual as the
    sponsor or co-ordinator of an employment support measure other than
    one referred to in paragraph 59(c) or (d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded employment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Insurable employment does not include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} employment of a casual nature other than for the
    purpose of the employer's trade or business;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the employment of a person by a corporation if the
    person controls more than 40% of the voting shares of the
    corporation;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} employment in Canada by Her Majesty in right of a
    province;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} employment in Canada by the government of a country
    other than Canada or of any political subdivision of the other
    country;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} employment in Canada by an international
    organization;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} employment in Canada under an exchange program if
    the employment is not remunerated by an employer that is resident in
    Canada;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} employment that constitutes an exchange of work or
    services;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} employment excluded by regulations made under
    subsection (6); and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} employment if the employer and employee are not
    dealing with each other at arm's length.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Arm's length dealing

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (2)(i),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the question of whether persons are not dealing
    with each other at arm's length shall be determined in accordance
    with the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the employer is, within the meaning of that Act,
    related to the employee, they are deemed to deal with each other at
    arm's length if the Minister of National Revenue is satisfied that,
    having regard to all the circumstances of the employment, including
    the remuneration paid, the terms and conditions, the duration and
    the nature and importance of the work performed, it is reasonable to
    conclude that they would have entered into a substantially similar
    contract of employment if they had been dealing with each other at
    arm's length.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations to include employment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may, with the approval of the Governor
  in Council, make regulations for including in insurable employment

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} employment outside Canada or partly outside Canada
    that would be insurable employment if it were in Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the entire employment of a person who is engaged by
    one employer partly in insurable employment and partly in other
    employment;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} employment that is not employment under a contract
    of service if it appears to the Commission that the terms and
    conditions of service of, and the nature of the work performed by,
    persons employed in that employment are similar to the terms and
    conditions of service of, and the nature of the work performed by,
    persons employed under a contract of service;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} employment in Canada by Her Majesty in right of a
    province if the government of the province waives exclusion and
    agrees to insure all its employees engaged in that employment;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} employment in Canada by the government of a country
    other than Canada or of any political subdivision of the other
    country if the employing government consents;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} employment in Canada by an international
    organization if the organization consents; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the tenure of an office as defined in subsection
    2(1) of the [Canada Pension Plan](/eng/acts/C-8).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations to include persons in
  business

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may, with the approval of the Governor
  in Council and subject to affirmative resolution of Parliament, make
  regulations for including in insurable employment the business
  activities of a person who is engaged in a business, as defined in
  subsection 248(1) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations to exclude employment

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may, with the approval of the Governor
  in Council, make regulations for excluding from insurable employment

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any employment if it appears to the Commission that
    because of the laws of a country other than Canada a duplication of
    contributions or benefits will result;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the entire employment of a person who is engaged by
    one employer partly in insurable employment and partly in other
    employment;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any employment if it appears to the Commission that
    the nature of the work performed by persons employed in that
    employment is similar to the nature of the work performed by persons
    employed in employment that is not insurable employment;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the employment of a member of a religious order who
    has taken a vow of poverty and whose remuneration is paid directly
    or by the member to the order;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any employment in which persons are employed hardly
    at all or for nominal remuneration; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any employment provided under regulations made
    under section 24 or under an employment support measure other than
    one referred to in paragraph 59(c) or (d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Defining certain expressions

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may, with the approval of the Governor
  in Council, make regulations defining, for the purposes of this
  section, the expressions [[casual nature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},
  [[government]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a government of a
  country other than Canada or of a political subdivision of the other
  country, and [[international organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 5
- [2022, c. 10, s. 388](#1357835-1366634){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357835-1366634.html"}
:::

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[Unemployment Benefits]{.HTitleText1} {#h-215806 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-215807 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this
  Part,

  [[disentitled]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[disentitled]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means not entitled under
      section 13, 18, 21, 31, 32, 33, 36, 37, 49 or 50 or under the
      regulations; ([inadmissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[disqualified]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[disqualified]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means disqualified under
      section 27 or 30; ([exclu du bénéfice des
      prestations]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[initial claim for benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[initial claim for benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a claim
      made for the purpose of establishing a claimant's benefit
      period; ([demande initiale de prestations]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[major attachment claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[major attachment claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a claimant
      who qualifies to receive benefits and has 600 or more hours of
      insurable employment in their qualifying period; ([prestataire de
      la première catégorie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[minor attachment claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[minor attachment claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a claimant
      who qualifies to receive benefits and has fewer than 600 hours of
      insurable employment in their qualifying period; ([prestataire de
      la deuxième catégorie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[qualifying period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualifying period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the period
      described in section 8; ([période de référence]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[waiting period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[waiting period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the one week of the
      benefit period described in section 13. ([délai de
      carence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding off percentages or fractions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A reference in this Part to an amount equal to a
  percentage or fraction of earnings or benefits in a period shall be
  rounded to the nearest multiple of one dollar or, if the amount is
  equidistant from two multiples of one dollar, to the higher multiple.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hours of insurable employment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, the number of hours of
  insurable employment that a claimant has in any period shall be
  established as provided under section 55, subject to any regulations
  made under paragraph 54(z.1) allocating the hours to the claimant's
  qualifying period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Employment not suitable

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraphs 18(1)(a) and 27(1)(a)
  to (c) and subsection 50(8), employment is not suitable employment for
  a claimant if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it arises in consequence of a work stoppage
    attributable to a labour dispute;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is in the claimant's usual occupation and is
    either at a lower rate of earnings or on conditions less favourable
    than those observed by agreement between employers and employees or,
    in the absence of any such agreement, than those recognized by good
    employers; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is not in the claimant's usual occupation and is
    either at a lower rate of earnings or on conditions less favourable
    than those that the claimant might reasonably expect to obtain,
    having regard to the conditions that the claimant usually obtained
    in their usual occupation, or would have obtained if they had
    continued to be so employed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reasonable interval

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} After a lapse of a reasonable interval from the date
  on which an insured person becomes unemployed, paragraph (4)(c) does
  not apply to the employment described in that paragraph if it is
  employment at a rate of earnings not lower and on conditions not less
  favourable than those observed by agreement between employers and
  employees or, in the absence of any such agreement, than those
  recognized by good employers.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 6
- 2000, c. 14, s. 2
- 2016, c. 7, s. 208, c. 12, s. 101

### [Qualifying for Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-215831 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefits payable to persons who qualify

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unemployment benefits are payable as
  provided in this Part to an insured person who qualifies to receive
  them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualification requirement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An insured person qualifies if the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has had an interruption of earnings from
    employment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has had during their qualifying period at least the
    number of hours of insurable employment set out in the following
    table in relation to the regional rate of unemployment that applies
    to the person.

- [(3) to (5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2016, c. 7, s. 209\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other benefit rights --- Canada-U.S.
  agreement

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} An insured person is not qualified to receive
  benefits if it is jointly determined that the insured person must
  first exhaust or end benefit rights under the laws of another
  jurisdiction, as provided by Article VI of the Agreement Between
  Canada and the United States Respecting Unemployment Insurance, signed
  on March 6 and 12, 1942.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Increase in required hours

- **[[7.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  number of hours that an insured person requires under section 7 to
  qualify for benefits is increased to the number set out in the
  following table in relation to the applicable regional rate of
  unemployment if the insured person accumulates one or more violations
  in the 260 weeks before making their initial claim for benefits.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2016, c. 7, s. 210\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed violation

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} A violation accumulated by an individual under
  section 152.07 is deemed to be a violation accumulated by the
  individual under this section on the day on which the notice of
  violation was given to the individual.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A violation may not be taken into account under
  subsection (1) in more than two initial claims for benefits under this
  Act by an individual if the individual who accumulated the violation
  qualified for benefits in each of those two initial claims, taking
  into account subsection (1), subparagraph 152.07(1)(d)(ii) or
  regulations made under Part VIII, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Violations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An insured person accumulates a violation if in any
  of the following circumstances the Commission issues a notice of
  violation to the person:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} one or more penalties are imposed on the person
    under section 38, 39, 41.1 or 65.1, as a result of acts or omissions
    mentioned in section 38, 39 or 65.1;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is found guilty of one or more offences
    under section 135 or 136 as a result of acts or omissions mentioned
    in those sections; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person is found guilty of one or more offences
    under the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46) as a result of acts or
    omissions relating to the application of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Classification of violations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Except for violations for which a warning was
  imposed, each violation is classified as a minor, serious, very
  serious or subsequent violation as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the value of the violation is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} less than \$1,000, it is a minor violation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} \$1,000 or more, but less than \$5,000, it is a
      serious violation, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} \$5,000 or more, it is a very serious
      violation; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the notice of violation is issued within 260
    weeks after the person accumulates another violation, it is a
    subsequent violation, even if the acts or omissions on which it is
    based occurred before the person accumulated the other violation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Value of violations

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The value of a violation is the total of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the overpayment of benefits resulting
    from the acts or omissions on which the violation is based, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the claimant is disqualified or disentitled from
    receiving benefits, or the act or omission on which the violation is
    based relates to qualification requirements under section 7, the
    amount determined, subject to subsection (7), by multiplying the
    claimant's weekly rate of benefit by the average number of weeks of
    regular benefits, as determined under the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The maximum amount to be determined under paragraph
  (6)(b) is the amount of benefits that could have been paid to the
  claimant if the claimant had not been disentitled or disqualified or
  had met the qualification requirements under section 7.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying period

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsections (2) to (7), the qualifying period of an insured person
  is the shorter of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the 52-week period immediately before the beginning
    of a benefit period under subsection 10(1), and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the period that begins on the first day of an
    immediately preceding benefit period and ends with the end of the
    week before the beginning of a benefit period under subsection
    10(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of qualifying period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A qualifying period mentioned in paragraph (1)(a) is
  extended by the aggregate of any weeks during the qualifying period
  for which the person proves, in such manner as the Commission may
  direct, that throughout the week the person was not employed in
  insurable employment because the person was

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} incapable of work because of a prescribed illness,
    injury, quarantine or pregnancy;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} confined in a jail, penitentiary or other similar
    institution and was not found guilty of the offence for which the
    person was being held or any other offence arising out of the same
    transaction;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} receiving assistance under an employment support
    measure other than one referred to in paragraph 59(c) or (d); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} receiving payments under a provincial law on the
    basis of having ceased to work because continuing to work would have
    resulted in danger to the person, her unborn child or a child whom
    she was breast-feeding.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2021, c. 23, s. 305\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further extension of qualifying period

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A qualifying period is further extended by the
  aggregate of any weeks during an extension for which the person
  proves, in any manner that the Commission may direct, that the person
  was not employed in insurable employment because of a reason specified
  in subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period not counted if benefits received

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections (2) and (4), a week
  during which the person was in receipt of benefits does not count.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2021, c. 23, s. 305\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum extension of qualifying period

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} No extension under subsection (2) or (4) may result
  in a qualifying period of more than 104 weeks.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 8
- 2013, c. 35, s. 1

### [Benefit Period]{.HTitleText2} {#h-215911 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Establishment of benefit period

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** When an insured person who
qualifies under section 7 or 7.1 makes an initial claim for benefits, a
benefit period shall be established and, once it is established,
benefits are payable to the person in accordance with this Part for each
week of unemployment that falls in the benefit period.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Beginning of benefit period

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  benefit period begins on the later of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Sunday of the week in which the interruption of
    earnings occurs, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Sunday of the week in which the initial claim
    for benefits is made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Length of benefit period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided in subsections (10)
  to (15) and section 24, the length of a benefit period is 52 weeks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prior benefit period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to a change or cancellation of a benefit
  period under this section, a benefit period shall not be established
  for the claimant if a prior benefit period has not ended.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Late initial claims

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An initial claim for benefits made after the day when
  the claimant was first qualified to make the claim shall be regarded
  as having been made on an earlier day if the claimant shows that the
  claimant qualified to receive benefits on the earlier day and that
  there was good cause for the delay throughout the period beginning on
  the earlier day and ending on the day when the initial claim was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other late claims

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A claim for benefits, other than an initial claim for
  benefits, made after the time prescribed for making the claim shall be
  regarded as having been made on an earlier day if the claimant shows
  that there was good cause for the delay throughout the period
  beginning on the earlier day and ending on the day when the claim was
  made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} A claim for benefits referred to in section 23.1
  with respect to a family member shall not be regarded as having been
  made on an earlier day under subsection (4) or (5) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the time the claim is made, all benefits that
    may otherwise have been payable in relation to that claim have
    already been exhausted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the beginning of the period referred to in
    subsection 23.1(4) has already been determined with respect to that
    family member and the claim would have the effect of moving the
    beginning of that period to an earlier date; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claim is made in any other circumstances set
    out in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(5.2)]{.lawlabel} A claim for benefits referred to in section 23.2
  with respect to a critically ill child must not be regarded as having
  been made on an earlier day under subsection (4) or (5) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the time the claim is made, all benefits that
    may otherwise have been payable in relation to that claim have
    already been exhausted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the beginning of the period referred to in
    subsection 23.2(3) has already been determined with respect to that
    child and the claim would have the effect of moving the beginning of
    that period to an earlier date; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claim is made in any other circumstances set
    out in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(5.3)]{.lawlabel} A claim for benefits referred to in section 23.3
  with respect to a critically ill adult must not be regarded as having
  been made on an earlier day under subsection (4) or (5) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the time the claim is made, all benefits that
    may otherwise have been payable in relation to that claim have
    already been exhausted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the beginning of the period referred to in
    subsection 23.3(3) has already been determined with respect to that
    adult and the claim would have the effect of moving the beginning of
    that period to an earlier date; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claim is made in any other circumstances set
    out in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancelling benefit period

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Once a benefit period has been established for a
  claimant, the Commission may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cancel the benefit period if it has ended and no
    benefits were paid or payable during the period; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whether or not the period has ended, cancel at the
    request of the claimant that portion of the benefit period
    immediately before the first week for which benefits were paid or
    payable, if the claimant

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} establishes under this Part, as an insured
      person, a new benefit period beginning the first week for which
      benefits were paid or payable or establishes, under Part VII.1, as
      a self-employed person within the meaning of subsection 152.01(1),
      a new benefit period beginning the first week for which benefits
      were paid or payable, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} shows that there was good cause for the delay in
      making the request throughout the period beginning on the day when
      benefits were first paid or payable and ending on the day when the
      request for cancellation was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of cancellation

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A cancelled benefit period or portion of a benefit
  period is deemed never to have begun.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}End of benefit period

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A benefit period ends when any of the following first
  occurs:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} no further benefits are payable to the claimant in
    their benefit period, including for the reason that benefits have
    been paid for the maximum number of weeks for which benefits may be
    paid under section 12;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the benefit period would otherwise end under this
    section; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2002, c. 9, s. 12\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the claimant

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} requests that their benefit period end,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} makes a new initial claim for benefits under
      this Part or Part VII.1, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} qualifies, as an insured person, to receive
      benefits under this Part or qualifies, as a self-employed person
      within the meaning of subsection 152.01(1), to receive benefits
      under Part VII.1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Late requests

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Whether or not the benefit period has ended, a
  request under paragraph 8(d) shall be regarded as having been made on
  an earlier day if the claimant shows that there was good cause for the
  delay throughout the period beginning on the earlier day and ending on
  the day when the request was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of benefit period

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} A claimant's benefit period is extended by the
  aggregate of any weeks during the benefit period for which the
  claimant proves, in such manner as the Commission may direct, that the
  claimant was not entitled to benefits because the claimant was

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} confined in a jail, penitentiary or other similar
    institution and was not found guilty of the offence for which the
    claimant was being held or any other offence arising out of the same
    transaction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in receipt of earnings paid because of the complete
    severance of their relationship with their former employer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in receipt of workers' compensation payments for an
    illness or injury; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in receipt of payments under a provincial law on
    the basis of having ceased to work because continuing to work would
    have resulted in danger to the claimant, her unborn child or a child
    whom she was breast-feeding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further extension of benefit period

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} A claimant's benefit period is extended by the
  aggregate of any weeks during an extension of a benefit period under
  subsection (10) for which the claimant proves, in such manner as the
  Commission may direct, that the claimant was not entitled to benefits
  because of a reason specified in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of benefit period ---
  children in hospital

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} If the child or children referred to in subsection
  23(1) are hospitalized during the period referred to in subsection
  23(2), the benefit period is extended by the number of weeks during
  which the child or children are hospitalized.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of benefit period ---
  Canadian Forces

  [(12.1)]{.lawlabel} If, during the period referred to in subsection
  23(2), the start date of a claimant's period of parental leave is
  deferred or a claimant is directed to return to duty from parental
  leave, in accordance with regulations made under the [National Defence
  Act](/eng/acts/N-5), the benefit period is extended by the number of
  weeks during which the claimant's parental leave is deferred or the
  claimant is directed to return to duty, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of benefit period --- special
  benefits

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} If, during a claimant's benefit period,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} regular benefits were not paid to the claimant,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} benefits were paid to the claimant for more than
    one of the reasons mentioned in paragraphs 12(3)(a) to (f) and at
    least one of those benefits was paid for fewer than the applicable
    maximum number of weeks established for those reasons, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the maximum total number of weeks established for
    those reasons is greater than 50,

  the benefit period is extended so that those benefits may be paid up
  to that maximum total number of weeks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of benefit period --- reason
  mentioned in paragraph 12(3)(b)

  [(13.01)]{.lawlabel} The benefit period is extended by 26 weeks so
  that benefits may be paid for the reason mentioned in paragraph
  12(3)(b) up to the maximum number of weeks if, during a claimant's
  benefit period,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} benefits were paid to the claimant for that reason
    and the applicable maximum number of weeks is established in
    subparagraph 12(3)(b)(ii);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} regular benefits were not paid to the claimant; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} benefits were not paid for any reason mentioned in
    paragraph 12(3)(a), (c), (d), (e) or (f).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of benefit period --- regular
  and special benefits

  [(13.02)]{.lawlabel} If, during a claimant's benefit period, regular
  benefits were paid, benefits were paid for the reason mentioned in
  paragraph 12(3)(b) in the case where the applicable maximum number of
  weeks is established under subparagraph 12(3)(b)(ii), and benefits
  were paid for fewer than the total number of weeks established under
  subsection 12(6) as determined under subsection 12(7), the benefit
  period is extended so that benefits may be paid up to that total
  number of weeks. The extension must not exceed 26 weeks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(13.03)]{.lawlabel} Only the regular benefits and benefits for any of
  the reasons mentioned in paragraphs 12(3)(a) to (f) that were paid
  during the claimant's benefit period before it was extended under
  subsection (13.02) are payable during that extension.

- [(13.1) to (13.7)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2016, c. 7, s.
  211\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum extension under
  subsections (10) to (13.02)

  [(14)]{.lawlabel} An extension under one or more of subsections (10)
  to (13.02) must not result in a benefit period of more than 104 weeks.

- [(14.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2016, c. 7, s. 211\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum extension under subsection (13)

  [(15)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (14), unless the benefit
  period is also extended under any of subsections (10) to (12.1), an
  extension under subsection (13) must not result in a benefit period of
  more than the sum of two weeks and the total of the maximum number of
  weeks established under subsection 12(3) for each of the benefits paid
  to the claimant for one of the reasons mentioned in paragraphs
  12(3)(a) to (f) during the claimant's benefit period before it was
  extended under subsection (13).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 10
- 2002, c. 9, s. 12
- 2003, c. 15, s. 16
- 2009, c. 30, s. 1, c. 33, s. 5
- 2010, c. 9, s. 2
- 2012, c. 27, s. 13
- 2013, c. 35, s. 2

- 2017, c. 20, s. 230

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Week of unemployment

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A week
  of unemployment for a claimant is a week in which the claimant does
  not work a full working week.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- no duties to perform

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A week during which a claimant's contract of service
  continues and in respect of which the claimant receives or will
  receive their usual remuneration for a full working week is not a week
  of unemployment, even though the claimant may be excused from
  performing their normal duties or does not have any duties to perform
  at that time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- leave with deferred
  remuneration

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A week or part of a week during a period of leave
  from employment is not a week of unemployment if the employee

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} takes the period of leave under an agreement with
    their employer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} continues to be an employee of the employer during
    the period; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} receives remuneration that was set aside during a
    period of work, regardless of when it is paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- compensatory leave

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An insured person is deemed to have worked a full
  working week during each week that falls wholly or partly in a period
  of leave if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in each week the insured person regularly works a
    greater number of hours, days or shifts than are normally worked in
    a week by persons employed in full-time employment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is entitled to the period of leave under
    an employment agreement to compensate for the extra time worked.

### [Payment of Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-216010 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefits

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  benefit period has been established for a claimant, benefits may be
  paid to the claimant for each week of unemployment that falls in the
  benefit period, subject to the maximums established by this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General maximum

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The maximum number of weeks for which benefits may be
  paid in a benefit period because of a reason other than those
  mentioned in subsection (3) shall be determined in accordance with the
  table in Schedule I by reference to the regional rate of unemployment
  that applies to the claimant and the number of hours of insurable
  employment of the claimant in their qualifying period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General maximum --- exception

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), the maximum number of weeks
  for which benefits may be paid in a benefit period because of a reason
  other than those mentioned in subsection (3) to a claimant whose
  benefit period begins during the period beginning on September 27,
  2020 and ending on September 25, 2021 is 50.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(2.2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2.1) does not apply to a claimant under
  the [Employment Insurance (Fishing)
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-445).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General maximum --- exception for
  seasonal workers

  [(2.3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), the maximum number of weeks
  for which benefits may be paid in a benefit period to a claimant
  because of a reason other than those mentioned in subsection (3) shall
  be determined in accordance with the table set out in Schedule V by
  reference to the regional rate of unemployment that applies to the
  claimant and the number of hours of insurable employment of the
  claimant in their qualifying period if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the following conditions are met:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which a benefit period for the
      claimant is established falls within the period beginning on
      September 26, 2021 and ending on October 24, 2026,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} on the date on which the benefit period is
      established, the claimant is ordinarily resident in a region
      described in Schedule VI,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the 260 weeks before the date on which the
      benefit period referred to in subparagraph (i) begins, at least
      three benefit periods were established during which regular
      benefits were paid or payable, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} at least two of the benefit periods referred to
      in subparagraph (iii) began around the same time of year as the
      benefit period referred to in subparagraph (i) began; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the conditions referred to in subparagraphs (a)(i)
    and (ii) are met and the claimant had met the criteria set out in
    paragraphs 77.992(2)(b) to (d) of the [Employment Insurance
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332) --- taking into account
    subsections 77.992(3) and (4) of those Regulations --- in respect of
    a benefit period established for the claimant on a date within the
    period referred to in paragraph 77.992(2)(a) of those Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Establishment of benefit period ---
  presumption

  [(2.4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (2.3)(a)(iii), a
  claimant's benefit period established before the beginning of the
  260-week period is considered to have been established within the
  260-week period if the claimant received a notification of payment or
  non-payment with respect to any week that falls within that 260-week
  period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Beginning of benefit period ---
  presumption

  [(2.5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (2.3)(a)(iv), a
  benefit period in a previous year is considered to have begun around
  the same time of year if it began during the period that begins eight
  weeks before and ends eight weeks after the week that is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 52 weeks before the first week of the benefit
    period referred to in subparagraph (2.3)(a)(i);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 104 weeks before the first week of the benefit
    period referred to in subparagraph (2.3)(a)(i);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} 156 weeks before the first week of the benefit
    period referred to in subparagraph (2.3)(a)(i);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} 208 weeks before the first week of the benefit
    period referred to in subparagraph (2.3)(a)(i); or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} 260 weeks before the first week of the benefit
    period referred to in subparagraph (2.3)(a)(i).

- [(2.6) to (2.8)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2016, c. 7, s.
  212\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum --- special benefits

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The maximum number of weeks for which benefits may be
  paid in a benefit period

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} because of pregnancy is 15;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} because the claimant is caring for one or more
    new-born children of the claimant or one or more children placed
    with the claimant for the purpose of adoption is, in accordance with
    the election under section 23,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 35, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 61;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} because of a prescribed illness, injury or
    quarantine is 26;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} because the claimant is providing care or support
    to one or more family members described in subsection 23.1(2) is 26;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} because the claimant is providing care or support
    to one or more critically ill children described in subsection
    23.2(1), is 35; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} because the claimant is providing care or support
    to one or more critically ill adults described in subsection 23.3(1)
    is 15.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum --- special benefits

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The maximum number of weeks for which benefits may be
  paid

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a single pregnancy is 15; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the care of one or more new-born or adopted
    children as a result of a single pregnancy or placement is,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the maximum number of weeks that has been
      elected under subsection 23(1.1) is established under subparagraph
      (3)(b)(i), 35 or, if the weeks for which benefits may be paid are
      divided in accordance with section 23, 40, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the maximum number of weeks that has been
      elected under subsection 23(1.1) is established under subparagraph
      (3)(b)(ii), 61 or, if the weeks for which benefits may be paid are
      divided in accordance with section 23, 69.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum --- parental benefits

  [(4.01)]{.lawlabel} If a claim is made under this Part in respect of a
  child or children referred to in paragraph (4)(b) and a claim is made
  under section 152.05 in respect of the same child or children, the
  maximum number of weeks of benefits payable under this Act in respect
  of the child or children is,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the maximum number of weeks that has been
    elected under subsection 23(1.1) is established under subparagraph
    (3)(b)(i), 35 or, if the weeks for which benefits may be paid are
    divided in accordance with section 23, 40; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the maximum number of weeks that has been
    elected under subsection 23(1.1) is established under subparagraph
    (3)(b)(ii), 61 or, if the weeks for which benefits may be paid are
    divided in accordance with section 23, 69.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum --- compassionate care benefits

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} Even if more than one claim is made under this Act,
  at least one of which is made under section 23.1 --- or even if more
  than one certificate is issued for the purposes of this Act, at least
  one of which is issued for the purposes of section 23.1 --- for the
  same reason and in respect of the same family member, the maximum
  number of weeks of benefits payable under this Act in respect of that
  family member is 26 weeks during the period of 52 weeks that begins on
  the first day of the week referred to in paragraph 23.1(4)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shorter period

  [(4.2)]{.lawlabel} If a shorter period is prescribed for the purposes
  of subsection 23.1(5), then that shorter period applies for the
  purposes of subsection (4.1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expiration of shorter period

  [(4.3)]{.lawlabel} When a shorter period referred to in subsection
  (4.2) has expired in respect of a family member, no further benefits
  are payable under section 23.1 in respect of that family member until
  the minimum prescribed number of weeks has elapsed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum --- critically ill child

  [(4.4)]{.lawlabel} Even if more than one claim is made under this Act,
  at least one of which is made under section 23.2 --- or even if more
  than one certificate is issued for the purposes of this Act, at least
  one of which is issued for the purposes of section 23.2 --- for the
  same reason and in respect of the same critically ill child, the
  maximum number of weeks of benefits payable under this Act in respect
  of that child is 35 weeks during the period of 52 weeks that begins on
  the first day of the week referred to in paragraph 23.2(3)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum --- critically ill adult

  [(4.5)]{.lawlabel} Even if more than one claim is made under this Act,
  at least one of which is made under section 23.3 --- or even if more
  than one certificate is issued for the purposes of this Act, at least
  one of which is issued for the purposes of section 23.3 --- for the
  same reason and in respect of the same critically ill adult, the
  maximum number of weeks of benefits payable under this Act in respect
  of that adult is 15 weeks during the period of 52 weeks that begins on
  the first day of the week referred to in paragraph 23.3(3)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Combined weeks of benefits

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In a claimant's benefit period, the claimant may
  combine weeks of benefits to which they are entitled because of a
  reason mentioned in subsection (3), but the maximum number of combined
  weeks is 50. If the benefit period is extended under subsection
  10(13), the maximum number of combined weeks equals the maximum number
  of weeks in the benefit period calculated under subsection 10(15) less
  two weeks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Combined weeks of benefits

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} In a claimant's benefit period, the claimant may,
  subject to the applicable maximums, combine weeks of benefits to which
  the claimant is entitled under subsection (2), (2.1) or (2.3) and
  because of a reason mentioned in subsection (3), but the total number
  of weeks of benefits shall not exceed 50.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination --- total number of weeks

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of determining whether the total
  number of weeks of benefits established under subsection (6) has been
  reached, in the case where benefits have been paid for the reason
  mentioned in paragraph 12(3)(b) and the applicable maximum number of
  weeks is established under subparagraph 12(3)(b)(ii),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of weeks of benefits that were paid for
    the reason mentioned in paragraph 12(3)(b) is converted to the
    number of weeks of benefits that would have been paid at a rate of
    weekly benefits of 55%, as set out in the table in Schedule IV; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} other than the weeks referred to in subsections
    10(10) to (12.1) in respect of which the benefit period is extended
    and the week referred to in section 13, the weeks for which no
    benefits were paid are deemed to be weeks for which benefits were
    paid at a rate of weekly benefits of 55%.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adoption

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, the placement with
  a major attachment claimant, at the same or substantially the same
  time, of two or more children for the purpose of adoption is a single
  placement of a child or children for the purpose of adoption.
