[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiting period

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** A claimant is not
entitled to be paid benefits in a benefit period until, after the
beginning of the benefit period, the claimant has served a waiting
period of one week of unemployment for which benefits would otherwise be
payable.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 13
- 2016, c. 7, s. 213
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 13"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presumption

- **[[13.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of determining a
  claimant's waiting period, a week of unemployment is deemed to be a
  week of unemployment for which benefits would otherwise be payable if
  --- were the week not the waiting period --- the amount of the
  benefits that would have been payable in that week after deducting the
  following would be greater than zero:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the earnings, if any, that is greater
    than \$50, if the claimant's rate of weekly benefits is less than
    \$200; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the earnings, if any, that is greater
    than 25% of the claimant's rate of weekly benefits, if that rate is
    \$200 or more.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}References

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in determining the amount of the benefits that
    would have been payable, the deduction referred to in subsection
    19(2) is to be excluded; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a reference to earnings means earnings as
    determined for the purpose of subsection 19(2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 284
:::

### [Rate of Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-216070 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rate of weekly benefits

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  rate of weekly benefits payable to a claimant is 55% of their weekly
  insurable earnings, or 33% of their weekly insurable earnings for the
  weeks for which the claimant is paid benefits under section 23 in the
  case where the applicable maximum number of weeks is established under
  subparagraph 12(3)(b)(ii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum weekly insurable earnings

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The maximum weekly insurable earnings is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$750 if the claimant's benefit period begins
    during the years 1997 to 2000; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the claimant's benefit period begins in a
    subsequent year, the maximum yearly insurable earnings divided by
    52.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Weekly insurable earnings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A claimant's weekly insurable earnings are their
  insurable earnings in the calculation period divided by the number of
  weeks determined in accordance with the following table by reference
  to the applicable regional rate of unemployment.

  <figure id="216080">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize9 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Regional Rate
  of Unemployment</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom aligncenter" scope="col">Number of
  Weeks</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">not more than 6%</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom top aligncenter">22</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 6% but
  not more than 7%</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom bottom aligncenter">21</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 7% but
  not more than 8%</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom bottom aligncenter">20</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 8% but
  not more than 9%</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom bottom aligncenter">19</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 9% but
  not more than 10%</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom bottom aligncenter">18</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 10% but
  not more than 11%</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom bottom aligncenter">17</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 11% but
  not more than 12%</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom bottom aligncenter">16</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 12% but
  not more than 13%</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom bottom aligncenter">15</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight top">more than 13%</td>
  <td class="bottom aligncenter">14</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Insurable earnings

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Insurable earnings in the calculation period are
  equal to the total of the following amounts established and calculated
  in accordance with the regulations:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant's insurable earnings during the
    calculation period including those from insurable employment that
    has not ended but not including any insurable earnings paid or
    payable to the claimant by reason of lay-off or separation from
    employment in the qualifying period; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the insurable earnings paid or payable to the
    claimant, during the qualifying period, by reason of lay-off or
    separation from employment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Calculation period

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The calculation period of a claimant is the number of
  weeks, whether consecutive or not, determined in accordance with the
  table set out in subsection (2) by reference to the applicable
  regional rate of unemployment, in the claimant's qualifying period for
  which he or she received the highest insurable earnings.

- [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2012, c. 19, s. 604\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 14
- 2012, c. 19, s. 604
- 2017, c. 20, s. 232
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14-20130407.html "Link to previous version of section 14"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 5,
s. 5\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rate increase --- family supplement

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  rate of weekly benefits of a claimant who has one or more dependent
  children shall be increased by the amount of a family supplement
  determined in accordance with the regulations if the claimant
  establishes, in such manner as the Commission may direct, that the
  claimant meets the prescribed low-income family eligibility criteria.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criteria

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The criteria for low-income family eligibility may
  include criteria that are the same as or similar to the criteria for
  receiving a Canada child benefit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Canada child benefit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), a Canada child
  benefit is a deemed overpayment under subdivision a.1 of Division E of
  Part I of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum increase

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The amount of the increase in the claimant's rate of
  weekly benefits shall not exceed the prescribed percentage of the
  claimant's weekly insurable earnings or, if no percentage is
  prescribed, 25%.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 16
- 2016, c. 12, s. 86
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 16"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum rate of weekly benefits

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** The maximum rate of
weekly benefits is 55 % of the maximum yearly insurable earnings divided
by 52.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 17
- 2001, c. 5, s. 6
:::

### [Disentitlement to Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-216111 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Availability for work, etc.

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  claimant is not entitled to be paid benefits for a working day in a
  benefit period for which the claimant fails to prove that on that day
  the claimant was

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} capable of and available for work and unable to
    obtain suitable employment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} unable to work because of a prescribed illness,
    injury or quarantine, and that the claimant would otherwise be
    available for work; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} engaged in jury service.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A claimant to whom benefits are payable under any of
  sections 23 to 23.3 is not disentitled under paragraph (1)(b) for
  failing to prove that he or she would have been available for work
  were it not for the illness, injury or quarantine.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 18
- 2012, c. 27, s. 15
- 2014, c. 20, s. 247
- 2017, c. 20, s. 233
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-18-20141012.html "Link to previous version of section 18"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Deductions from Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-216122 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Earnings in waiting period

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  claimant has earnings during their waiting period, an amount not
  exceeding those earnings shall, as prescribed, be deducted from the
  benefits payable for the first three weeks for which benefits are
  otherwise payable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Earnings in periods of unemployment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3), (4), 21(3) and 22(5), if
  the claimant has earnings during any other week of unemployment, there
  shall be deducted from benefits payable in that week the amount equal
  to the total of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 50% of the earnings that are less than or
    equivalent to 90% of the claimant's weekly insurable earnings, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 100% of the earnings that are greater than 90% of
    the claimant's weekly insurable earnings.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undeclared earnings

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the claimant has failed to declare all or some of
  their earnings to the Commission for a period, determined under the
  regulations, for which benefits were claimed,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the following amount shall be deducted from the
    benefits paid to the claimant for that period:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the undeclared earnings, if, in the
      opinion of the Commission, the claimant knowingly failed to
      declare the earnings, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the amount of the undeclared
      earnings less the difference between

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} all amounts determined under paragraph (2)(a)
        or (b) for the period,

      and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} all amounts that were applied under those
        paragraphs in respect of the declared earnings for the period;
        and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the deduction shall be made

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} from the benefits paid for a number of weeks that
      begins with the first week for which the earnings were not
      declared in that period, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in such a manner that the amount deducted in
      each consecutive week equals the claimant's benefits paid for that
      week.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Earnings and allowances from employment
  support measures, courses and programs

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Earnings from employment under an employment support
  measure other than one referred to in paragraph 59(c) or (d) and
  earnings or allowances payable to a claimant for attending a course or
  program of instruction or training shall not be deducted under this
  section except in accordance with the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 19
- 2018, c. 12, s. 285
- [2022, c. 10, s. 390](#1357842-1366639){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357842-1366639.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20180812.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deduction for excluded days in waiting
period

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  claimant is not entitled to receive benefits for a working day in
  their waiting period, an amount equal to 1/5 of their weekly rate of
  benefits for each such working day shall be deducted from the benefits
  payable for the three weeks described in subsection 19(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deduction for excluded days not in
  waiting period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant is disentitled from receiving benefits
  for a working day in a week of unemployment that is not in their
  waiting period, an amount equal to 1/5 of their weekly rate of
  benefits for each such working day shall be deducted from the benefits
  payable for that week.

### [Special Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-216150 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Illness, etc. --- minor attachment
claimants

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  minor attachment claimant who ceases work because of illness, injury
  or quarantine is not entitled to receive benefits while unable to work
  for that reason.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If benefits are payable to a claimant as a result of
  illness, injury or quarantine and any allowances, money or other
  benefits are payable to the claimant for that illness, injury or
  quarantine under a provincial law, the benefits payable to the
  claimant under this Act shall be reduced or eliminated as prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deduction

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 19(3), if, as a result of
  illness, injury or quarantine, benefits are payable to a claimant for
  a week of unemployment, there shall be deducted from those benefits
  any allowances, money or other benefits payable to the claimant for
  that week under a plan that covers insured persons employed by an
  employer and in respect of which the employer's premium has been
  reduced in accordance with regulations made under subsection 69(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 21
- 2012, c. 27, s. 16
- 2018, c. 12, s. 286
- [2021, c. 23, s. 308](#1307886-1327280){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307886-1327280.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-21-20210926.html "Link to previous version of section 21"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pregnancy

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 18, but subject to
  this section, benefits are payable to a major attachment claimant who
  proves her pregnancy.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Weeks for which benefits may be paid

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 12, benefits are payable to a
  major attachment claimant under this section for each week of
  unemployment in the period

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that begins the earlier of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 12 weeks before the week in which her confinement
      is expected, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the week in which her confinement occurs; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that ends 17 weeks after the later of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the week in which her confinement is expected,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the week in which her confinement occurs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} When benefits are payable to a claimant for
  unemployment caused by pregnancy and any allowances, money or other
  benefits are payable to the claimant for that pregnancy under a
  provincial law, the benefits payable to the claimant under this Act
  shall be reduced or eliminated as prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 18

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 13, the provisions of
  section 18 do not apply to the week that immediately precedes the
  period described in subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deduction

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 19(3), if benefits are payable
  under this section to a major attachment claimant for a week of
  unemployment, there shall be deducted from those benefits any
  allowances, money or other benefits payable to the claimant for that
  week under a plan that covers insured persons employed by an employer
  and in respect of which the employer's premium has been reduced in
  accordance with regulations made under subsection 69(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a child who is born of the claimant's pregnancy is
  hospitalized, the period during which benefits are payable under
  subsection (2) shall be extended by the number of weeks during which
  the child is hospitalized.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The extended period shall end no later than 52 weeks
  after the week of confinement.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 22
- 2016, c. 7, s. 214
- 2017, c. 20, s. 234
- 2018, c. 12, s. 287
- [2021, c. 23, s. 309](#1307890-1327285){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307890-1327285.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-22-20210926.html "Link to previous version of section 22"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parental benefits

- **[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 18, but subject to
  this section, benefits are payable to a major attachment claimant to
  care for one or more new-born children of the claimant or one or more
  children placed with the claimant for the purpose of adoption under
  the laws governing adoption in the province in which the claimant
  resides.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Election by claimant

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} In a claim for benefits made under this section, a
  claimant shall elect the maximum number of weeks referred to in either
  subparagraph 12(3)(b)(i) or (ii) for which benefits may be paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Irrevocability of election

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} The election is irrevocable once benefits are paid
  under this section or under section 152.05 in respect of the same
  child or children.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}First to elect

  [(1.3)]{.lawlabel} If two major attachment claimants each make a claim
  for benefits under this section --- or one major attachment claimant
  makes a claim for benefits under this section and an individual makes
  a claim for benefits under section 152.05 --- in respect of the same
  child or children, the election made under subsection (1.1) or
  152.05(1.1) by the first claimant or individual, as the case may be,
  to make a claim for benefits under this section or under section
  152.05 is binding on both claimants or on the claimant and the
  individual.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Weeks for which benefits may be paid

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 12, benefits under this section
  are payable for each week of unemployment in the period

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that begins with the week in which the child or
    children of the claimant are born or the child or children are
    actually placed with the claimant for the purpose of adoption; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that ends 52 weeks after the week in which the
    child or children of the claimant are born or the child or children
    are actually placed with the claimant for the purpose of adoption.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period --- children in
  hospital

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the child or children referred to in
  subsection (1) are hospitalized during the period referred to in
  subsection (2), the period is extended by the number of weeks during
  which the child or children are hospitalized.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period --- Canadian Forces

  [(3.01)]{.lawlabel} If, during the period referred to in subsection
  (2), the start date of a claimant's period of parental leave is
  deferred or a claimant is directed to return to duty from parental
  leave, in accordance with regulations made under the [National Defence
  Act](/eng/acts/N-5), the period is extended by the number of weeks
  during which the claimant's parental leave is deferred or the claimant
  is directed to return to duty, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} No extension under subsection (3) or (3.01) may
  result in the period being longer than 104 weeks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period --- special
  benefits

  [(3.2)]{.lawlabel} The period referred to in subsection (2) is
  extended so that benefits may be paid up to the applicable maximum
  number of weeks referred to in subparagraph 12(3)(b)(i) or (ii) if,
  during a claimant's benefit period,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} regular benefits were not paid to the claimant;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} benefits were paid to the claimant for more than
    one of the reasons mentioned in paragraphs 12(3)(a) to (f);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the maximum total number of weeks established for
    those reasons is greater than 50; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} benefits were paid for the reason mentioned in
    paragraph 12(3)(b) but for fewer than the applicable maximum number
    of weeks established for that reason.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period --- reason
  mentioned in paragraph 12(3)(b)

  [(3.21)]{.lawlabel} The period referred to in subsection (2) is
  extended by 26 weeks so that benefits may be paid for the reason
  mentioned in paragraph 12(3)(b) up to the maximum number of weeks if,
  during a claimant's benefit period,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} benefits were paid to the claimant for that reason
    and the applicable maximum number of weeks is established in
    subparagraph 12(3)(b)(ii);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} regular benefits were not paid to the claimant; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} benefits were not paid for any reason mentioned in
    paragraph 12(3)(a), (c), (d), (e) or (f).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period --- regular and
  special benefits

  [(3.22)]{.lawlabel} If, during a claimant's benefit period, regular
  benefits were paid, benefits were paid for the reason mentioned in
  paragraph 12(3)(b) in the case where the applicable maximum number of
  weeks is established under subparagraph 12(3)(b)(ii), and benefits
  were paid for fewer than the total number of weeks established under
  subsection 12(6) as determined under subsection 12(7), the period
  referred to in subsection (2) is extended for the same number of weeks
  as the extension established under subsection 10(13.02).

- [(3.23)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2012, c. 27, s. 17\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3.3)]{.lawlabel} An extension under subsection (3.2) must not result
  in the period referred to in subsection (2) being longer than the
  maximum benefit period calculated under subsection 10(15).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3.4)]{.lawlabel} An extension under one or more of subsections
  10(10) to (13.02) must not result in the period referred to in
  subsection (2) being longer than 104 weeks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3.5)]{.lawlabel} If benefits are payable to a claimant for the
  reasons set out in this section and any allowances, money or other
  benefits are payable to the claimant for the same reasons under a
  provincial law, the benefits payable to the claimant under this Act
  are to be reduced or eliminated as prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Division of weeks of benefits

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If two major attachment claimants each make a claim
  for benefits under this section --- or if one major attachment
  claimant makes a claim for benefits under this section and an
  individual makes a claim for benefits under section 152.05 --- in
  respect of the same child or children, the weeks of benefits payable
  under this section, under section 152.05 or under both those sections
  may be divided between them up to a maximum of 40, if the maximum
  number of weeks that has been elected under subsection (1.1) or
  152.05(1.1) is established under subparagraph 12(3)(b)(i) or
  152.14(1)(b)(i), or up to a maximum of 69, if that number of weeks is
  established under subparagraph 12(3)(b)(ii) or 152.14(1)(b)(ii). If
  they cannot agree, the weeks of benefits are to be divided in
  accordance with the prescribed rules.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum number of weeks that can be
  divided

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, if, in respect of the same
  child or children, a major attachment claimant makes a claim for
  benefits under this section and an individual makes a claim for
  benefits under section 152.05, the total number of weeks of benefits
  payable under this section and section 152.05 that may be divided
  between them may not exceed

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 40, if the maximum number of weeks that has been
    elected under subsection (1.1) or 152.05(1.1) is established under
    subparagraph 12(3)(b)(i) or 152.14(1)(b)(i); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 69, if that number of weeks is established under
    subparagraph 12(3)(b)(ii) or 152.14(1)(b)(ii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum number of weeks per claimant

  [(4.11)]{.lawlabel} Even if the weeks of benefits payable are divided
  in accordance with subsections (4) and (4.1), the maximum number of
  weeks for which benefits may be paid to a claimant is 35 or 61 weeks,
  in accordance with the election made under subsection (1.1) or
  152.05(1.1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deferral of waiting period

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A major attachment claimant who makes a claim for
  benefits under this section may have their waiting period deferred
  until they make another claim for benefits in the same benefit period,
  otherwise than under section 22 or this section, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant has already made a claim for benefits
    under section 22 or this section in respect of the same child and
    has served the waiting period;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} another major attachment claimant has made a claim
    for benefits under section 22 or this section in respect of the same
    child and that other claimant has served or is serving their waiting
    period;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} another major attachment claimant is making a claim
    for benefits under section 22 or this section in respect of the same
    child at the same time as the claimant and that other claimant
    elects to serve the waiting period; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the claimant or another major attachment claimant
    meets the prescribed requirements.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a major attachment claimant makes a claim under
  section 22 or this section and an individual makes a claim under
  section 152.04 or 152.05 in respect of the same child or children and
  one of them has served or elected to serve their waiting period, then

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the major attachment claimant is not the one who
    served or elected to serve the waiting period, that claimant is not
    required to serve a waiting period; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the individual is not the one who served or
    elected to serve the waiting period, that claimant may have his or
    her waiting period deferred in accordance with section 152.05.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 23
- 2000, c. 12, s. 107, c. 14, s. 4
- 2002, c. 9, s. 14
- 2003, c. 15, s. 18
- 2005, c. 30, s. 130
- 2009, c. 33, s. 7
- 2010, c. 9, s. 3
- 2012, c. 27, s. 17
- 2017, c. 20, s. 235
- [2018, c. 27, s. 304](#1039480-1119410){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1039480-1119410.html"}
- [2021, c. 23, s. 310](#1307903-1327299){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307903-1327299.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-23-20210926.html "Link to previous version of section 23"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[23.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2017, c. 20, s.
  236\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compassionate care benefits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 18, but subject to this section,
  benefits are payable to a major attachment claimant if a medical
  doctor or nurse practitioner has issued a certificate stating that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a family member of the claimant has a serious
    medical condition with a significant risk of death within 26 weeks

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} from the day the certificate is issued,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a claim that is made before the
      day on which the certificate is issued, from the day from which
      the medical doctor or nurse practitioner certifies the family
      member's medical condition, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a claim that is regarded to have
      been made on an earlier day under subsection 10(4) or (5), from
      that earlier day; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the family member requires the care or support of
    one or more other family members.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Medical practitioner

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the circumstances set out in the regulations, the
  certificate required under subsection (2) may be issued by a member of
  a prescribed class of medical practitioners.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Weeks for which benefits may be paid

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 12, benefits under this section
  are payable for each week of unemployment in the period

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that begins with the first day of the week in which
    the following falls, namely,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day of issuance of the first certificate in
      respect of the family member that meets the requirements of
      subsection (2) and is filed with the Commission,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a claim that is made before the
      day on which the certificate is issued, the day from which the
      medical doctor or nurse practitioner certifies the family member's
      medical condition, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a claim that is regarded to have
      been made on an earlier day under subsection 10(4) or (5), that
      earlier day; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that ends on the last day of the week in which any
    of the following occurs, namely,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} all benefits payable under this section in
      respect of the family member are exhausted,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the family member dies, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the period of 52 weeks following the first day
      of the week referred to in paragraph (a) ends.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate not necessary

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, but subject to
  subsections (4) and 50(8.1), for benefits under this section to be
  payable after the end of the period of 26 weeks set out in paragraph
  (2)(a), it is not necessary for a medical doctor or nurse practitioner
  to issue an additional certificate under subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shorter period

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a shorter period is prescribed for the purposes of
  this section,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the certificate referred to in subsection (2) must
    state that the family member has a serious medical condition with a
    significant risk of death within that period; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that period applies for the purposes of
    subparagraph (4)(b)(iii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subparagraph (4)(a)(ii) does not apply to a claim if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the time the certificate is filed with the
    Commission, all benefits that may otherwise have been payable in
    relation to that claim have already been exhausted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the beginning of the period referred to in
    subsection (4) has already been determined with respect to the
    family member, and the filing of the certificate with the Commission
    would have the effect of moving the beginning of that period to an
    earlier date; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claim is made in any other circumstances set
    out in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deferral of waiting period

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A claimant who makes a claim for benefits under this
  section may have their waiting period deferred until they make another
  claim for benefits in the same benefit period if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} another claimant has made a claim for benefits
    under this section or section 152.06 in respect of the same family
    member during the period described in subsection (4) and that other
    claimant has served or is serving their waiting period in respect of
    that claim;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} another claimant is making a claim for benefits
    under this section or section 152.06 in respect of the same family
    member at the same time as the claimant and that other claimant
    elects to serve the waiting period; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claimant, or another claimant who has made a
    claim for benefits under this section or section 152.06 in respect
    of the same family member, meets the prescribed requirements.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Division of weeks of benefits

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant makes a claim for benefits under this
  section and another claimant makes a claim for benefits under this
  section or section 152.06 in respect of the same family member, any
  remaining weeks of benefits payable under this section, under section
  152.06 or under both those sections, up to a maximum of 26 weeks, may
  be divided in the manner agreed to by those claimants.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum number of weeks that can be
  divided

  [(8.1)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, if, in respect of the same
  family member, a claimant makes a claim for benefits under this
  section and another claimant makes a claim for benefits under section
  152.06, the total number of weeks of benefits payable under this
  section and section 152.06 that may be divided between them may not
  exceed 26 weeks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to agree

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} If the claimants referred to in subsection (8) cannot
  agree, the weeks of benefits are to be divided in accordance with the
  prescribed rules.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} When benefits are payable to a claimant for the
  reasons set out in this section and any allowances, money or other
  benefits are payable to the claimant under a provincial law for the
  same or substantially the same reasons, the benefits payable to the
  claimant under this section shall be reduced or eliminated as
  prescribed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefits --- critically ill child

- **[[23.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 18, but subject to
  this section, benefits are payable to a major attachment claimant who
  is a family member of a critically ill child in order to care for or
  support that child, if a medical doctor or nurse practitioner has
  issued a certificate that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} states that the child is a critically ill child and
    requires the care or support of one or more of their family members;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} sets out the period during which the child requires
    that care or support.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Medical practitioner

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the circumstances set out in the regulations, the
  certificate referred to in subsection (1) may be issued by a member of
  a prescribed class of medical practitioners.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Weeks for which benefits may be paid

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 12, benefits under this section
  are payable for each week of unemployment in the period

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that begins on the first day of the week in which
    either of the following falls:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the first certificate is issued
      in respect of the child that meets the requirements of
      subsection (1) and is filed with the Commission, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a claim that is made before the
      day on which the certificate is issued, the day from which the
      medical doctor or nurse practitioner certifies that the child is
      critically ill; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that ends on the last day of the week in which any
    of the following occurs:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} all benefits payable under this section in
      respect of the child are exhausted,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the child dies, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the expiry of 52 weeks following the first day
      of the week referred to in paragraph (a).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2017, c. 20, s. 237\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subparagraph (3)(a)(ii) does not apply to a claim if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the time the certificate is filed with the
    Commission, all benefits that may otherwise have been payable in
    relation to that claim have already been exhausted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the beginning of the period referred to in
    subsection (3) has already been determined and the filing of the
    certificate with the Commission would have the effect of moving the
    beginning of that period to an earlier date; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claim is made in any other circumstances set
    out in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deferral of waiting period

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A claimant who makes a claim for benefits under this
  section may have their waiting period deferred until they make another
  claim for benefits in the same benefit period if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} another claimant has made a claim for benefits
    under this section or section 152.061 in respect of the same child
    during the period described in subsection (3) and that other
    claimant has served or is serving their waiting period in respect of
    that claim;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} another claimant is making a claim for benefits
    under this section or section 152.061 in respect of the same child
    at the same time as the claimant and that other claimant elects to
    serve their waiting period; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claimant, or another claimant who has made a
    claim for benefits under this section or section 152.061 in respect
    of the same child, meets the prescribed requirements.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2017, c. 20, s. 237\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Division of weeks of benefits

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant makes a claim for benefits under this
  section and another claimant makes a claim for benefits under this
  section or section 152.061 in respect of the same child, any remaining
  weeks of benefits payable under this section, under section 152.061 or
  under both those sections, up to a maximum of 35 weeks, may be divided
  in the manner agreed to by those claimants. If they cannot agree, the
  weeks of benefits are to be divided in accordance with the prescribed
  rules.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum divisible number of weeks

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, if, in respect of the same
  child, a claimant makes a claim for benefits under this section and
  another claimant makes a claim for benefits under section 152.061, the
  total number of weeks of benefits payable under this section and
  section 152.061 that may be divided between them must not exceed 35
  weeks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation --- compassionate care
  benefits

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} Benefits under section 23.1 or 152.06 are not
  payable in respect of a child during the period referred to in
  subsection (3) or 152.061(3) that is established in respect of that
  child.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation --- benefits for critically
  ill adult

  [(10.1)]{.lawlabel} Benefits under section 23.3 or 152.062 are not
  payable during the period of 52 weeks that begins on the first day of
  the week referred to in paragraph (3)(a) in respect of a person who
  was a critically ill child if benefits were paid in respect of that
  person under this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} When benefits are payable to a claimant for the
  reasons set out in this section and any allowances, money or other
  benefits are payable to the claimant under a provincial law for the
  same or substantially the same reasons, the benefits payable to the
  claimant under this section shall be reduced or eliminated as
  prescribed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 27, s. 18
- 2017, c. 20, s. 237
- [2021, c. 23, s. 312](#1307937-1327302){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307937-1327302.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-23.2-20210926.html "Link to previous version of section 23.2"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Work-Sharing]{.HTitleText2} {#h-216361 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations for work-sharing benefits

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission may, with the approval of the Governor in Council, make
  regulations providing for the payment of work-sharing benefits to
  claimants who are qualified to receive benefits under this Act and are
  employed under a work-sharing agreement that has been approved for the
  purposes of this section by special or general direction of the
  Commission, including regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} defining and determining the nature of work-sharing
    employment for which benefits may be paid;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the maximum number of weeks for which
    benefits may be paid;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the method of paying benefits;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the rate of weekly benefits;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} providing a method for setting the amount that is
    the weekly insurable earnings of a claimant employed in work-sharing
    employment for the purposes of section 14;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner of treating, for benefit
    purposes, earnings received from the claimant's employer or from
    other sources;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} providing for the extension of a claimant's
    qualifying period or benefit period for a number of weeks not
    exceeding the number of weeks of work-sharing employment;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} deferring service by a claimant of all or any part
    of their waiting period until their work-sharing employment has
    ended; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} providing for any other matters necessary to carry
    out the purposes and provisions of this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No appeal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A special or general direction of the Commission
  approving or disapproving a work sharing agreement for the purposes of
  subsection (1) is not subject to review under section 112.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presumption

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, a claimant is
  unemployed and capable of and available for work during a week when
  the claimant works in work-sharing employment.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 24
- 2012, c. 19, s. 241
:::

### [Courses, Programs and Employment Support Measures]{.HTitleText2} {#h-216380 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Status of claimants

- **[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this Part, a claimant is unemployed and capable of and
  available for work during a period when the claimant is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} attending a course or program of instruction or
    training --- at the claimant's own expense, under an employment
    support measure referred to in paragraph 59(a) or under a measure
    that is the subject of an agreement under section 63 --- to which
    the Commission, or an authority that the Commission designates, has
    referred the claimant; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} participating in any other employment activity

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for which assistance has been provided for the
      claimant under a prescribed employment support measure --- other
      than one referred to in paragraph 59(a) or (c) --- or a prescribed
      measure that is the subject of an agreement under section 63, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to which the Commission, or an authority that
      the Commission designates, has referred the claimant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No appeal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A decision of the Commission about the referral of a
  claimant to a course, program or other employment activity mentioned
  in subsection (1) is not subject to review under section 112.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 25
- 1997, c. 26, s. 88
- 1999, c. 31, s. 76(F)
- 2012, c. 19, s. 242
- [2022, c. 10, s. 392](#1357846-1366643){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357846-1366643.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-25-20130401.html "Link to previous version of section 25"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefits are not earnings

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Part, Part IV, the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) and the [Canada
Pension Plan](/eng/acts/C-8), benefits paid to a claimant while employed
under an employment support measure --- other than one referred to in
paragraph 59(c) or (d) --- or under a measure that is the subject of an
agreement under section 63 are not earnings from employment.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 26
- 1997, c. 26, s. 89
- [2022, c. 10, s. 393](#1357851-1366646){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357851-1366646.html"}
:::

### [Disqualification and Disentitlement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-216396 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disqualification --- general

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  claimant is disqualified from receiving benefits under this Part if,
  without good cause since the interruption of earnings giving rise to
  the claim, the claimant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has not applied for a suitable employment that is
    vacant after becoming aware that it is vacant or becoming vacant, or
    has failed to accept the employment after it has been offered to the
    claimant;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has not taken advantage of an opportunity for
    suitable employment;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} has not carried out a written direction given to
    the claimant by the Commission with a view to assisting the claimant
    to find suitable employment, if the direction was reasonable having
    regard both to the claimant's circumstances and to the usual means
    of obtaining that employment; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} has not attended an interview that the Commission
    has directed the claimant to attend to enable the Commission or
    another appropriate agency

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to provide information and instruction to help
      the claimant find employment, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to identify whether the claimant might be
      assisted by job training or other employment assistance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Termination of referral

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} A claimant is disqualified from receiving benefits
  under this Part if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Commission or an authority that the Commission
    designates has, with the agreement of the claimant, referred the
    claimant to a course or program of instruction or training or to any
    other employment activity for which assistance has been provided
    under an employment support measure other than one referred to in
    paragraph 59(c); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Commission has terminated the referral because

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} without good cause, the claimant has not attended
      or participated in the course, program or employment activity and,
      in the opinion of the Commission, it is unlikely that the claimant
      will successfully complete the course, program or employment
      activity,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} without good cause, the claimant has withdrawn
      from the course, program or employment activity, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the organization providing the course, program
      or employment activity has expelled the claimant.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2016, c. 12, s. 102\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2012, c. 19, s. 605\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 27
- 2001, c. 34, s. 41(E)
- 2012, c. 19, s. 605
- 2016, c. 12, s. 102
- [2022, c. 10, s. 394](#1357853-1366648){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357853-1366648.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-27-20170312.html "Link to previous version of section 27"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration of disqualification

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  disqualification under section 27 is for the number of weeks that the
  Commission may determine, but

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of weeks of a disqualification arising
    under paragraph 27(1)(a) or (b) shall be not fewer than 7 or more
    than 12; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the number of weeks of a disqualification arising
    under paragraph 27(1)(c) or (d) or subsection 27(1.1) shall be not
    more than 6.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When disqualification is to be served

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) to (5), the weeks of
  disqualification are to be served during the weeks following the
  waiting period for which benefits would otherwise be payable if the
  disqualification had not been imposed and, for greater certainty, the
  length of the disqualification is not affected by any subsequent loss
  of employment by the claimant during the benefit period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disqualification to be carried forward

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any portion of the disqualification that has not been
  served when the claimant's benefit period ends shall, subject to
  subsections (4) and (5), be served in any benefit period subsequently
  established within two years after the event giving rise to the
  disqualification.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No weeks of disqualification shall be carried forward
  against a claimant who has had 700 or more hours of insurable
  employment since the event giving rise to the disqualification.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deferral

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Commission shall defer the serving of the
  disqualification if the claimant is otherwise entitled to special
  benefits or benefits by virtue of section 25.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presumption

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, benefits are deemed to
  be paid for the weeks of disqualification.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (6) does not apply to prevent a claimant
  from requesting that a benefit period established for the claimant as
  a minor attachment claimant be cancelled under subsection 10(6) and
  that a benefit period be established for the claimant as a major
  attachment claimant to enable the claimant to receive special
  benefits.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 28
- 2001, c. 5, s. 7
- [2021, c. 23, s. 314](#1307947-1327306){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307947-1327306.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-28-20210926.html "Link to previous version of section 28"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
sections 30 to 33,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} [[employment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} refers to any
  employment of the claimant within their qualifying period or their
  benefit period;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} loss of employment includes a suspension from
  employment, but does not include loss of, or suspension from,
  employment on account of membership in, or lawful activity connected
  with, an association, organization or union of workers;

- [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} voluntarily leaving an employment includes

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the refusal of employment offered as an alternative
    to an anticipated loss of employment, in which case the voluntary
    leaving occurs when the loss of employment occurs,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the refusal to resume an employment, in which case
    the voluntary leaving occurs when the employment is supposed to be
    resumed, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the refusal to continue in an employment after
    the work, undertaking or business of the employer is transferred to
    another employer, in which case the voluntary leaving occurs when
    the work, undertaking or business is transferred; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} just cause for voluntarily leaving an employment or
  taking leave from an employment exists if the claimant had no
  reasonable alternative to leaving or taking leave, having regard to
  all the circumstances, including any of the following:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} sexual or other harassment,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} obligation to accompany a spouse, common-law
    partner or dependent child to another residence,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} discrimination on a prohibited ground of
    discrimination within the meaning of the [Canadian Human Rights
    Act](/eng/acts/H-6),

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} working conditions that constitute a danger to
    health or safety,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} obligation to care for a child or a member of the
    immediate family,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} reasonable assurance of another employment in the
    immediate future,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} significant modification of terms and conditions
    respecting wages or salary,

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} excessive overtime work or refusal to pay for
    overtime work,

  - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} significant changes in work duties,

  - [(x)]{.lawlabel} antagonism with a supervisor if the claimant is not
    primarily responsible for the antagonism,

  - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} practices of an employer that are contrary to law,

  - [(xii)]{.lawlabel} discrimination with regard to employment because
    of membership in an association, organization or union of workers,

  - [(xiii)]{.lawlabel} undue pressure by an employer on the claimant to
    leave their employment, and

  - [(xiv)]{.lawlabel} any other reasonable circumstances that are
    prescribed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 29
- 2000, c. 12, s. 108
- [2021, c. 23, s. 315](#1307951-1327308){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307951-1327308.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-29-20210926.html "Link to previous version of section 29"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disqualification --- misconduct or
leaving without just cause

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  claimant is disqualified from receiving any benefits if the claimant
  lost any employment because of their misconduct or voluntarily left
  any employment without just cause, unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant has, since losing or leaving the
    employment, been employed in insurable employment for the number of
    hours required by section 7 or 7.1 to qualify to receive benefits;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant is disentitled under sections 31 to 33
    in relation to the employment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Length of disqualification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The disqualification is for each week of the
  claimant's benefit period following the waiting period and, for
  greater certainty, the length of the disqualification is not affected
  by any subsequent loss of employment by the claimant during the
  benefit period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Not retroactive

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the event giving rise to the disqualification
  occurs during a benefit period of the claimant, the disqualification
  does not include any week in that benefit period before the week in
  which the event occurs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (6), the disqualification is
  suspended during any week for which the claimant is otherwise entitled
  to special benefits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on qualifying for benefits

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant who has lost or left an employment as
  described in subsection (1) makes an initial claim for benefits, the
  following hours may not be used to qualify under section 7 or 7.1 to
  receive benefits:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} hours of insurable employment from that or any
    other employment before the employment was lost or left; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} hours of insurable employment in any employment
    that the claimant subsequently loses or leaves, as described in
    subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on number of weeks and rate
  of benefits

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} No hours of insurable employment in any employment
  that a claimant loses or leaves, as described in subsection (1), may
  be used for the purpose of determining the maximum number of weeks of
  benefits under subsection 12(2) or the claimant's rate of weekly
  benefits under section 14.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, but subject to paragraph
  (1)(a), a claimant may be disqualified under subsection (1) even if
  the claimant's last employment before their claim for benefits was not
  lost or left as described in that subsection and regardless of whether
  their claim is an initial claim for benefits.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 30
- [2021, c. 23, s. 316](#1307956-1327318){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307956-1327318.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-30-20210926.html "Link to previous version of section 30"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disentitlement --- suspension for
misconduct

**[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** A claimant who is
suspended from their employment because of their misconduct is not
entitled to receive benefits until

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the period of suspension expires;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant loses or voluntarily leaves the
  employment; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claimant, after the beginning of the period of
  suspension, accumulates with another employer the number of hours of
  insurable employment required by section 7 or 7.1 to qualify to
  receive benefits.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disentitlement --- period of leave
without just cause

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  claimant who voluntarily takes a period of leave from their employment
  without just cause is not entitled to receive benefits if, before or
  after the beginning of the period of leave,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the period of leave was authorized by the employer;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant and the employer agreed as to the day
    on which the claimant would resume employment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration of disentitlement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The disentitlement lasts until the claimant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} resumes the employment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} loses or voluntarily leaves the employment; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} after the beginning of the period of leave,
    accumulates with another employer the number of hours of insurable
    employment required by section 7 or 7.1 to qualify to receive
    benefits.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disentitlement --- anticipated loss of
employment

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  claimant is not entitled to receive benefits if the claimant loses an
  employment because of their misconduct or voluntarily leaves without
  just cause within three weeks before

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the expiration of a term of employment, in the case
    of employment for a set term; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the claimant is to be laid off
    according to a notice already given by the employer to the claimant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duration of disentitlement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The disentitlement lasts until the expiration of the
  term of employment or the day on which the claimant was to be laid
  off.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of disentitlement

**[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** A disentitlement under
sections 31 to 33 is suspended during any week for which the claimant is
otherwise entitled to special benefits.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

**[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** Notwithstanding anything
in this Part, no claimant is disqualified or disentitled under sections
30 to 33 from receiving benefits only because the claimant left or
refused to accept employment if, by remaining in or accepting the
employment, the claimant would lose the right

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to become or refrain from becoming a member of an
  association, organization or union of workers; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} to continue to be a member and to observe the lawful
  rules of an association, organization or union of workers.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Labour disputes

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the regulations, if a
  claimant loses an employment, or is unable to resume an employment,
  because of a work stoppage attributable to a labour dispute at the
  factory, workshop or other premises at which the claimant was
  employed, the claimant is not entitled to receive benefits until the
  earlier of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the end of the work stoppage, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the claimant becomes regularly
    engaged elsewhere in insurable employment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may, with the approval of the Governor
  in Council, make regulations for determining the number of days of
  disentitlement in a week of a claimant who loses a part-time
  employment or is unable to resume a part-time employment because of
  the reason mentioned in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of disentitlement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A disentitlement under this section is suspended
  during any period for which the claimant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} establishes that the claimant is otherwise entitled
    to special benefits or benefits by virtue of section 25; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} establishes, in such manner as the Commission may
    direct, that before the work stoppage, the claimant had anticipated
    being absent from their employment because of any reason entitling
    them to those benefits and had begun making arrangements in relation
    to the absence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply if a claimant proves that
  the claimant is not participating in, financing or directly interested
  in the labour dispute that caused the stoppage of work.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Separate branches of work

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If separate branches of work that are commonly
  carried on as separate businesses in separate premises are carried on
  in separate departments on the same premises, each department is, for
  the purpose of this section, a separate factory or workshop.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prison inmates and persons outside Canada

**[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** Except as may otherwise
be prescribed, a claimant is not entitled to receive benefits for any
period during which the claimant

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is an inmate of a prison or similar institution; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not in Canada.

### [Penalties]{.HTitleText2} {#h-216527 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Penalty for claimants, etc.

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission may impose on a claimant, or any other person acting for a
  claimant, a penalty for each of the following acts or omissions if the
  Commission becomes aware of facts that in its opinion establish that
  the claimant or other person has

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a claim for benefits, made a
    representation that the claimant or other person knew was false or
    misleading;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} being required under this Act or the regulations to
    provide information, provided information or made a representation
    that the claimant or other person knew was false or misleading;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} knowingly failed to declare to the Commission all
    or some of the claimant's earnings for a period determined under the
    regulations for which the claimant claimed benefits;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} made a claim or declaration that the claimant or
    other person knew was false or misleading because of the
    non-disclosure of facts;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} being the payee of a special warrant, knowingly
    negotiated or attempted to negotiate it for benefits to which the
    claimant was not entitled;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} knowingly failed to return a special warrant or the
    amount of the warrant or any excess amount, as required by section
    44;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} imported or exported a document issued by the
    Commission, or had it imported or exported, for the purpose of
    defrauding or deceiving the Commission; or

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} participated in, assented to or acquiesced in an
    act or omission mentioned in paragraphs (a) to (g).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum penalty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may set the amount of the penalty for
  each act or omission at not more than

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} three times the claimant's rate of weekly benefits;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the penalty is imposed under paragraph (1)(c),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} three times the amount of the deduction from the
      claimant's benefits under subsection 19(3), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} three times the benefits that would have been
      paid to the claimant for the period mentioned in that paragraph if
      the deduction had not been made under subsection 19(3) or the
      claimant had not been disentitled or disqualified from receiving
      benefits; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} three times the maximum rate of weekly benefits in
    effect when the act or omission occurred, if no benefit period was
    established.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination under subsection 145(2)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, weeks of regular benefits that
  are repaid as a result of an act or omission mentioned in
  subsection (1) are deemed to be weeks of regular benefits paid for the
  purposes of the application of subsection 145(2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 38
- 2001, c. 5, s. 8
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Penalty for employers, etc.

- **[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission may impose on an employer, or any other person acting for
  an employer or pretending to be or act for an employer, a penalty for
  each of the following acts if the Commission becomes aware of facts
  that in its opinion establish that the employer or other person has

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} made, in relation to any matter arising under this
    Act, a representation that the employer or other person knew was
    false or misleading;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} being required under this Act or the regulations to
    provide information, provided information or made a representation
    that the employer or other person knew was false or misleading;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in relation to any matter arising under this Act,
    made a declaration that the employer or other person knew was false
    or misleading because of the non-disclosure of facts;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} imported or exported a document issued by the
    Commission, or had it imported or exported, for the purpose of
    defrauding or deceiving the Commission; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} participated in, assented to or acquiesced in an
    act mentioned in paragraphs (a) to (d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum penalty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may set the amount of the penalty for
  each act at not more than nine times the maximum rate of weekly
  benefits in effect when the penalty is imposed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Officers, etc., of corporations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Commission becomes aware of facts that in its
  opinion establish that a corporation has committed an act described in
  subsection (1) and that any officer, director or agent of the
  corporation has directed, authorized, assented to, acquiesced in or
  participated in the act, the Commission may impose a penalty on the
  officer, director or agent, whether or not a penalty has been imposed
  on the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of information
  requirements

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (2), if the act involves
  the provision of information about any matter on which the fulfilment
  of conditions for the qualification and entitlement for receiving or
  continuing to receive benefits depends, the Commission may set the
  amount of the penalty at not more than the greater of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$12,000, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the penalty imposed under section 38
    on any person who made a claim for benefits based on the information
    provided.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Major contraventions

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (2), the Commission may
  set the amount of the penalty at an amount required or authorized by
  the regulations if the act is a major contravention, as defined under
  the regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation on imposition of penalties

**[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** A penalty shall not be
imposed under section 38 or 39 if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a prosecution for the act or omission has been
  initiated against the employee, employer or other person; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} 36 months have passed since the day on which the act
  or omission occurred.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rescission, etc., of penalty

**[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** The Commission may
rescind the imposition of a penalty under section 38 or 39, or reduce
the penalty, on the presentation of new facts or on being satisfied that
the penalty was imposed without knowledge of, or on the basis of a
mistake as to, some material fact.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warning

- **[[41.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may issue a warning
  instead of setting the amount of a penalty for an act or omission
  under subsection 38(2) or 39(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding paragraph 40(b), a warning may be
  issued within 72 months after the day on which the act or omission
  occurred.

### [Non-assignment of Benefits and Liability to Return Benefits and Pay Penalties]{.HTitleText2} {#h-216579 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefits not assignable

- **[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3),
  benefits are not capable of being assigned, charged, attached,
  anticipated or given as security and any transaction appearing to do
  so is void.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- recovery of amounts
  payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any amounts payable under this Act by any person and
  required to be credited to the Employment Insurance Operating Account
  may be recovered out of any benefits payable to that person, without
  affecting any other mode of recovery.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- payment to other
  governments

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Government of Canada, a provincial or
  municipal government or any other prescribed authority pays a person
  an advance or assistance or a welfare payment for a week that would
  not be paid if unemployment benefits were paid for that week, and
  unemployment benefits subsequently become payable to that person for
  that week, the Commission may, subject to the regulations, deduct from
  those or any subsequent benefits and pay to the government or the
  prescribed authority an amount equal to the amount of the advance,
  assistance or welfare payment paid, if the person had, on or before
  receiving the advance, assistance or welfare payment, consented to the
  deduction and payment by the Commission.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 42
- 2001, c. 4, s. 74(F)
- 2010, c. 12, s. 2194
- 2012, c. 19, s. 606
:::

[Previous
Version](section-42-20100712.html "Link to previous version of section 42"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability for overpayments

**[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** A claimant is liable to
repay an amount paid by the Commission to the claimant as benefits

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} for any period for which the claimant is
  disqualified; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} to which the claimant is not entitled.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability to return overpayment

**[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** A person who has received
or obtained a benefit payment to which the person is disentitled, or a
benefit payment in excess of the amount to which the person is entitled,
shall without delay return the amount, the excess amount or the special
warrant for payment of the amount, as the case may be.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of benefits by claimant

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** If a claimant receives
benefits for a period and, under a labour arbitration award or court
judgment, or for any other reason, an employer, a trustee in bankruptcy
or any other person subsequently becomes liable to pay earnings,
including damages for wrongful dismissal or proceeds realized from the
property of a bankrupt, to the claimant for the same period and pays the
earnings, the claimant shall pay to the Receiver General as repayment of
an overpayment of benefits an amount equal to the benefits that would
not have been paid if the earnings had been paid or payable at the time
the benefits were paid.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of benefits by employer or other
person

- **[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  under a labour arbitration award or court judgment, or for any other
  reason, an employer, a trustee in bankruptcy or any other person
  becomes liable to pay earnings, including damages for wrongful
  dismissal or proceeds realized from the property of a bankrupt, to a
  claimant for a period and has reason to believe that benefits have
  been paid to the claimant for that period, the employer or other
  person shall ascertain whether an amount would be repayable under
  section 45 if the earnings were paid to the claimant and if so shall
  deduct the amount from the earnings payable to the claimant and remit
  it to the Receiver General as repayment of an overpayment of benefits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of benefits by employer

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant receives benefits for a period and
  under a labour arbitration award or court judgment, or for any other
  reason, the liability of an employer to pay the claimant earnings,
  including damages for wrongful dismissal, for the same period is or
  was reduced by the amount of the benefits or by a portion of them, the
  employer shall remit the amount or portion to the Receiver General as
  repayment of an overpayment of benefits.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

**[[46.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46.01 .sectionLabel}** No amount is
payable under section 45, or deductible under subsection 46(1), as a
repayment of an overpayment of benefits if more than 36 months have
elapsed since the lay-off or separation from the employment in relation
to which the earnings are paid or payable and, in the opinion of the
Commission, the administrative costs of determining the repayment would
likely equal or exceed the amount of the repayment.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 19, s. 607
- [2021, c. 23, s. 317](#1307965-1327321){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307965-1327321.html"}
:::
