[Previous
Version](section-46.01-20210926.html "Link to previous version of section 46.01"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability of directors to pay penalties

- **[[46.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  a penalty is imposed on a corporation under section 38 or 39 for an
  act or omission, the directors of the corporation at the time of the
  act or omission are, subject to subsections (2) to (7), jointly and
  severally, or solidarily, liable, together with the corporation, to
  pay the amount of the penalty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitations on liability

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A director is not liable unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a certificate for the amount of the corporation's
    liability for the penalty has been registered in the Federal Court
    under section 126 and execution for that amount has been returned
    unsatisfied in whole or in part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the corporation has commenced liquidation or
    dissolution proceedings or has been dissolved and a claim for the
    amount of its liability has been proved within six months after the
    date of commencement of the proceedings or the date of the
    dissolution, whichever is earlier; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the corporation has made an assignment or a
    bankruptcy order has been made against it under the [Bankruptcy and
    Insolvency Act](/eng/acts/B-3) and a claim for the amount of its
    liability has been proved within six months after the date of the
    assignment or bankruptcy order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Defence of due diligence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A director is not liable if the director exercised
  the degree of care, diligence and skill that a reasonably prudent
  person would have exercised in comparable circumstances to prevent the
  act or omission for which the penalty is imposed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation period

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No action or proceedings to recover any amount
  payable by a director shall be commenced more than six years after the
  occurrence of the act or omission for which the penalty is imposed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount recoverable

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If execution referred to in paragraph (2)(a) has
  issued, the amount recoverable from a director is the amount remaining
  unsatisfied after execution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preference

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a director pays an amount in respect of a
  corporation's liability that is proved in liquidation, dissolution or
  bankruptcy proceedings,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the director is entitled to any preference that Her
    Majesty in right of Canada would have been entitled to if that
    amount had not been paid; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a certificate that relates to that amount has
    been registered, the director is entitled to an assignment of the
    certificate to the extent of the director's payment and the
    Commission shall make the assignment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contribution from other directors

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A director who has satisfied a claim under this
  section is entitled to contribution from the other directors who are
  liable for the claim.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 46.1
- 1999, c. 31, s. 77(F)
- 2004, c. 25, ss. 133, 197
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-46.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 46.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debts to Crown

- **[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All
  amounts payable under section 38, 39, 43, 45, 46 or 46.1 are debts due
  to Her Majesty and are recoverable in the Federal Court or any other
  court of competent jurisdiction or in any other manner provided by
  this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If benefits become payable to a claimant, the amount
  of the indebtedness may be deducted and retained out of the benefits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No amount due under this section may be recovered
  more than 72 months after the day on which the liability arose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeals

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A limitation period established by subsection (3)
  does not run when there is pending an appeal or other review of a
  decision establishing the liability.

### [Claim Procedure]{.HTitleText2} {#h-216637 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Claim required

- **[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  benefit period shall be established for a person unless the person
  makes an initial claim for benefits in accordance with section 50 and
  the regulations and proves that the person is qualified to receive
  benefits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information required

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No benefit period shall be established unless the
  claimant supplies information in the form and manner directed by the
  Commission, giving the claimant's employment circumstances and the
  circumstances pertaining to any interruption of earnings, and such
  other information as the Commission may require.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On receiving an initial claim for benefits, the
  Commission shall decide whether the claimant is qualified to receive
  benefits and notify the claimant of its decision.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof required

- **[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person is not entitled to receive benefits for a week of unemployment
  until the person makes a claim for benefits for that week in
  accordance with section 50 and the regulations and proves that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person meets the requirements for receiving
    benefits; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no circumstances or conditions exist that have the
    effect of disentitling or disqualifying the person from receiving
    benefits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefit of the doubt

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission shall give the benefit of the doubt to
  the claimant on the issue of whether any circumstances or conditions
  exist that have the effect of disqualifying the claimant under section
  30 or disentitling the claimant under section 31, 32 or 33, if the
  evidence on each side of the issue is equally balanced.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On receiving a claim for benefits, the Commission
  shall decide whether benefits are payable to the claimant for that
  week and notify the claimant of its decision.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entitlement to benefits

- **[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  claimant who fails to fulfil or comply with a condition or requirement
  under this section is not entitled to receive benefits for as long as
  the condition or requirement is not fulfilled or complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}How a claim must be made

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A claim for benefits shall be made in the manner
  directed at the office of the Commission that serves the area in which
  the claimant resides, or at such other place as is prescribed or
  directed by the Commission.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A claim for benefits shall be made by completing a
  form supplied or approved by the Commission, in the manner set out in
  instructions of the Commission.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A claim for benefits for a week of unemployment in a
  benefit period shall be made within the prescribed time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may at any time require a claimant to
  provide additional information about their claim for benefits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Making claim or providing information
  in person

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may require a claimant or group or
  class of claimants to be at a suitable place at a suitable time in
  order to make a claim for benefits in person or provide additional
  information about a claim.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration for employment

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of proving that a claimant is
  available for work, the Commission may require the claimant to
  register for employment at an agency administered by the Government of
  Canada or a provincial government and to report to the agency at such
  reasonable times as the Commission or agency directs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of efforts to obtain employment

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of proving that a claimant is
  available for work and unable to obtain suitable employment, the
  Commission may require the claimant to prove that the claimant is
  making reasonable and customary efforts to obtain suitable employment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof --- additional certificate

  [(8.1)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of proving that the conditions of
  subsection 23.1(2) or 152.06(1) are met, the Commission may require
  the claimant to provide it with an additional certificate issued by a
  medical doctor or nurse practitioner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mailing address

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A claimant shall provide the mailing address of their
  normal place of residence, unless otherwise permitted by the
  Commission.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiver or variation of requirements

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may waive or vary any of the
  conditions and requirements of this section or the regulations
  whenever in its opinion the circumstances warrant the waiver or
  variation for the benefit of a claimant or a class or group of
  claimants.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 50
- 2015, c. 36, s. 76
- 2017, c. 20, s. 239
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-50-20160103.html "Link to previous version of section 50"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

**[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** If, in considering a
claim for benefits, the Commission finds an indication from the
documents relating to the claim that the loss of employment resulted
from the claimant's misconduct or that the claimant voluntarily left
employment, the Commission shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} give the claimant and the employer an opportunity to
  provide information as to the reasons for the loss of employment; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the information is provided, take it into account
  in determining the claim.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 51
- [2021, c. 23, s. 318](#1307970-1327325){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307970-1327325.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-51-20210926.html "Link to previous version of section 51"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reconsideration of claim

- **[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 111, but subject to
  subsection (5), the Commission may reconsider a claim for benefits
  within 36 months after the benefits have been paid or would have been
  payable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Commission decides that a person has received
  money by way of benefits for which the person was not qualified or to
  which the person was not entitled, or has not received money for which
  the person was qualified and to which the person was entitled, the
  Commission must calculate the amount of the money and notify the
  claimant of its decision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount repayable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Commission decides that a person has received
  money by way of benefits for which the person was not qualified or to
  which the person was not entitled,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount calculated is repayable under section
    43; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day that the Commission notifies the person of
    the amount is, for the purposes of subsection 47(3), the day on
    which the liability arises.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount payable

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Commission decides that a person was qualified
  and entitled to receive money by way of benefits, and the money was
  not paid, the amount calculated is payable to the claimant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extended time to reconsider claim

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If, in the opinion of the Commission, a false or
  misleading statement or representation has been made in connection
  with a claim, the Commission has 72 months within which to reconsider
  the claim.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 52
- 2012, c. 19, s. 243
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-52-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 52"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

**[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** If the Commission is
required to notify a person of a decision under this Part, it may have
that person notified in such manner as it considers adequate.

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-216700 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** The Commission may, with
the approval of the Governor in Council, make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the conditions on which the requirement
  under this Part or Part VII.1 of serving a waiting period may be
  waived;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} defining or determining what is a working day or
  working week in any employment or for the purposes of Part VII.1;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the conditions and circumstances under
  which a claimant

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} while self-employed or employed in employment that
    is not insurable employment, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} whose pattern of full-time employment differs from
    the normal and customary employment pattern of employed persons
    generally,

  is to be considered to have worked or not worked a full working week;

- [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} for determining the average number of weeks of
  regular benefits for the purposes of paragraph 7.1(6)(b);

- [(c.2)]{.lawlabel} setting out circumstances for the purposes of
  paragraphs 10(5.1)(c), 10(5.2)(c), 10(5.3)(c), 23.1(6)(c), 23.2(5)(c),
  23.3(4)(c), 152.06(5)(c), 152.061(5)(c), 152.062(4)(c), 152.11(6)(c),
  152.11(6.1)(c) and 152.11(6.2)(c);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} defining or determining who are dependent children,
  prescribing low-income family eligibility criteria and determining the
  amount of family supplements for the purposes of section 16 or 152.17;

- [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} determining, for the purposes of subsection 19(3)
  or 152.18(3), the period for which benefits were claimed;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} providing for the deduction under section 19 of
  earnings and allowances mentioned in subsection 19(4);

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} determining the amount to be deducted under
  subsection 20(2) from weekly benefits paid if the claimant normally
  works other than a five day week;

- [(f.1)]{.lawlabel} \[Not in force\]

- [(f.2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2017, c. 20, s. 240\]]{.Repealed}

- [(f.21)]{.lawlabel} defining or determining what is a medical doctor,
  a nurse practitioner, a family member, a critically ill child and a
  critically ill adult for the purposes of subsections 23.1(2), 23.2(1),
  23.3(1), 152.06(1), 152.061(1) and 152.062(1);

- [(f.3)]{.lawlabel} defining or determining what is care or support for
  the purposes of paragraphs 23.1(2)(b), 23.2(1)(a), 23.3(1)(a),
  152.06(1)(b), 152.061(1)(a) and 152.062(1)(a);

- [(f.4)]{.lawlabel} prescribing classes of medical practitioners for
  the purposes of subsections 23.1(3), 23.2(2), 23.3(2), 152.06(2),
  152.061(2) and 152.062(2) and setting out the circumstances in which a
  certificate may be issued by them under subsection 23.1(2), 23.2(1),
  23.3(1), 152.06(1), 152.061(1) or 152.062(1);

- [(f.5)]{.lawlabel} prescribing a shorter period for the purposes of
  subsections 23.1(5) and 152.06(4) and prescribing a minimum number of
  weeks in relation to that shorter period for the purposes of
  subsections 12(4.3) and 152.14(7);

- [(f.6)]{.lawlabel} prescribing requirements for the purposes of
  paragraphs 23.1(7)(c), 23.2(6)(c), 23.3(5)(c), 152.06(6)(c),
  152.061(6)(c) and 152.062(5)(c);

- [(f.7)]{.lawlabel} prescribing rules for the purposes of subsections
  23(4), 23.1(9), 23.2(8), 23.3(6), 152.05(12), 152.06(7), 152.061(8)
  and 152.062(6);

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} setting out the circumstances that constitute the
  commencement or termination of a stoppage of work for the purposes of
  section 36;

- [(g.1)]{.lawlabel} for defining as a major contravention for the
  purposes of subsection 39(5) anything that constitutes an act
  mentioned in subsection 39(1) or (3) and for setting or calculating
  the amount or maximum amount of the penalty for the major
  contravention, up to a maximum of \$25,000;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} providing for the making of claims by, and the
  payment of benefits to, any person or agency on behalf of deceased or
  incapacitated persons or persons with mental disabilities;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} imposing additional conditions and terms with respect
  to the payment and receipt of benefits and restricting the amount or
  period of benefits in relation to persons who by custom of their
  occupation, trade or industry or under their agreement with an
  employer are paid in whole or in part by the piece or on a basis other
  than time;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} prohibiting the payment of benefits, in whole or in
  part, and restricting the amount of benefits payable, in relation to
  persons or to groups or classes of persons who work or have worked for
  any part of a year in an industry or occupation in which, in the
  opinion of the Commission, there is a period that occurs annually, at
  regular or irregular intervals, during which no work is performed by a
  significant number of persons engaged in that industry or occupation,
  for any or all weeks in that period;

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} for the ratification of amounts paid to persons while
  they are not entitled to them and for writing off those amounts and
  any penalties under section 38, 39 or 65.1 and amounts owing under
  section 43, 45, 46, 46.1 or 65 and any costs recovered against those
  persons;

- [(k.1)]{.lawlabel} establishing criteria for defining or determining
  what constitutes suitable employment for different categories of
  claimants for the purposes of any provision of this Act;

- [(k.2)]{.lawlabel} establishing criteria for defining or determining
  what constitutes reasonable and customary efforts for the purposes of
  subsection 50(8);

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} respecting the proof of fulfilment of the conditions
  and the absence of the disqualification from receiving or continuing
  to receive benefits, and for that purpose requiring the attendance of
  insured persons at such offices or places and at such times as may be
  required;

- [(m)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner in which claims for benefits
  shall be made and the information to be provided with those claims;

- [(n)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the procedure to be followed for the
  consideration and the examination of claims and questions to be
  considered by officers of the Commission and the way in which a
  question may be raised as to the continuation of benefits in the case
  of a person in receipt of benefits;

- [(o)]{.lawlabel} respecting the payment of benefits during any period
  intervening between an application for the determination of a question
  or a claim for benefits and the final determination of the question or
  claim;

- [(p)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the time and manner of paying benefits;

- [(q)]{.lawlabel} requiring employers to provide information about any
  matter on which the fulfilment of conditions for the qualification and
  entitlement for receiving or continuing to receive benefits depends,
  prescribing the time and manner in which the information shall be
  provided and requiring the certification or affirmation of the
  information;

- [(r)]{.lawlabel} providing the manner of ascertaining a claimant's
  employment history if their employer has failed to provide a record of
  their employment on separation or the employer is not available or is
  unable to provide the necessary employment history because their
  records are destroyed or lost;

- [(s)]{.lawlabel} defining and determining earnings for benefit
  purposes, determining the amount of those earnings and providing for
  the allocation of those earnings to weeks or other periods;

- [(t)]{.lawlabel} establishing criteria for defining and determining
  what constitutes a supplemental unemployment benefit plan and
  providing for the making of those determinations, including the
  consideration of late applications, reconsidering determinations and
  appeals from determinations;

- [(u)]{.lawlabel} defining and determining the circumstances in which
  and the time at which an interruption of earnings occurs;

- [(v)]{.lawlabel} prescribing conditions under which benefits may be
  paid in advance;

- [(w)]{.lawlabel} establishing regions appropriate for the purpose of
  applying this Part and Part VIII and delineating their boundaries
  based on geographical units established or used by Statistics Canada;

- [(x)]{.lawlabel} determining the regional rates of unemployment
  produced by Statistics Canada or the averages of those rates that
  shall apply to a claimant for the purposes of this Part and Part VIII
  and incorporating in those rates an estimate of the rates for status
  Indians living on Indian reserves;

- [(y)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the information and evidence to be
  provided by a claimant to prove

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} inability to work because of illness, injury or
    quarantine, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} pregnancy or the expected date of confinement;

- [(z)]{.lawlabel} for carrying out the purposes and provisions of
  section 14, including regulations

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} respecting the circumstances under which, the
    criteria by which and the manner in which

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} weeks are to be considered as weeks for which a
      claimant has insurable earnings during a calculation period,
      including the number of those weeks to be considered in that
      period, and

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} amounts are to be considered as the insurable
      earnings for any week or number of weeks in that period, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for allocating insurable earnings to a calculation
    period, for example by including them in that period or excluding
    them from that period;

- [(z.1)]{.lawlabel} for allocating hours of insurable employment to a
  qualifying period, for example by including them in that period or
  excluding them from that period;

- [(z.2)]{.lawlabel} prescribing

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the circumstances in which a claimant who leaves
    employment in accordance with an employer work-force reduction
    process that preserves the employment of coworkers may,
    notwithstanding section 30, be paid benefits, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} what constitutes an employer work-force reduction
    process for the purposes of the regulations;

- [(z.3)]{.lawlabel} reducing the special benefits payable when
  allowances, money or other benefits are payable to a claimant under a
  plan other than one established under a provincial law;

- [(z.31)]{.lawlabel} eliminating the special benefits payable to a
  claimant in respect of any period that constitutes, under a plan other
  than one established under a provincial law, an elimination period
  during which no benefit is payable to the claimant under the plan; and

- [(z.4)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that by section 2 or this Part
  is to be prescribed.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hours of insurable employment

- **[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission may, with the approval of the Governor in Council, make
  regulations for establishing how many hours of insurable employment a
  person has, including regulations providing that persons whose
  earnings are not paid on an hourly basis are deemed to have hours of
  insurable employment as established in accordance with the
  regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative methods

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Commission considers that it is not possible
  to apply the provisions of the regulations, it may authorize an
  alternative method of establishing how many hours of insurable
  employment a person has.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alteration or rescission of
  authorization

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may at any time alter the authorized
  method or rescind the authorization, subject to any conditions that it
  considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreement to provide alternative
  methods

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may enter into agreements with
  employers or employees to provide for alternative methods of
  establishing how many hours of insurable employment persons have and
  the Commission may at any time rescind the agreements.

## [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Employment Support Measures and National Employment Service]{.HTitleText1} {#h-216770 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purpose

**[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** The purpose of this Part
is to help maintain a sustainable employment insurance system through
the establishment of employment support measures and the maintenance of
a national employment service.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 56
- [2022, c. 10, s. 396](#1357857-1366654){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357857-1366654.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-56-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 56"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Guidelines

- **[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Employment support measures under
  this Part shall be established in accordance with the following
  guidelines:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} harmonization with provincial employment
    initiatives to ensure that there is no unnecessary overlap or
    duplication;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} reduction of dependency on unemployment benefits by
    helping individuals obtain or keep employment;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} co-operation and partnership with other
    governments, employers, community-based organizations and other
    interested organizations;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} flexibility to allow significant decisions about
    implementation to be made at a local level;

  - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} availability of assistance under the measures in
    either official language where there is significant demand for that
    assistance in that language;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} commitment by persons receiving assistance under
    the measures to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} achieving the goals of the assistance,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} taking primary responsibility for identifying
      their employment needs and locating services necessary to allow
      them to meet those needs, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if appropriate, sharing the cost of the
      assistance; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} implementation of the measures within a framework
    for evaluating their success in assisting persons to obtain or keep
    employment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Working in concert and consultations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} To give effect to the purpose and guidelines of this
  Part, the Commission shall work in concert with provincial governments
  and consult with workers and employers to align employment support
  measures with labour market needs.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2022, c. 10, s. 397\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 57
- [2022, c. 10, s. 397](#1357859-1366664){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357859-1366664.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-57-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 57"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[insured
participant]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** In this Part, [[insured
participant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an insured person who requests assistance under an
  employment support measure and, when requesting the assistance, is a
  person for whom a benefit period is established or whose benefit
  period has ended within the previous 60 months or a person who paid,
  in at least 3 of the last 10 years, employee's premiums that did not
  entitle them to a refund under subsection 96(4); and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an insured person who requests assistance under an
  employment support measure and, when requesting assistance, is a
  person who was in receipt of the employment insurance emergency
  response benefit within the previous 60 months.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 58
- 2015, c. 36, s. 153
- 2016, c. 7, s. 216
- 2017, c. 20, s. 306
- [2021, c. 23, s. 319](#1307975-1327330){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307975-1327330.html"}
- [2022, c. 10, s. 398](#1357872-1366667){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357872-1366667.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-58-20210926.html "Link to previous version of section 58"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Employment support measures

**[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** The Commission may
establish employment support measures to help insured participants and
other workers, including workers in groups underrepresented in the
labour market, to obtain or keep employment, including measures to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} provide insured participants with courses or programs
  of instruction or training;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} provide insured participants with employment
  opportunities or provide employment support;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} provide workers with employment assistance services;
  and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} support research, innovation or partnerships related
  to helping workers to prepare for, obtain or keep employment and to be
  productive participants in the labour market.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 59
- [2022, c. 10, s. 399](#1357874-1366676){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357874-1366676.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-59-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 59"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}National employment service

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission shall maintain a national employment service to provide
  information on employment opportunities across Canada to help workers
  find suitable employment and help employers find suitable workers.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duties of the Commission

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} collect information concerning employment for
    workers and workers seeking employment and, to the extent the
    Commission considers necessary, make the information available with
    a view to assisting workers to obtain employment for which they are
    suited and assisting employers to obtain workers most suitable to
    their needs; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that in referring a worker seeking
    employment there will be no discrimination on a prohibited ground of
    discrimination within the meaning of the [Canadian Human Rights
    Act](/eng/acts/H-6) or because of political affiliation, but nothing
    in this paragraph prohibits the national employment service from
    giving effect to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any limitation, specification or preference based
      on a [bona fide]{.otherLang lang="la"} occupational requirement,
      or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any special program, plan or arrangement
      mentioned in section 16 of the [Canadian Human Rights
      Act](/eng/acts/H-6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may, with the approval of the Governor
  in Council, make regulations for the purposes of subsections (1) and
  (2).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2022, c. 10, s. 400\]]{.Repealed}

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2022, c. 10, s. 400\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 60
- 2017, c. 20, s. 307
- [2022, c. 10, s. 400](#1357876-1366679){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357876-1366679.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-60-20180401.html "Link to previous version of section 60"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Financial assistance

**[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** The Commission may, in
accordance with terms and conditions approved by the Treasury Board,
provide financial assistance for the purpose of implementing employment
support measures.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 61
- 2001, c. 4, s. 75(E)
- [2022, c. 10, s. 401](#1357877-1366682){.wb-lbx

:::

Version](section-61-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 61"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreements for administering employment
support measures

**[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** The Commission may, with
the approval of the Minister, enter into an agreement or arrangement for
the administration of employment support measures on its behalf by a
department, board or agency of the Government of Canada, another
government or government agency in Canada or any other public or private
organization.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 62
- [2022, c. 10, s. 401](#1357877-1366687){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357877-1366687.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-62-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 62"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreements for paying costs of similar
benefits and measures

- **[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission may, with the approval of the Minister, enter into an
  agreement with a government or government agency in Canada or any
  other public or private organization to provide for the payment of
  contributions for all or a portion of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any costs of measures implemented by the
    government, government agency or organization that are consistent
    with the purpose and guidelines of this Part; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any administration costs that the government,
    government agency or organization incurs in implementing the
    measures.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2017, c. 20, s. 308\]]{.Repealed}

20, s. 309\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No appeal

**[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** A decision of the
Commission made in relation to employment support measures, other than a
decision under section 65.1, is not subject to review under section 112.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 64
- 2012, c. 19, s. 244
- [2022, c. 10, s. 403](#1357881-1366693){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357881-1366693.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-64-20130401.html "Link to previous version of section 64"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability for repayments

**[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** A person is liable to
repay the following amounts paid under section 61:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} principal and interest on a loan to the person;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an amount paid on a guarantee or suretyship of a loan
  made to the person; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an amount paid to the person to which the person is
  not entitled.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 65
- 2001, c. 4, s. 76(E)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Penalties

- **[[65.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may impose on a
  person to whom financial assistance has been provided under section 61
  a penalty for each of the following acts or omissions if the
  Commission becomes aware of facts that in its opinion establish that
  the person has

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to an application or request for the
    assistance,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} made a representation that the person knew was
      false or misleading, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} made a declaration that the person knew was
      false or misleading because of the non-disclosure of facts; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} without good cause failed to attend, carry out or
    complete the course, program or activity for which the assistance
    was provided or was expelled from it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum penalty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may set the amount of the penalty for
  each act or omission at not more than the amount of the financial
  assistance that was provided.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation on imposition of penalties

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The penalty shall not be imposed if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a prosecution for the act or omission has been
    initiated against the person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 36 months have passed since the day on which the
    act or omission occurred.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rescission, etc., of penalty

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may rescind the imposition of the
  penalty, or reduce the penalty, on the presentation of new facts or on
  being satisfied that the penalty was imposed without knowledge of, or
  on the basis of a mistake as to, some material fact.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 65.1
- 1999, c. 31, s. 78(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debts due to the Crown

- **[[65.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Amounts repayable under section 65
  and penalties under section 65.1 are debts due to Her Majesty and are
  recoverable in the Federal Court or any other court of competent
  jurisdiction or in any other manner provided by this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery by deduction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an amount becomes payable to the person under
  section 61, the amount of their indebtedness to Her Majesty may be
  recovered out of the amount payable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No amount due under this section may be recovered
  more than 72 months after the day on which the liability arose.

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[Premiums and Other Financial Matters]{.HTitleText1} {#h-216884 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-216885 .Subheading}

Definition of [[actuary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[65.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65.21 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[actuary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Fellow of the Canadian
Institute of Actuaries whose services are engaged by the Commission
under subsection 28(4) of the [Department of Employment and Social
Development Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2008, c. 28, s. 125
- 2012, c. 31, s. 434
- 2013, c. 40, s. 236
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-65.21-20130307.html "Link to previous version of section 65.21"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Premiums]{.HTitleText2} {#h-216890 .Subheading}

**[[65.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65.3 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2008, c.
28, s. 126\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-65.3-20051005.html "Link to previous version of section 65.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual premium rate setting

- **[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (7) and
  section 66.32, the Commission shall set the premium rate for each year
  in order to generate just enough premium revenue to ensure that, at
  the end of the seven-year period that commences at the beginning of
  that year, the total of the amounts credited to the Employment
  Insurance Operating Account after December 31, 2008 is equal to the
  total of the amounts charged to that Account after that date.

- [(1.1) and (1.2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2013, c. 40, s.
  126\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission shall set the premium rate based on

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information provided under sections 66.1 and
    66.2;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the actuary's report provided under section 66.3
    for that year;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2012, c. 19, s. 609\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any regulations made under section 69;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any changes, announced by the Minister on or before
    July 22 in a year, to payments to be made under paragraph
    77(1)(a), (b) or (c) during the following year; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any other information that the Commission considers
    relevant.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} \[No subsection (3)\]

- [(4) to (6)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2012, c. 19, s. 609\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Difference year to year

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The premium rate may not be increased or decreased by
  more than five one-hundredths of one per cent (0.05%) from one year to
  the next.

- [(7.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2013, c. 40, s. 126\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Governor in Council --- maximum change
  in premium rate

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} On the joint recommendation of the Minister and the
  Minister of Finance, the Governor in Council may change the maximum
  percentage referred to in subsection (7) by which the premium rate may
  be increased or decreased from one year to the next, if the Governor
  in Council considers it to be in the public interest.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limit

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} On or before September 14 in a year, the Commission
  shall set the premium rate for the following year.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 66
- 2005, c. 30, s. 126
- 2008, c. 28, s. 127
- 2009, c. 2, ss. 222, 230
- 2010, c. 12, s. 2204
- 2012, c. 19, s. 609, c. 31, s. 435
- 2013, c. 40, s. 126
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-66-20170101.html "Link to previous version of section 66"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information provided

- **[[66.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall, on or before
  July 22 in a year, provide the actuary and the Commission with the
  following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the Minister has made an announcement referred
    to in paragraph 66(2)(e), the forecast change in the amount of the
    payments to be made during each of the following seven years under
    paragraph 77(1)(a), (b) or (c), as the case may be;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the forecast costs to be paid under paragraphs
    77(1)(d), (d.1) and (g) during each of the following seven years,
    including any forecast change in those costs resulting from any
    change to the payments referred to in paragraph (a);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the total of the amounts charged to the Employment
    Insurance Operating Account as of the last day of the most recent
    month for which that total is known by the Minister; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any prescribed information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On the recommendation of the Minister, the Governor
  in Council may make regulations prescribing information referred to in
  paragraph (1)(d).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 5, s. 9
- 2005, c. 30, s. 126
- 2008, c. 28, s. 127
- 2010, c. 12, s. 2204
- 2012, c. 19, s. 610, c. 31, ss. 436, 462
- 2013, c. 40, s. 127
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-66.1-20131212.html "Link to previous version of section 66.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information provided

- **[[66.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of Finance shall, on or
  before July 22 in a year, provide the actuary and the Commission with
  the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the most current available forecast values of the
    economic variables that are relevant to the determination under
    section 66 of a premium rate for the following year;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amounts forecast under subparagraphs 77.1(a)(i)
    and (ii) and the total estimated under subparagraph 77.1(a)(iii);
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any prescribed information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On the recommendation of the Minister of Finance, the
  Governor in Council may make regulations prescribing information
  referred to in paragraph (1)(c).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2003, c. 15, s. 21
- 2005, c. 30, s. 126
- 2008, c. 28, s. 127
- 2010, c. 12, s. 2204
- 2012, c. 19, s. 611, c. 31, s. 437
- 2013, c. 40, s. 128
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-66.2-20131212.html "Link to previous version of section 66.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Actuary's report

**[[66.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.3 .sectionLabel}** The actuary shall
prepare actuarial forecasts and estimates for the purposes of sections
4, 66 and 69 and shall, on or before August 22 in a year, provide the
Commission with a report that sets out

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the forecast premium rate for the following year and
  a detailed analysis in support of the forecast;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the calculations performed for the purposes of
  sections 4 and 69;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the information provided under section 66.1; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the source of the data, the actuarial and economic
  assumptions and the actuarial methodology used.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2004, c. 22, s. 25
- 2005, c. 30, s. 126
- 2008, c. 28, s. 127
- 2012, c. 19, s. 612, c. 31, s. 438
- 2013, c. 40, s. 129
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-66.3-20130307.html "Link to previous version of section 66.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report and summary

- **[[66.31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.31
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Commission shall, on or before
  August 31 in a year, provide the Minister and the Minister of Finance
  with the report referred to in section 66.3 and a summary of that
  report.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Available to public

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission shall, on the day on which a premium
  rate is set under section 66 or 66.32, make available to the public
  the report and its summary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tabling in Parliament

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} After a premium rate is set, the Minister shall cause
  the report and its summary to be laid before each House of Parliament
  on any of the next 10 days during which that House is sitting.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2012, c. 31, s. 438
- 2013, c. 40, ss. 130, 131
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-66.31-20131212.html "Link to previous version of section 66.31"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Premium rate setting by Governor in
Council

- **[[66.32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.32
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On the joint recommendation of the
  Minister and the Minister of Finance, the Governor in Council may, on
  or before September 30 in a year,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the Governor in Council considers it to be in
    the public interest, substitute a premium rate for the following
    year that is different from the one set by the Commission under
    section 66; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the Commission has not set a premium rate under
    that section by September 14 in the year, set one for the following
    year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsection 66(7)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 66(7) does not apply to the setting of the
  premium rate under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2013, c. 40, s. 131
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding percentage rates

**[[66.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.4 .sectionLabel}** If the calculation of
a premium rate under section 66 or 66.32 results in a rate that includes
a fraction of one per cent, the resulting percentage is to be rounded to
the nearest one hundredth of one per cent or, if the resulting
percentage is equidistant from two one-hundredths of one percent, to the
higher of them.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 30, s. 126
- 2008, c. 28, s. 127
- 2012, c. 31, s. 438
- 2013, c. 40, s. 131
:::

[Previous
Version](section-66.4-20130307.html "Link to previous version of section 66.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication in [Canada
Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/)

**[[66.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.5 .sectionLabel}** As soon as possible
after a premium rate is set under section 66 or 66.32, the Commission
shall publish it in Part I of the [Canada
Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

::: HistoricalNote

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Service Fees Act](/eng/acts/S-8.4)

**[[66.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66.6 .sectionLabel}** For greater
certainty, the [Service Fees Act](/eng/acts/S-8.4) does not apply in
respect of the premium rate set under section 66 or 66.32 or the
premiums determined under sections 67 and 68.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 30, s. 126
- 2012, c. 31, s. 438
- 2013, c. 40, s. 131

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Employee's premium

**[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 70, a
person employed in insurable employment shall pay, by deduction as
provided in subsection 82(1), a premium equal to their insurable
earnings multiplied by the premium rate set under section 66 or 66.32,
as the case may be.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 67
- 2001, c. 5, s. 10
- 2003, c. 15, s. 21
- 2004, c. 22, s. 26
- 2005, c. 30, s. 126
- 2013, c. 40, ss. 131, 133
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-67-20131212.html "Link to previous version of section 67"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Employer's premium

**[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 69
and 70, an employer shall pay a premium equal to 1.4 times the
employees' premiums that the employer is required to deduct under
subsection 82(1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Premium reduction --- wage-loss plans

- **[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission shall, with the approval of the Governor in Council, make
  regulations to provide a system for reducing the employer's premium
  when

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the payment of any allowances, money or other
    benefits because of illness, injury, quarantine, pregnancy, child
    care, compassionate care, a child's critical illness or an adult's
    critical illness under a plan that covers insured persons employed
    by the employer, other than one established under a provincial law,
    would have the effect of reducing the special benefits payable to
    the insured persons; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the insured persons will benefit from the reduction
    of the employer's premium in an amount at least equal to 5/12 of the
    reduction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provincial plans

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission shall, with the approval of the
  Governor in Council, make regulations to provide a system for reducing
  the employer's and employee's premiums, the premiums under Part VII.1
  or all those premiums, when the payment of any allowances, money or
  other benefits because of illness, injury, quarantine, pregnancy,
  child care, compassionate care, a child's critical illness or an
  adult's critical illness under a provincial law to insured persons, or
  to self-employed persons, as the case may be, would have the effect of
  reducing or eliminating the special benefits payable to those insured
  persons or the benefits payable to those self-employed persons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Included provisions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The regulations may include provisions

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner and time for making an
    application for a premium reduction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the standards that must be met by a
    plan to qualify for a premium reduction and the time during which
    the plan must be in effect;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the method for determining the amount
    of reduction for plans that meet the prescribed standards and the
    use to be made of actuarial calculations and estimates;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner in which insured persons are
    to benefit from the premium reduction;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} providing for the making of decisions relating to
    premium reduction and appeals in cases of dispute;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} prescribing how the insured earnings of insured
    persons will be reported by employers to the Canada Revenue Agency;
    and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} generally, providing for any other matters
    necessary for carrying out the purposes and provisions of
    subsections (1) and (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Late applications

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If an application for an employer's premium reduction
  is made within 36 months after the time prescribed for making it, the
  Commission may, subject to prescribed conditions, regard the
  application as having been made at the prescribed time if the
  applicant shows that there was good cause for the delay throughout the
  period beginning at the prescribed time and ending on the day when the
  application was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reconsideration of application

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may reconsider any decision relating
  to an employer's premium reduction within 36 months after the date of
  the decision and any new decision that it makes shall apply in place
  of the reconsidered decision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The reference to the payment of allowances, money or
  other benefits because of compassionate care in subsections (1)
  and (2) means the payment of allowances, money or other benefits for
  the same or substantially the same reasons for which benefits are
  payable under section 23.1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The reference to the payment of allowances, money or
  other benefits because of a child's critical illness in
  subsections (1) and (2) means the payment of allowances, money or
  other benefits for the same or substantially the same reasons for
  which benefits are payable under section 23.2.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The reference to the payment of allowances, money or
  other benefits because of an adult's critical illness in
  subsections (1) and (2) means the payment of allowances, money or
  other benefits for the same or substantially the same reasons for
  which benefits are payable under section 23.3.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 69
- 1999, c. 17, s. 135
- 2003, c. 15, s. 22
- 2005, c. 38, s. 138
- 2009, c. 33, s. 10
- 2012, c. 27, s. 20
- 2017, c. 20, s. 241
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-69-20130609.html "Link to previous version of section 69"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overlapping pay periods

**[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** If insurable earnings are
paid to a person after the end of the year in which their insurable
employment occurred, the insurable employment is, for the purposes of
determining insurable earnings and premiums payable, deemed to have
occurred in the year in which the insurable earnings are paid.

**[[70.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70.1 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2012, c.
19, s. 613\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-70.1-20100923.html "Link to previous version of section 70.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Employment Insurance Operating Account]{.HTitleText2} {#h-217011 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Employment Insurance Operating Account
established

**[[70.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70.2 .sectionLabel}** There is established
in the accounts of Canada an account to be known as the Employment
Insurance Operating Account.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2010, c. 12, s. 2185
:::

**[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2010, c. 12,
s. 2186\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-71-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 71"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment into Consolidated Revenue Fund

**[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** There shall be paid into
the Consolidated Revenue Fund

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} all amounts received under Parts I and III to IX, as
  or on account of premiums, fines, penalties, interest, repayment of
  overpaid benefits and benefit repayment;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} all amounts collected by the Commission for services
  rendered to other government departments or agencies or to the public;
  and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} all amounts received on account of principal or
  interest on loans made by the Commission under Part II or as repayment
  of overpayments made by the Commission under that Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Credits to Employment Insurance Operating
Account

**[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** There shall be credited
to the Employment Insurance Operating Account and charged to the
Consolidated Revenue Fund

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an amount in each year equal to the amount receivable
  as or on account of premiums payable for that year under this Act;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any other amounts provided out of the Consolidated
  Revenue Fund appropriated by Parliament for any purpose related to
  employment insurance and administered by the Commission; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an amount equal to all benefit repayments receivable
  under Part VII.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 73
- 2010, c. 12, s. 2194
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-73-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 73"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefit enhancements under this Act

**[[73.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73.1 .sectionLabel}** There shall be
credited to the Employment Insurance Operating Account on August 1, 2010
the amount determined by the Minister of Finance that corresponds to the
cost of the benefit enhancement measures under this Act, provided for in
the budget tabled in Parliament on January 27, 2009 in which the cost is
estimated to be \$2,900,000,000.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 2, s. 223
- 2010, c. 12, s. 2194
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-73.1-20090312.html "Link to previous version of section 73.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Government premiums

**[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** There shall be credited
to the Employment Insurance Operating Account and charged to the
Consolidated Revenue Fund an amount equal to the premiums required to be
paid by Her Majesty in right of Canada as employer's premiums for
persons employed in insurable employment by Her Majesty in right of
Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 74
- 2010, c. 12, s. 2194
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-74-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 74"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other credits to Employment Insurance
Operating Account

**[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** There shall be credited
to the Employment Insurance Operating Account all amounts paid into the
Consolidated Revenue Fund that are

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} received as or on account of penalties imposed under
  section 38, 39 or 65.1 and repayments of overpaid benefits, except
  interest and penalties on benefit repayment;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} collected by the Commission for services rendered to
  other government departments or agencies or to the public;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} received on account of principal or interest on loans
  made by the Commission under Part II;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} received as repayments of overpayments by the
  Commission under section 61 for employment support measures authorized
  by Part II;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} received as repayments of overpayments by the
  Commission under agreements entered into under section 63; or

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} received as interest under section 80.1.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 75
- 2010, c. 12, s. 2194
- [2022, c. 10, s. 404](#1357883-1366695){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1357883-1366695.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-75-20100712.html "Link to previous version of section 75"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2010, c. 12,
s. 2187\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 76"){.PITLink}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charges to the Account

- **[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} There
  shall be paid out of the Consolidated Revenue Fund and charged to the
  Employment Insurance Operating Account

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all amounts paid as or on account of benefits under
    this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} all amounts paid under section 61 for employment
    support measures;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all amounts paid under paragraph 63(1)(a);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the costs of administering this Act, including
    administration fees or costs paid under section 62 or paragraph
    63(1)(b);

  - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} the costs of administering sections 28.1 and 28.2
    of the [Department of Employment and Social Development
    Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7); and

  - [(e) and (f)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2012, c. 31, s.
    439\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the costs of administering Part 5 of the
    [Department of Employment and Social Development
    Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7) in respect of appeals respecting this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment by special warrants

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding the [Financial Administration
  Act](/eng/acts/F-11), amounts mentioned in paragraph (1)(a) shall be
  paid by special warrants drawn on the Receiver General and issued by
  the Commission by electronic means or bearing the printed signature of
  the Chairperson and Vice-Chairperson of the Commission, and amounts
  mentioned in paragraphs (1)(b) and (c) may be paid by the special
  warrants.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No charge for negotiation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The special warrants are negotiable without charge at
  any financial institution in Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Forecasts and estimates

- **[[77.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77.1 .sectionLabel}** []{.lawlabel} On or
  before July 22 in a year,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister of Finance shall

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} forecast the amount to be credited to the
      Employment Insurance Operating Account under sections 73 to 75
      during the year,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} forecast the amount to be charged, during the
      year, to that Account under subsection 77(1) based on, among other
      things, information provided by the Minister, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} estimate the total, at December 31 of the
      immediately preceding year, of the amounts credited to that
      Account; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister shall estimate the total, at December
    31 of the immediately preceding year, of the amounts charged to that
    Account.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum amount that may be paid under
Part II

**[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** The total amount that may
be paid out by the Commission under section 61 and paragraph 63(1)(a)
and charged to the Employment Insurance Operating Account under this
Part in a fiscal year must not exceed 0.8% of the insurable earnings of
all insured persons from which the prescribed amount is deducted under
subsection 82(1) in that year as or on account of employee's premiums,
as estimated by the Commission and set out in the Main Estimates tabled
in Parliament.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Plan

**[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** The Minister shall, with
the concurrence of the Minister of Finance,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} submit to the Treasury Board for approval a plan for
  each fiscal year estimating the amounts to be paid for that year under
  Part II; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} have the plan included in the Main Estimates tabled
  in Parliament for the fiscal year.
