### [Objection and Review]{.HTitleText2} {#h-217531 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal to the Tax Court of Canada

- **[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commission or a person affected by a decision on an appeal to the
  Minister under section 91 or 92 may appeal from the decision to the
  Tax Court of Canada in accordance with the [Tax Court of Canada
  Act](/eng/acts/T-2) and the applicable rules of court made thereunder
  within 90 days after the decision is communicated to the Commission or
  the person, or within such longer time as the Court allows on
  application made to it within 90 days after the expiration of those 90
  days.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time to appeal

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Section 167, except paragraph 167(5)(a), of the
  [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) applies, with such modifications as
  the circumstances require, in respect of applications made under
  subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Communication of decision

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The determination of the time at which a decision on
  an appeal to the Minister under section 91 or 92 is communicated to
  the Commission or to a person shall be made in accordance with the
  rule, if any, made under paragraph 20(1.1)(h.1) of the [Tax Court of
  Canada Act](/eng/acts/T-2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On an appeal, the Tax Court of Canada

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may vacate, confirm or vary a decision on an appeal
    under section 91 or an assessment that is the subject of an appeal
    under section 92;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an appeal under section 92, may
    refer the matter back to the Minister for reconsideration and
    reassessment;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} shall notify in writing the parties to the appeal
    of its decision; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} give reasons for its decision but, except where the
    Court deems it advisable in a particular case to give reasons in
    writing, the reasons given by it need not be in writing.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 103
- 1998, c. 19, s. 268
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority to decide questions

- **[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Tax Court of Canada and the Minister have authority to decide any
  question of fact or law necessary to be decided in the course of an
  appeal under section 91 or 103 or to reconsider an assessment under
  section 92 and to decide whether a person may be or is affected by the
  decision or assessment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decisions and rulings final

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided in this Act, a decision
  of the Tax Court of Canada or the Minister and a ruling of an
  authorized officer under section 90 are final and binding for all
  purposes of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Allowance for attending appeal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If, on an appeal to the Tax Court of Canada from a
  decision of the Minister, a person affected by the decision is
  requested by the Court to attend before it on the consideration of the
  appeal and so attends, the person shall be paid such travel and other
  allowances, including compensation for loss of remunerative time, as
  are approved by the Treasury Board.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision final

**[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105 .sectionLabel}** The decision of the Tax
Court of Canada under section 103 is final and, except for an appeal
under the [Federal Courts Act](/eng/acts/F-7), is not subject to appeal
to or review by any court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 105
- 2002, c. 8, s. 182
- 2006, c. 11, s. 19
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-105-20030702.html "Link to previous version of section 105"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Offences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-217558 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence and punishment

- **[[106]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every employer who contravenes
  subsection 82(1) or 86(2) is guilty of an offence and, in addition to
  any penalty otherwise provided, is liable on summary conviction to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a fine of not more than \$5,000; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} both the fine and imprisonment for a term of not
    more than six months.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence and punishment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who contravenes section 87 or 88 is
  guilty of an offence punishable on summary conviction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence and punishment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every person who contravenes regulations made under
  paragraph 108(1)(a) or (b) is guilty of an offence and, in addition to
  any penalty otherwise provided, is liable on summary conviction to a
  fine of not less than \$25 a day for each day of default, but not more
  than \$1,000 in all.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every person is guilty of an offence who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} makes, or participates in, assents to or acquiesces
    in the making of, false or deceptive statements in a return,
    certificate, statement or answer filed or made as required by or
    under this Part or Part VII.1 or the regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to evade payment of a premium imposed by this Act,
    destroys, alters, mutilates, secretes or otherwise disposes of the
    records or books of account of an employer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} makes false or deceptive entries in records or
    books of account of an employer, omits to enter a material
    particular in the records or books of account or assents to or
    acquiesces in the making or omission of the entries;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} wilfully, in any manner, evades or attempts to
    evade compliance with this Act or payment of premiums imposed by
    this Act; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} conspires with any person to commit an offence
    described in paragraphs (a) to (d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any penalty otherwise provided, the
  person is liable on summary conviction to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a fine of not less than \$25 and not more than
    \$5,000 plus, in an appropriate case, not more than double the
    amount of the premium that should have been shown to be payable or
    that was sought to be evaded; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} both the fine and imprisonment for a term of not
    more than six months.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability to pay penalty

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a person has been convicted under this Part for
  contravening subsection 82(1) or regulations made under paragraph
  108(1)(a) or (b), the person is not liable to pay a penalty imposed
  under section 82 or under any regulation made under section 108 for
  the same contravention unless the person was assessed for that penalty
  or it was demanded from them before the information or complaint
  giving rise to the conviction was laid or made.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 106
- 2009, c. 33, s. 13
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-106-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 106"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Officers, etc., of corporations

**[[107]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107 .sectionLabel}** If a corporation
commits an offence under this Part, any officer, director or agent of
the corporation who directed, authorized, assented to, acquiesced in or
participated in the commission of the offence is a party to and guilty
of the offence and is liable on conviction to the punishment provided
for the offence, whether or not the corporation has been prosecuted or
convicted.

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-217585 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[108]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-108 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may, with the approval of the Governor in Council, make
  regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} requiring any class of persons to file information
    returns respecting any class of information required in connection
    with premiums under this Act, including information respecting
    premiums of persons employed by any of those persons identified by
    the province in which those persons were employed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} requiring a person who is, by regulations made
    under paragraph (a), required to file an information return to
    supply a copy of the return or a prescribed portion of it to the
    person or persons in respect of whose premiums the return or portion
    relates;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing a penalty for non-compliance with
    regulations made under paragraph (a) or (b) equal to the greater of
    \$100 and the product obtained when \$25 is multiplied by the number
    of days during which the non-compliance occurs, to a maximum of
    \$2,500;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the manner in which any provision of
    this Act that applies or extends to an employer of an insured person
    shall apply or extend to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person by whom the remuneration of an insured
      person for services performed in insurable employment is paid
      either wholly or in part, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the employer of that person;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} for permitting an employer to deduct premiums paid
    on behalf of insured persons otherwise than from the remuneration
    for the period for which the premiums were payable;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} providing that, in any case or class of cases where
    insured persons work

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} under the general control or direct supervision
      of or are paid by a person other than their actual employer, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} with the concurrence of a person other than
      their actual employer on premises or property with respect to
      which that person has any rights or privileges under a licence,
      permit or agreement,

    the other person is, for the purposes of paying premiums, deemed to
    be the employer of the insured persons in addition to the actual
    employer, and providing for the payment and recovery of premiums
    paid for the insured persons;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} for defining and determining earnings, pay periods
    and the amount of insurable earnings of insured persons and for
    allocating their earnings to any period of insurable employment;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} for determining the amount of premiums payable;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for prescribing and regulating the manner,
    conditions and times for paying and recording premiums;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} for determining the earnings and premiums paid or
    payable for one or more insured persons employed by an employer who
    has failed to keep books, records or accounts as required under this
    Act;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} for regulating the possession, custody or control
    of documents or things used in the administration of this Act;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} for the registration of employers;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} for allocating to particular insured persons
    payments of premiums made by an employer;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} regulating the procedure to be followed in making
    rulings or deciding appeals under sections 90 to 92;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} for defining and determining whether employers are
    associated and determining how any refund under section 96 is to be
    allocated to them; and

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} prescribing or providing for anything that, by this
    Part, is to be prescribed or is to be provided for by regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may authorize an officer or a class of
  officers to exercise powers or perform duties of the Minister under
  this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative method of calculation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister considers that it is not possible to
  apply any of the regulations, the Minister may, on his or her own
  initiative or on the request of an employer, approve another method or
  methods of defining and determining insurable earnings and determining
  the premiums payable for them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minister may alter or rescind method

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may at any time alter or rescind the
  approved method, subject to such conditions, if any, as the Minister
  considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effective date of certain regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Regulations made under paragraph (1)(p) prescribing
  or providing for anything mentioned in subsection 82(1) take effect on
  the day they are published in the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) or on any later or earlier day
  specified in the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 108
- 1998, c. 19, s. 269
:::

## [PART V]{.HLabel1}[Pilot Projects]{.HTitleText1} {#h-217620 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[109]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109 .sectionLabel}** Notwithstanding
anything in this Act, the Commission may, with the approval of the
Governor in Council, make such regulations as it deems necessary
respecting the establishment and operation of pilot projects for testing
whether or which possible amendments to this Act or the regulations
would make this Act or the regulations more consistent with current
industry employment practices, trends or patterns or would improve
service to the public, including regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the time and manner in which employers are
  to supply their employees or former employees or the Commission with
  information on their employment history;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} providing for the use in a pilot project

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} of gross earnings, as defined by regulation, or
    prescribed amounts that are functions of gross earnings, as so
    defined, for any purpose for which insurable earnings, maximum
    insurable earnings or weekly insurable earnings are relevant to the
    operation of this Act, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} of periods other than weeks, for any purpose for
    which a period of weeks or a number of weeks is relevant to the
    operation of this Act;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} providing for the application of a pilot project in
  respect of one or more of the following:

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} prescribed employers or groups or classes of
    employers, including groups or classes consisting of randomly
    selected employers,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed areas, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed claimants, employees, former employees
    or groups or classes of claimants, employees or former employees,
    including groups or classes consisting of randomly selected
    claimants, employees or former employees; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} respecting the manner in which and the extent to
  which any provision of this Act or the regulations applies to a pilot
  project, and adapting any such provision for the purposes of that
  application.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expiration of regulations

**[[110]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-110 .sectionLabel}** Regulations made under
this Part that are not repealed cease to have effect three years after
they come into force.

## [PART VI]{.HLabel1}[Administrative Provisions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-217634 .Part}

### [Administrative Review]{.HTitleText2} {#h-217635 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rescission or amendment of decision

**[[111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111 .sectionLabel}** The Commission may
rescind or amend a decision given in any particular claim for benefits
if new facts are presented or if it is satisfied that the decision was
given without knowledge of, or was based on a mistake as to, some
material fact.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 111
- 2012, c. 19, s. 247
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-111-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 111"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reconsideration --- Commission

- **[[112]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-112 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  claimant or other person who is the subject of a decision of the
  Commission, or the employer of the claimant, may make a request to the
  Commission in the prescribed form and manner for a reconsideration of
  that decision at any time within

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 30 days after the day on which a decision is
    communicated to them; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any further time that the Commission may allow.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reconsideration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission must reconsider its decision if a
  request is made under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may make regulations setting
  out the circumstances in which the Commission may allow a longer
  period to make a request under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 112
- 1998, c. 19, s. 270
- 1999, c. 31, s. 81(F)
- 2002, c. 8, s. 135
- 2012, c. 19, s. 247
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-112-20030702.html "Link to previous version of section 112"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision not reviewable

**[[112.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-112.1 .sectionLabel}** A decision of the
Commission made under the [Employment Insurance
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332) respecting the writing off of
any penalty owing, amount payable or interest accrued on any penalty
owing or amount payable is not subject to review under section 112.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 39, s. 226
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal to Social Security Tribunal

**[[113]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-113 .sectionLabel}** A party who is
dissatisfied with a decision of the Commission made under section 112,
including a decision in relation to further time to make a request, may
appeal the decision to the Social Security Tribunal established under
section 44 of the [Department of Employment and Social Development
Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 113
- 2012, c. 19, s. 247
- 2013, c. 40, s. 236
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-113-20130401.html "Link to previous version of section 113"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of benefit pending appeal

- **[[114]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-114 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  claim for benefits is allowed by the General Division of the Social
  Security Tribunal established under section 44 of the [Department of
  Employment and Social Development Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7), benefits are
  payable in accordance with the decision of the Tribunal even though an
  appeal is pending, and any benefits paid under this section after the
  Tribunal's decision are to be treated as having been duly paid and are
  not recoverable from the claimant, even if the final determination of
  the question is adverse to the claimant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the appeal to the Appeal Division of the Social
    Security Tribunal was brought within 21 days after the day on which
    the decision of the General Division of the Social Security Tribunal
    was given and on the ground that the claimant ought to be
    disentitled under section 36; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case that the Commission may, with the
    approval of the Governor in Council, prescribe by regulation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 114
- 2012, c. 19, s. 247
- 2013, c. 40, s. 236
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-114-20130401.html "Link to previous version of section 114"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[115]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-115 .sectionLabel}** The Commission may,
with the approval of the Governor in Council, make regulations
prescribing the procedure to be followed in the reconsideration of
decisions under section 112.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 115
- 2012, c. 19, s. 247
:::

[Previous
Version](section-115-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 115"){.PITLink}

### [Investigations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-217680 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investigation by Commission

- **[[124]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may direct the Commission to investigate and
  report on all matters that the Governor in Council deems advisable or
  necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission has all the powers of a commissioner
  under Part I of the [Inquiries Act](/eng/acts/I-11) for the purpose of
  conducting an investigation under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Commission shall give such public notice as it
  considers sufficient of its intention to investigate any matters that
  under this Act it is empowered to investigate, and it shall receive
  representations submitted to it by persons or associations of persons
  appearing to the Commission to have an interest in the matters under
  investigation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall lay every report made under this
  section before Parliament within 30 days after it is submitted to the
  Governor in Council or, if Parliament is not then sitting, on any of
  the first 30 days that either House of Parliament is sitting after its
  submission.

### [Enforcement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-217690 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information or complaint

- **[[125]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-125 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  information or complaint under this Act, other than Part IV, may be
  laid or made by a member of the Royal Canadian Mounted Police or by a
  person acting for the Commission and, if an information or complaint
  appears to have been laid or made under this Act, other than Part IV,
  it is deemed to have been laid or made by a person acting for the
  Commission and shall not be called into question for lack of authority
  of the informant or complainant except by the Commission or by a
  person acting for it or for Her Majesty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Two or more offences

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An information or complaint about an offence under
  this Act, other than Part IV, may be for one or more offences and no
  information, complaint, warrant, conviction or other proceeding in a
  prosecution under this Act, other than Part IV, is objectionable or
  insufficient because it relates to two or more offences.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Territorial jurisdiction

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An information or complaint about an offence under
  this Act, other than Part IV, may be heard, tried or determined by any
  provincial court judge, as defined in section 2 of the [Criminal
  Code](/eng/acts/C-46), if the accused is resident, carrying on
  business, found, apprehended or in custody within the judge's
  territorial jurisdiction although the matter of the information or
  complaint did not arise within that jurisdiction.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation of prosecutions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A prosecution for an offence under this Act, other
  than Part IV, may be commenced at any time within five years after the
  Commission became aware of the subject-matter of the prosecution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A document appearing to have been issued by the
  Commission, certifying the day on which it became aware of the
  subject-matter of the prosecution, is admissible in evidence and shall
  be considered conclusive proof of that fact without proof of the
  signature or the official character of the person appearing to have
  signed the document and without any other proof.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of personal service

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If provision is made by this Act, other than Part IV,
  or the regulations for personal service of a request for information,
  notice or demand, an affidavit of a person acting for the Commission
  stating that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person has charge of the appropriate records
    and has knowledge of the facts in the particular case,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} such a request, notice or demand was served
    personally on a named day on the person to whom it was directed, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person identifies as an exhibit attached to the
    affidavit a true copy of the request, notice or demand,

  is evidence of the personal service and of the request, notice or
  demand.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of failure to comply

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If a person is required by this Act, other than Part
  IV, or the regulations to make a return, statement, answer or
  certificate, an affidavit of a person acting for the Commission
  stating that the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has charge of the appropriate records, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after a careful examination and search of the
    records, has been unable to find in a given case that the return,
    statement, answer or certificate, as the case may be, has been filed
    or made by the person required to do so,

  is evidence that in that case the person did not do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of time of compliance

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If a person is required by this Act, other than Part
  IV, or the regulations to make a return, statement, answer or
  certificate, an affidavit of a person acting for the Commission
  stating that the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has charge of the appropriate records, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after careful examination of the records, has found
    that the return, statement, answer or certificate was filed or made
    on a particular day,

  is evidence that it was filed or made on that day and not before.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of documents

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} An affidavit of a person acting for the Commission
  stating that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person has charge of the appropriate records,
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a document annexed to the affidavit is a document
    or true copy of a document made by or for an employer, the
    Commission or a person acting for the Commission,

  is evidence of the nature and contents of the document and is
  admissible in evidence and has the same probative force as the
  original document would have if it were proven in the ordinary way.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presumption

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} If evidence is offered under this section by an
  affidavit from which it appears that the person making the affidavit
  is acting for the Commission, it is not necessary to prove

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person's signature;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that the person is acting for the Commission; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the signature or official character of the person
    before whom the affidavit was sworn.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Judicial notice

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} Judicial notice shall be taken of all orders made
  under this Act, other than Part IV, without the orders being specially
  pleaded or proven.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of documents

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} Every document appearing to be an order, direction,
  demand, notice, certificate, requirement, decision or other document
  executed under, or in the course of, the administration or enforcement
  of this Act, other than Part IV, over the name in writing of the
  Commission, or a person acting for the Commission under this Act,
  other than Part IV, is deemed to be a document signed, made and issued
  by the Commission or the person unless it has been called into
  question by the Commission or by a person acting for it or for Her
  Majesty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Forms authorized

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} A form that appears to be authorized by the
  Commission is deemed to be a form authorized by the Commission under
  this Act, other than Part IV, unless called into question by the
  Commission or a person acting for it or for Her Majesty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of return in prosecution

  [(14)]{.lawlabel} In a prosecution for an offence under this Act,
  other than Part IV, the production of a return, certificate, statement
  or answer required by or under this Act, other than Part IV, or the
  regulations appearing to have been filed or delivered by or for the
  person charged with the offence or to have been made or signed by or
  for the person is, in the absence of evidence to the contrary, proof
  that the return, certificate, statement or answer was filed or
  delivered by or for that person or was made or signed by or for them.

- [(15)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2012, c. 19, s. 248\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of records

  [(16)]{.lawlabel} In a prosecution for an offence under this Act, an
  affidavit of a person acting for the Commission stating that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person has charge of the appropriate records,
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an examination of the records shows that an amount
    required under this Act to be remitted to the Receiver General on
    account of fines, penalties, interest and repayment of overpayments
    of benefits has not been received by the Receiver General,

  is evidence of the statements contained in the affidavit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Officers, etc., of corporations

  [(17)]{.lawlabel} If a corporation commits an offence under this Act,
  other than Part IV, any officer, director or agent of the corporation
  who directed, authorized, assented to, acquiesced in or participated
  in the commission of the offence is a party to and guilty of the
  offence and is liable on conviction to the punishment provided for the
  offence whether or not the corporation has been prosecuted or
  convicted.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 125
- 2012, c. 19, s. 248
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-125-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 125"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificates

- **[[126]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-126 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  amount or part of an amount payable under Part I, II or VII.1 that has
  not been paid may be certified by the Commission

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} without delay, if in the opinion of the Commission
    the person liable to pay the amount is attempting to avoid payment;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, on the expiration of 30 days
    after the default.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Judgments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On production to the Federal Court, the resulting
  certificate shall be registered in the Court and when registered has
  the same force and effect, and all proceedings may be taken, as if the
  certificate were a judgment obtained in the Court for a debt of the
  amount specified in the certificate plus interest to the day of
  payment as provided for in this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Costs

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} All reasonable costs and charges attendant on the
  registration of the certificate are recoverable in like manner as if
  they had been certified and the certificate had been registered under
  this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Garnishment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Commission has knowledge or suspects that a
  person is or is about to become indebted or liable to make a payment
  to a person liable to make a payment under Part I, II or VII.1 or
  under subsection (7), it may, by a notice served personally or sent by
  a confirmed delivery service, require the first person to pay the
  money otherwise payable to the second person in whole or in part to
  the Receiver General on account of the second person's liability.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicability to future payments

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the Commission has, under subsection (4), required
  an employer to pay to the Receiver General on account of an insured
  person's liability under Part I or II or an individual's liability
  under Part VII.I, money otherwise payable by the employer to the
  insured person or the individual, as the case may be, as remuneration,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the requirement is applicable to all future
    payments by the employer to the insured person or individual, as the
    case may be, as remuneration until the liability under that Part is
    satisfied; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the employer shall make payments to the Receiver
    General out of each payment of remuneration of the amount that may
    be stipulated by the Commission in the notice mentioned in
    subsection (4).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discharge of liability

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The receipt of the Commission for money paid as
  required under subsection (4) or (5) is a good and sufficient
  discharge of the original liability to the extent of the payment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt due to the Crown

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} An amount not paid as required by a notice under
  subsection (4) or (5) is a debt due to Her Majesty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service of person carrying on business
  under another name

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A notice mentioned in subsection (4) may be addressed
  to the name or style under which a person carries on business if the
  person carries on business under a name or style other than their own
  name and, in the case of personal service, the notice is validly
  served if it is left with an adult person employed at the place of
  business of the addressee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service of partnership

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A notice mentioned in subsection (4) may be addressed
  to a partnership name if the person who is to receive it carries on
  business in partnership under that name and, in the case of personal
  service, the notice is validly served if it is served on one of the
  partners or left with an adult person employed at the place of
  business of the partnership.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inspections

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} An authorized person may, at any reasonable time,
  for any purpose relating to the administration or enforcement of this
  Act, other than Part IV, inspect or examine any document that relates
  or may relate to the information that is or should be contained in the
  records or books of account or to the amount of any benefits payable
  under this Act and, for those purposes, the authorized person may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection (11), enter any premises or
    place where the authorized person believes, on reasonable grounds,
    that persons are or were employed or where any records or books of
    account are or should be kept; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} require the owner, occupant or person in charge of
    the premises or place to give the authorized person all reasonable
    assistance and to answer all proper questions relating to the
    administration or enforcement of this Act, other than Part IV, and,
    for that purpose, require the owner, occupant or person in charge to
    attend at the premises or place with the authorized person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warrant required to enter
  dwelling-house

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} If the premises or place is a dwelling-house, an
  authorized person may only enter with the consent of the occupant or
  under the authority of a warrant issued under subsection (12).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Warrant or order

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} If, on [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} application
  by the Commission, a judge is satisfied by information on oath that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are reasonable grounds for believing that a
    dwelling-house is a premises or place mentioned in subsection (10),

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} entry into the dwelling-house is necessary for any
    purpose relating to the administration or enforcement of this Act,
    other than Part IV, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} entry into the dwelling-house has been refused or
    there are reasonable grounds for believing that entry will be
    refused,

  the judge may issue a warrant authorizing an authorized person to
  enter the dwelling-house subject to such conditions as may be
  specified in the warrant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order for access to documents, etc.

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} If the judge is not satisfied that entry into the
  dwelling-house is necessary for any purpose relating to the
  administration or enforcement of this Act, other than Part IV, the
  judge may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} order the occupant of the dwelling-house to provide
    an authorized person with reasonable access to any document that is
    or should be kept in the dwelling-house, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} make such other order as is appropriate in the
    circumstances to carry out the purposes of this Act, other than Part
    IV,

  to the extent that access has been or may be expected to be refused
  and that the document is or may be expected to be kept in the
  dwelling-house.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to provide documents and
  information

  [(14)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding any other provision of this Act, but
  subject to subsection (15), the Commission may for any purpose
  relating to the administration or enforcement of this Act, other than
  Part IV, by notice served personally or by confirmed delivery service,
  require that any person provide, within such reasonable time as is
  stated in the notice,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any information or additional information,
    including any information return or supplementary return; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Unnamed persons

  [(15)]{.lawlabel} The Commission shall not impose on any person, in
  this section referred to as a "third party", a requirement under
  subsection (14) to provide information or any document relating to one
  or more unnamed persons unless it first obtains the authorization of a
  judge under subsection (16).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Judicial authorization

  [(16)]{.lawlabel} On [ex parte]{.otherLang lang="la"} application by
  the Commission, a judge may, subject to such conditions as the judge
  considers appropriate, authorize the Commission to impose on a third
  party a requirement under subsection (14) relating to an unnamed
  person or more than one unnamed person, in this section referred to as
  the "group", if the judge is satisfied by information on oath that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person or group is ascertainable; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the requirement is made to verify compliance by the
    person or persons in the group with any duty or obligation under
    this Act, other than Part IV.

  - [(c) and (d)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1998, c. 19, s.
    271\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service of authorization

  [(17)]{.lawlabel} If the authorization is granted, it shall be served
  together with the notice mentioned in subsection (14).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review of authorization

  [(18)]{.lawlabel} If the authorization is granted, a third party on
  whom it is served may, within 15 days after it is served, apply for a
  review of the authorization to the judge who granted it or, if the
  judge is unable to act, to another judge of the same court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers on review

  [(19)]{.lawlabel} On hearing the application, the judge may cancel the
  authorization previously granted if the judge is not then satisfied
  that the conditions in paragraphs (16)(a) and (b) have been met and
  the judge may confirm or vary the authorization if satisfied that
  those conditions have been met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies as evidence

  [(20)]{.lawlabel} If a document is inspected, examined or provided in
  accordance with subsection (10) or (14),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person by whom it is inspected or examined or
    to whom it is provided, or any other person acting for the
    Commission, may make one or more copies, or have them made; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any document appearing to be certified by the
    Commission or an authorized person to be a copy made under this
    subsection is evidence of the nature and content of the original
    document and has the same probative force as the original document
    would have if it were proven in the ordinary way.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance

  [(21)]{.lawlabel} No person shall hinder, molest or interfere with any
  person doing anything that the person is authorized to do by or under
  this section or prevent or attempt to prevent any person from doing
  any such thing and, notwithstanding any other Act or law, every person
  shall, unless unable to do so, do everything required by or under this
  section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(22)]{.lawlabel} In this section,

  [[authorized person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[authorized person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person
      authorized in writing by the Commission for the purposes of this
      section; ([personne autorisée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[judge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[judge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a judge of a superior court
      having jurisdiction in the province where the matter arises or a
      judge of the Federal Court. ([juge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 126
- 1998, c. 19, s. 271
- 2009, c. 33, s. 14
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-126-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 126"){.PITLink}
:::

34, s. 64\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-127-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 127"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[128]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-128 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2005, c.
34, s. 64\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-128-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 128"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Privilege

**[[129]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-129 .sectionLabel}** When an employer,
claimant or other person gives the Commission written, oral or
documentary evidence required for the proper determination of the
entitlement of a claimant to benefits, the giving of the evidence is an
occasion of qualified privilege.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 129
- 2012, c. 19, s. 249
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-129-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 129"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Default

**[[130]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-130 .sectionLabel}** If, because of the
failure or neglect of any person to comply with this Act or the
regulations, another person loses the right to claim, in whole or in
part, any benefits to which the person would otherwise be entitled, the
Commission may nevertheless pay the benefits.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Question under section 90

- **[[131]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-131 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  question specified in section 90 arises in any legal proceedings, the
  justice, judge or court before whom it arises shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} refer the question to an authorized officer of the
    Canada Revenue Agency under that section and defer further
    proceedings until the officer's ruling is received, if the question
    has not been decided by the authorized officer; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on receipt of the ruling, proceed with the hearing
    and judgment of the legal proceedings.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deferral of judgment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an appeal has been made under section 91 or 103,
  the justice, judge or court shall defer judgment until

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a decision of the Minister of National Revenue is
    received, in the case of an appeal under section 91; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a decision of the Tax Court of Canada is received,
    in the case of an appeal under section 103.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 131
- 1999, c. 17, s. 135
- 2005, c. 38, s. 138
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-131-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 131"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Question for Commission

- **[[132]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  question that could be decided by the Commission arises in any legal
  proceedings, the justice, judge or court before whom the question
  arises shall refer the question to the Commission and defer further
  proceedings until the Commission's decision is received.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal pending

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an appeal from a decision of the Commission, or a
  person authorized by the Commission, is pending on the question
  arising in any legal proceedings, the justice, judge or court before
  whom the question arises shall defer further proceedings until the
  appeal decision is received.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Receipt of decision

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of the decision, the justice, judge or
  court shall proceed with the hearing and judgment and, in any
  proceedings under this Act, the decision is conclusive except in
  accordance with the [Federal Courts Act](/eng/acts/F-7).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 132
- 2002, c. 8, s. 182
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-132-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 132"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[133]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-133 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2015, c.
13, s. 54\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-133-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 133"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence of documents, etc.

- **[[134]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-134 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  any proceedings under this Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a document appearing to be a resolution, record or
    other proceeding of the Commission or other proceeding under this
    Act or a copy of it, and appearing to be certified by a Commissioner
    or the Secretary of the Commission,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a document appearing to be, or appearing to be a
    copy of or extract from,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a document in the custody of the Commission or a
      document issued under this Act, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an entry in any books or records in the custody
      of the Commission,

    and appearing to be certified by the Commission or a person employed
    in the administration of this Act,

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a document appearing to be certified by the
    Commission or a person employed in the administration of this Act
    and stating the amount of any contributions paid, payable or owing
    or the amount of any benefits or other amount paid to or owing by
    any person, or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a document appearing

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to be, or to be a copy of or extract from, any of
      the following, namely, an employer's register, books, pay sheets,
      records of wages, ledgers, accounts or other documents, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to be certified by an inspector or other person
      employed in the administration of this Act to whom the documents
      mentioned in subparagraph (i) were produced under this Act,

  is evidence of the facts appearing in the document without proof of
  the signature or official character of the person appearing to have
  signed the certificate and without further proof.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents sent by mail

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act and the regulations and
  any proceedings under them, a document appearing to be a certificate
  of the Commission or a person authorized by the Commission to the
  effect that a notice, request, demand or other document was sent by
  mail is evidence that the notice, request, demand or other document
  was received by the addressee in the ordinary course of the mails.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filmed or electronic evidence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In any proceedings under this Act or the regulations,
  a print that is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} made from a photographic film or from a document in
    electronic form made by the Commission for the purpose of keeping a
    permanent record of a document, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} certified by the Commission or a person employed in
    the administration of this Act

  is admissible in evidence for all purposes for which the recorded
  document would be admitted as evidence in the proceedings without
  proof of the signature or official character of the person appearing
  to have signed the certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents in electronic form

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a reference to a document in
  this section includes a document in electronic form.

### [Offences and Punishment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-217866 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence

- **[[135]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-135
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person is guilty of an offence
  punishable on summary conviction who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in relation to a claim for benefits, makes a
    representation that the person knows to be false or misleading;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} being required under this Act or the regulations to
    provide information, provides information or makes a representation
    the person knows to be false or misleading;

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} knowingly fails to declare to the Commission all
    or some of their earnings for a period determined under the
    regulations for which they claimed benefits;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} makes a claim or declaration that the person knows
    is false or misleading because of the non-disclosure of facts;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} being the payee of a special warrant, knowingly
    negotiates or attempts to negotiate it for benefits to which the
    person is not entitled;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} knowingly fails to return a special warrant, or the
    amount or any excess amounts, as required by section 44;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} imports or exports a document issued by the
    Commission, or has it imported or exported, for the purpose of
    defrauding or deceiving the Commission; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} participates in, assents to or acquiesces in an act
    or omission mentioned in paragraphs (a) to (f).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Saving

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No prosecution for an offence under this section
  shall be instituted if a penalty for that offence has been imposed
  under section 38, 39 or 65.1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Punishment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every person who commits an offence under this
  section is liable to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a fine of not less than \$200 and not more than
    \$5,000 plus,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in a case mentioned in paragraph (1)(b.1), an
      amount of not more than double the total of the amount by which
      the person's benefits were reduced under subsection 19(3) and the
      amount of the benefits that would have been paid to the claimant
      for the period mentioned in that paragraph if the benefits had not
      been reduced or the claimant had not been disentitled or
      disqualified from receiving benefits, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other appropriate case, an amount of not
      more than double the amount of any benefits that may have been
      paid as a result of committing the offence; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} both the fine and imprisonment for a term of not
    more than six months.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contravention of Act or regulations

- **[[136]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-136
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who contravenes this
  Act or the regulations is guilty of an offence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obstructing person authorized by
  Commission

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who delays or obstructs a person
  authorized by the Commission in exercising their powers or performing
  their duties under this Act or the regulations is guilty of an
  offence.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General penalty for offences

**[[137]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-137 .sectionLabel}** Every person who is
guilty of an offence under this Act for which no penalty is provided is
liable on summary conviction to a fine of not less than \$100 and not
more than \$2,000 or to imprisonment for a term of not more than six
months, or to both.

### [Social Insurance Number]{.HTitleText2} {#h-217893 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation

**[[138]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138 .sectionLabel}** Every person employed
in insurable employment, and every self-employed person in respect of
whom Part VII.1 applies, must have a Social Insurance Number that has
been assigned to that person under an Act of Parliament.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 138
- 2012, c. 19, s. 308
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-138-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 138"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change of name

**[[139]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-139 .sectionLabel}** When the name of a
person to whom a Social Insurance Number has been assigned changes
because of marriage or otherwise, the person shall inform the Commission
of their new name within 60 days after the day on which the change of
name becomes effective, unless they have already so informed another
authority empowered to receive that information.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 139
- 2012, c. 19, s. 308
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-139-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 139"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[140]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-140 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
sections 138 and 139, the Commission may, with the approval of the
Governor in Council, make regulations respecting

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} applications for a Social Insurance Number;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the assignment and use of Social Insurance Numbers;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} requirements that must be met by persons who have
  been assigned Social Insurance Numbers; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} requirements that must be met by employers.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 140
- 2012, c. 19, s. 308
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-140-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 140"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[141]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-141 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2012, c.
19, s. 308\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-141-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 141"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Reports]{.HTitleText2} {#h-217911 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reports

**[[142]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-142 .sectionLabel}** All reports,
recommendations and submissions required to be made to the Governor in
Council under this Act, whether by the Commission or otherwise, shall be
submitted through the Minister.

**[[143]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-143 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2012, c.
19, s. 250\]]{.Repealed}

## [PART VII]{.HLabel1}[Benefit Repayment]{.HTitleText1} {#h-217915 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[144]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[benefit repayment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[benefit repayment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an amount determined
    under section 145; ([remboursement de prestations]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means benefits under this Act, read
    without reference to this Part; ([prestations]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[income]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a person for a period means the
    amount that would be their income for the period determined under
    the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) if no amount were

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} deductible under paragraphs 60(v.1), (w), (y)
      and (z) of that Act,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} included in respect of a gain from a disposition
      of property to which section 79 of that Act applies, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} included under paragraph 56(1)(q.1) or subsection
      56(6) of that Act; ([revenu]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of National
    Revenue; ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the meaning given that term in
    subsection 248(1) of the [Income Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3); ([personne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[taxation year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[taxation year]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a taxation year within
    the meaning of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3). ([année
    d'imposition]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 144
- 2006, c. 4, s. 172
- 2007, c. 35, s. 128
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-144-20060701.html "Link to previous version of section 144"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefit repayment

- **[[145]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-145 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  claimant's income for a taxation year exceeds 1.25 times the maximum
  yearly insurable earnings, the claimant shall repay to the Receiver
  General 30% of the lesser of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the total benefits, other than special benefits and
    benefits under Part VII.1, paid to the claimant in the taxation
    year, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount by which the claimant's income for the
    taxation year exceeds 1.25 times the maximum yearly insurable
    earnings.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a
  claimant who was paid regular benefits for less than one week in the
  ten years before the taxation year referred to in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded benefits

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Regular benefits paid for weeks beginning before June
  30, 1996 shall not be taken into account when applying subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time for repayment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A repayment must be made

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a claimant who dies after October in
    the year and before May in the next year, within six months after
    the day of death; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, on or before April 30 in the
    next year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, repayments under this section
  do not affect the determination under subsection (2) of regular
  benefits paid to a claimant.

- [(6) to (8)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2001, c. 5, s. 11\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 145
- 1998, c. 19, s. 272
- 2001, c. 5, s. 11
- 2009, c. 33, s. 15
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-145-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 145"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Returns

**[[146]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-146 .sectionLabel}** If a claimant is
required to make a benefit repayment for a taxation year, a return in a
form, and containing information, authorized by the Minister shall,
without notice or demand, be filed with the Minister as part of the
claimant's return of income under Part I of the [Income Tax
Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3),

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a claimant who dies after October 31
  in the year and before May 1 in the next year, by the claimant's legal
  representative within six months after the day of death;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other claimant, on or before the
  claimant's filing-due date (as defined in subsection 248(1) of the
  [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3)) for the year, by that claimant or,
  if for any reason the claimant is unable to file the return, by their
  guardian, curator, tutor, committee or other legal representative; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the claimant or their legal representative has not
  filed the return, by such person as is required by notice in writing
  from the Minister to file the return, within such reasonable time as
  the notice specifies.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 146
- 1998, c. 19, s. 273
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Estimate of benefit repayment

**[[147]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-147 .sectionLabel}** Every claimant or other
person required by section 146 to file a return shall, in the return,
estimate the amount of benefit repayment payable by them.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsible Minister

**[[148]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-148 .sectionLabel}** The Minister shall
administer and enforce the provisions of this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of [Income Tax
Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) provisions

**[[149]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-149 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
this Part, subsections 150(2) and (3), section 152 (except subsections
152(1.1) to (1.3) and (6)), section 158, subsections 159(1) to (3),
sections 160 (except paragraph 160(1)(d)) and 160.1, subsections 161(1)
and (11), sections 162 to 167, Division J of Part I, sections 220 to
226, subsection 227(10), sections 229, 239, 243 and 244 and subsections
248(7) and (11) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) apply, with
such modifications as the circumstances require, except that, in the
application of those provisions,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} "Act" shall be read as "Part VII of the [Employment
  Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6)";

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} "person" and "taxpayer" shall be read as "claimant";

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} "tax" and "taxes" shall be read as "benefit
  repayment";

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} "under this Part" shall be read as "under Part VII of
  the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6)"; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 163(2)(a) of the [Income Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) shall be read as follows:

  - ["(a)]{.lawlabel} the benefit repayment payable by him for the year
    as determined under section 145 of the [Employment Insurance
    Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6);".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debts due Her Majesty

**[[150]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-150 .sectionLabel}** All benefit repayments,
interest, penalties and other amounts payable by a claimant under this
Part and under the provisions of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3)
as they apply for the purposes of this Part are debts due to Her Majesty
and recoverable in the Federal Court or any other court of competent
jurisdiction or in any other manner provided by the [Income Tax
Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Communication of information

- **[[151]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-151
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection 241(1) of
  the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), the Minister may communicate,
  or allow to be communicated, to the Commission, or a person authorized
  by the Commission, such information obtained under that Act as is
  necessary for the administration and enforcement of this Part and
  section 43 of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized person

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In relation to any information obtained under
  subsection (1), a person authorized by the Commission is an official
  or authorized person within the meaning of subsection 241(10) of the
  [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) and is subject to subsections
  239(2.2) and 241(1) and (2) of that Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[152]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may, with
the approval of the Governor in Council, make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that by this Part is to be
  prescribed; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} generally, to carry out the purposes and provisions
  of this Part.

## [PART VII.1]{.HLabel1}[Benefits For Self-Employed Persons]{.HTitleText1} {#h-217977 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-217978 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[152.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in
  this Part.

  [[balance-due day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[balance-due day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a self-employed person
      for a year means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the person died after October in the year
        and before May in the immediately following year, the day that
        is 6 months after the day of death, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, April 30 in the immediately
        following year. ([date d'exigibilité du solde]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a profession, calling,
      trade, manufacture or undertaking of any kind whatever, and
      includes an adventure or concern in the nature of trade but does
      not include an office or
      employment. ([entreprise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[disentitled]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[disentitled]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means not entitled under
      sections 49, 50, 152.03, 152.15 or 152.2 or under the
      regulations. ([inadmissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[family member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[family member]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, 2017, c. 20, s.
      242\]]{.Repealed}

  [[initial claim for benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[initial claim for benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a claim
      made for the purpose of establishing a self-employed person's
      benefit period. ([demande initiale de
      prestations]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[qualifying period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[qualifying period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the period
      described in section 152.08. ([période de
      référence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[self-employed person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[self-employed person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual
      who

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is or was engaged in a business; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is employed but does not have insurable
        employment by reason of paragraph 5(2)(b).

      However, individuals to whom regulations made under Part VIII
      apply, and individuals whose employment is included in insurable
      employment by a regulation made under paragraph 5(4)(c), are not
      included in this definition. ([travailleur
      indépendant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[waiting period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[waiting period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the one week of the
      benefit period described in section 152.15. ([délai de
      carence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of self-employed earnings for a
  year

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of this Part, the amount of the
  self-employed earnings of a self-employed person for a year is,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a self-employed person who is an
    individual referred to in paragraph (a) of the definition
    [[self-employed person]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection (1),
    the amount that is the aggregate of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an amount equal to

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} their income for the year, computed under the
        [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), from their businesses, other
        than a business more than fifty per cent of the gross revenue of
        which consisted of rent from land or buildings,

      minus

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} all losses, computed under the [Income Tax
        Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), sustained by the self-employed person in
        the year in carrying on the businesses they are engaged in, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} their income for the year from employment
      described in paragraph 5(6)(c) that has been excluded from
      insurable employment by a regulation made under subsection 5(6),
      as that income is computed under the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a self-employed person who is an
    individual referred to in paragraph (b) of the definition
    [[self-employed person]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection (1),
    the amount that would have been the person's insurable earnings for
    the year had the person's employment not been excluded from
    insurable employment; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a self-employed person who is an
    individual referred to in both paragraphs (a) and (b), the amount
    that is the aggregate of the amounts referred to in both those
    paragraphs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Indians

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of clause (2)(a)(i)(A), the income of
  an Indian, as defined in subsection 2(1) of the [Indian
  Act](/eng/acts/I-5), on a reserve, as defined in that subsection, is
  to be calculated without reference to paragraph 81(1)(a) of the
  [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Week of unemployment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of this Part, a week of unemployment
  for a self-employed person is a week of unemployment as defined or
  determined in accordance with the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rounding off percentages or fractions

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A reference in this Part to an amount equal to a
  percentage or fraction of earnings or benefits in a period shall be
  rounded to the nearest multiple of one dollar or, if the amount is
  equidistant from two multiples of one dollar, to the higher multiple.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
- 2016, c. 7, s. 219
- 2017, c. 20, s. 242
:::
