### [Qualifying for Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218232 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualification requirements

- **[[152.07]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.07
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A self-employed person qualifies for
  benefits if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least 12 months have expired since the day on
    which the person entered into an agreement referred to in subsection
    152.02(1) with the Commission, or if a period has been prescribed
    for the purpose of this section, a period that is at least as long
    as that prescribed period has expired since that day;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the agreement has not been terminated or deemed to
    have been terminated;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person has had an interruption of earnings from
    self-employment; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the person has had during their qualifying period
    an amount of self-employed earnings that is equal to or greater than
    the following amount:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the person has not accumulated a violation in
      the 260 weeks before making their initial claim for benefits,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the person's benefit period
        begins during the period beginning on January 3, 2021 and ending
        on September 25, 2021, and despite any amount fixed or
        determined in accordance with the regulations for that
        qualifying period, \$5,000,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the person's benefit period
        begins during the period beginning on September 26, 2021 and
        ending on September 24, 2022, and despite any amount fixed or
        determined in accordance with the regulations for that
        qualifying period, \$5,289, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, \$6,000 or the amount fixed
        or determined in accordance with the regulations, if any, for
        that qualifying period, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the person has accumulated a violation in the
      260 weeks before making their initial claim for benefits, the
      amount referred to in this paragraph that would otherwise apply in
      respect of that self-employed person but for this subparagraph
      multiplied by,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} if the violation is a minor violation, 1.25 or
        the prescribed multiplier, if one has been prescribed,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} if the violation is a serious violation, 1.5 or
        the prescribed multiplier, if one has been prescribed,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} if the violation is a very serious violation,
        1.75 or the prescribed multiplier, if one has been prescribed,
        or

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} if the violation is a subsequent violation, 2
        or the prescribed multiplier, if one has been prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Violations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A self-employed person accumulates a violation if in
  any of the following circumstances the Commission issues a notice of
  violation to the person:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} one or more penalties are imposed on the person
    under section 38, or under section 41.1 as a result of acts or
    omissions mentioned in section 38;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is found guilty of one or more offences
    under section 135 or 136 as a result of acts or omissions mentioned
    in those sections; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person is found guilty of one or more offences
    under the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46) as a result of acts or
    omissions relating to the application of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Value of violations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The value of a violation is the total of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the overpayment of benefits under
    this Part resulting from the acts or omissions on which the
    violation is based, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the self-employed person is disentitled from
    receiving benefits under this Part, or the act or omission on which
    the violation is based relates to qualification requirements under
    subsection (1), the amount determined, subject to subsection (4), by
    multiplying the self-employed person's weekly rate of benefits by
    the average number of weeks of benefits under this Part, as
    determined under the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The maximum amount to be determined under paragraph
  (3)(b) is the amount of benefits that could have been paid to the
  self-employed person if they had not been disentitled or had met the
  qualification requirements under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Classification of violations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Except for violations for which a warning was
  imposed, each violation is classified as a minor, serious, very
  serious or subsequent violation as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the value of the violation is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} less than \$1,000, it is a minor violation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} \$1,000 or more, but less than \$5,000, it is a
      serious violation, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} \$5,000 or more, it is a very serious
      violation; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the notice of violation is issued within 260
    weeks after the person accumulates another violation, it is a
    subsequent violation, even if the acts or omissions on which it is
    based occurred before the person accumulated the other violation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed violation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A violation accumulated by an individual under
  section 7.1 is deemed to be a violation accumulated by the individual
  under this section on the day on which the notice of violation was
  given to the individual.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A violation may not be taken into account under
  paragraph (1)(d) in more than two initial claims by an individual for
  benefits under this Act if the individual who accumulated the
  violation qualified for benefits in each of those two initial claims,
  taking into account subparagraph (1)(d)(ii), subsection 7.1(1) or
  regulations made under Part VIII, as the case may be.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
- 2016, c. 7, s. 220
- [2021, c. 3, s. 2](#1288045-1288558){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1288045-1288558.html"}
- [2021, c. 23, s. 321](#1307979-1327335){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307979-1327335.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-152.07-20210317.html "Link to previous version of section 152.07"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying period

- **[[152.08]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.08
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The qualifying period of a
  self-employed person is the year immediately before the year during
  which their benefit period begins.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Earnings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A self-employed person's self-employed earnings
  during a qualifying period may not be taken into account in respect of
  more than one initial claim for benefits.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefits under this Part and Part I

- **[[152.09]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.09
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If an individual qualifies for
  benefits under this Part as a self-employed person and for benefits
  under Part I as an insured person, the individual may receive benefits
  under one Part only and, to do so, the individual must, in the
  prescribed manner, at the time of making an initial claim for
  benefits, elect under which Part benefits are to be paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of election

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The election is binding on the individual in respect
  of the initial claim for all benefits payable, for any of the
  following reasons, during the benefit period established in relation
  to the initial claim:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} pregnancy;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} caring for one or more new-born children of the
    self-employed person, or one or more children placed with the
    self-employed person for the purpose of adoption;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed illness, injury or quarantine;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} providing care or support to one or more family
    members;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} providing care or support to one or more critically
    ill children; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} providing care or support to one or more critically
    ill adults.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
- 2014, c. 20, s. 249
- 2017, c. 20, s. 249
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-152.09-20140619.html "Link to previous version of section 152.09"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Benefit Period]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218290 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Establishment

- **[[152.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When a self-employed person who
  qualifies under section 152.07 makes an initial claim for benefits, a
  benefit period shall be established and, once it is established,
  benefits are payable to the person in accordance with this Part for
  each week of unemployment that falls in the benefit period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for establishment of benefit
  period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No benefit period is to be established for a
  self-employed person unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person makes an initial claim for benefits in
    accordance with section 50 and the regulations and proves that the
    person is qualified to receive benefits; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person supplies information in the form and
    manner directed by the Commission, giving the person's employment
    circumstances and the circumstances pertaining to any interruption
    of earnings, and any other information that the Commission may
    require.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Beginning of benefit period

- **[[152.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A benefit period begins on the later
  of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Sunday of the week in which the interruption of
    earnings occurs,

  and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Sunday of the week in which the initial claim
    for benefits is made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Length of benefit period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The length of a benefit period is 52 weeks, except as
  otherwise provided in subsections (11) to (19).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prior benefit period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any change or cancellation of a benefit
  period under this section, a benefit period shall not be established
  for a self-employed person if a prior benefit period has not ended.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Late initial claim

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An initial claim for benefits made after the day on
  which the self-employed person first qualified to make the claim shall
  be regarded as having been made on an earlier day if the self-employed
  person shows that they qualified to receive benefits on the earlier
  day and that there was good cause for the delay throughout the period
  that begins on the earlier day and ends on the day on which the
  initial claim was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other late claims

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A claim for benefits, other than an initial claim for
  benefits, made after the time prescribed for making the claim shall be
  regarded as having been made on an earlier day if the self-employed
  person shows that there was good cause for the delay throughout the
  period that begins on the earlier day and ends on the day on which
  their claim was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Late claims --- clause
  152.07(1)(d)(i)(A)

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections (4) and (5), the
  fact that, but for clause 152.07(1)(d)(i)(A), the claimant would not
  have qualified to receive benefits is good cause for a delay in making
  a claim for those benefits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A claim for benefits referred to in section 152.06
  with respect to a family member shall not be regarded as having been
  made on an earlier day under subsection (4) or (5) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the time the claim is made, all benefits that
    may otherwise have been payable in relation to that claim have
    already been exhausted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the beginning of the period referred to in
    subsection 152.06(3) has already been determined with respect to
    that family member and the claim would have the effect of moving the
    beginning of that period to an earlier date; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claim is made in any other circumstances set
    out in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(6.1)]{.lawlabel} A claim for benefits referred to in section 152.061
  with respect to a critically ill child must not be regarded as having
  been made on an earlier day under subsection (4) or (5) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the time the claim is made, all benefits that
    may otherwise have been payable in relation to that claim have
    already been exhausted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the beginning of the period referred to in
    subsection 152.061(3) has already been determined with respect to
    that child and the claim would have the effect of moving the
    beginning of that period to an earlier date; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claim is made in any other circumstances set
    out in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(6.2)]{.lawlabel} A claim for benefits referred to in section 152.062
  with respect to a critically ill adult must not be regarded as having
  been made on an earlier day under subsection (4) or (5) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the time the claim is made, all benefits that
    may otherwise have been payable in relation to that claim have
    already been exhausted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the beginning of the period referred to in
    subsection 152.062(3) has already been determined with respect to
    that adult and the claim would have the effect of moving the
    beginning of that period to an earlier date; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claim is made in any other circumstances set
    out in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancelling benefit period

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Once a benefit period has been established for a
  self-employed person, the Commission may

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cancel the benefit period if it has ended and no
    benefits were paid or payable during the period; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} whether or not the period has ended, cancel at the
    request of the self-employed person that portion of the benefit
    period immediately before the first week for which benefits were
    paid or payable, if the self-employed person

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} establishes under this Part --- or establishes
      under Part 1, as an insured person --- a new benefit period
      beginning the first week for which benefits were paid or payable,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} shows that there was good cause for the delay in
      making the request throughout the period that begins on the day on
      which benefits were first paid or payable and ends on the day on
      which the request for cancellation was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of cancellation

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A cancelled benefit period or portion of a benefit
  period is deemed never to have begun.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}End of benefit period

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A benefit period ends when any of the following first
  occurs:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} no further benefits are payable to the
    self-employed person in their benefit period, including for the
    reason that benefits have been paid for the maximum number of weeks
    for which benefits may be paid under section 152.14;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the benefit period would otherwise end under this
    section; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the self-employed person

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} requests that their benefit period end,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} makes a new initial claim for benefits, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} qualifies to receive benefits under this Part
      or qualifies, as an insured person, to receive benefits under Part
      I.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Late requests

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} Whether or not the benefit period has ended, a
  request under paragraph (9)(c) shall be regarded as having been made
  on an earlier day if the self-employed person shows that there was
  good cause for the delay throughout the period that begins on the
  earlier day and ends on the day on which the request was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of benefit period

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} A self-employed person's benefit period is extended
  by the aggregate of any weeks during the benefit period for which the
  self-employed person proves, in the manner that the Commission may
  direct, that they were not entitled to benefits because they were

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} confined in a jail, penitentiary or other similar
    institution and were not found guilty of the offence for which they
    were being held or any other offence arising out of the same
    transaction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in receipt of earnings paid because of the complete
    severance of their relationship with their former employer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in receipt of workers' compensation payments for an
    illness or injury; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in receipt of payments under a provincial law on
    the basis of having ceased to work because continuing to work would
    have resulted in danger to the self-employed person, her unborn
    child or a child whom she was breast-feeding.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Further extension of benefit period

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} A self-employed person's benefit period is extended
  by the aggregate of any weeks during an extension of a benefit period
  under subsection (11) for which the self-employed person proves, in
  the manner that the Commission may direct, that they were not entitled
  to benefits because of a reason specified in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of benefit period --- child
  in hospital

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} If the child or children referred to in subsection
  152.05(1) are hospitalized during the period referred to in subsection
  152.05(2), the benefit period is extended by the number of weeks
  during which the child or children are hospitalized.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of benefit period

  [(14)]{.lawlabel} If, during a self-employed person's benefit period,
  benefits were paid to the person for more than one of the reasons
  mentioned in paragraphs 152.14(1)(a) to (f), at least one of those
  benefits was paid for fewer than the applicable maximum number of
  weeks established for those reasons and the maximum total number of
  weeks established for those reasons is greater than 50, the benefit
  period is extended so that those benefits may be paid up to that
  maximum total number of weeks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of benefit period --- reason
  mentioned in paragraph 152.14(1)(b)

  [(14.1)]{.lawlabel} If, during a self-employed person's benefit
  period, benefits were not paid for any reason mentioned in paragraph
  152.14(1)(a), (c), (d), (e) or (f), and benefits were paid to the
  person for the reason mentioned in paragraph 152.14(1)(b) in the case
  where the applicable maximum number of weeks is established under
  subparagraph 152.14(1)(b)(ii), the benefit period is extended by 26
  weeks so that benefits may be paid up to that maximum number of weeks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum extension under
  subsections (11) to (14.1)

  [(15)]{.lawlabel} An extension under one or more of subsections (11)
  to (14.1) must not result in a benefit period of more than 104 weeks.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum extension under subsection (14)

  [(16)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (15), unless the benefit
  period is also extended under any of subsections (11) to (13), an
  extension under subsection (14) must not result in a benefit period of
  more than the sum of two weeks and the total of the maximum number of
  weeks established under subsection 152.14(1) for each of the benefits
  paid to the self-employed person for one of the reasons mentioned in
  paragraphs 152.14(1)(a) to (f) during the person's benefit period
  before it was extended under subsection (14).

- [(17) to (19)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2012, c. 27, s.
  24\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
- 2012, c. 27, s. 24
- 2013, c. 35, s. 3
- 2017, c. 20, s. 250
- [2021, c. 3, s. 3](#1288049-1288562){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1288049-1288562.html"}
- [2021, c. 23, s. 322](#1307984-1327337){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307984-1327337.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-152.11-20210926.html "Link to previous version of section 152.11"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

**[[152.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.12 .sectionLabel}** On receiving an
initial claim for benefits, the Commission shall decide whether the
self-employed person is qualified to receive benefits and notify the
person of its decision.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

### [Payment of Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218372 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefits

**[[152.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.13 .sectionLabel}** If a benefit
period has been established for a self-employed person, benefits may be
paid to the person for each week of unemployment that falls in the
benefit period, subject to the maximums established by section 152.14.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum number of weeks

- **[[152.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.14
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The maximum number of weeks for
  which benefits under this Part may be paid in a benefit period to a
  self-employed person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} because of pregnancy is 15;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} because the self-employed person is caring for one
    or more new-born children of the self-employed person, or one or
    more children placed with the self-employed person for the purpose
    of adoption is, in accordance with the election under section
    152.05,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 35, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} 61;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} because of a prescribed illness, injury or
    quarantine is 26;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} because the person is providing care or support to
    one or more family members described in subsection 152.06(1) is 26;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} because the self-employed person is providing care
    or support to one or more critically ill children described in
    subsection 152.061(1), is 35; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} because the self-employed person is providing care
    or support to one or more critically ill adults described in
    subsection 152.062(1) is 15.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum --- single pregnancy or
  placement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The maximum number of weeks for which benefits under
  this Part may be paid

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a single pregnancy is 15; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the care of one or more new-born or adopted
    children as a result of a single pregnancy or placement is,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the maximum number of weeks that has been
      elected under subsection 152.05(1.1) is established under
      subparagraph (1)(b)(i), 35 or, if the weeks for which benefits may
      be paid are divided in accordance with section 152.05, 40, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the maximum number of weeks that has been
      elected under subsection 152.05(1.1) is established under
      subparagraph (1)(b)(ii), 61 or, if the weeks for which benefits
      may be paid are divided in accordance with section 152.05, 69.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adoption

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, the placement with a
  self-employed person, at the same or substantially the same time, of
  two or more children for the purpose of adoption is to be regarded as
  a single placement of a child or children for the purpose of adoption.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum --- parental benefits

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a claim is made under this Part in respect of a
  child or children referred to in paragraph (2)(b) and a claim is made
  under section 23 in respect of the same child or children, the maximum
  number of weeks of benefits payable under this Act in respect of the
  child or children is,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the maximum number of weeks that has been
    elected under subsection 152.05(1.1) is established under
    subparagraph (1)(b)(i), 35 or, if the weeks for which benefits may
    be paid are divided in accordance with section 152.05, 40; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the maximum number of weeks that has been
    elected under subsection 152.05(1.1) is established under
    subparagraph (1)(b)(ii), 61 or, if the weeks for which benefits may
    be paid are divided in accordance with section 152.05, 69.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum --- compassionate care benefits

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Even if more than one claim is made under this Act,
  at least one of which is made under section 152.06 --- or even if more
  than one certificate is issued for the purposes of this Act, at least
  one of which is issued for the purposes of section 152.06 --- for the
  same reason and in respect of the same family member, the maximum
  number of weeks of benefits payable under this Act in respect of that
  family member is 26 weeks during the period of 52 weeks that begins on
  the first day of the week referred to in paragraph 152.06(3)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum --- critically ill child

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} Even if more than one claim is made under this Act,
  at least one of which is made under section 152.061 --- or even if
  more than one certificate is issued for the purposes of this Act, at
  least one of which is issued for the purposes of section 152.061 ---
  for the same reason and in respect of the same critically ill child,
  the maximum number of weeks of benefits payable under this Act in
  respect of that child is 35 weeks during the period of 52 weeks that
  begins on the first day of the week referred to in paragraph
  152.061(3)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum --- critically ill adult

  [(5.2)]{.lawlabel} Even if more than one claim is made under this Act,
  at least one of which is made under section 152.062 --- or even if
  more than one certificate is issued for the purposes of this Act, at
  least one of which is issued for the purposes of section 152.062 ---
  for the same reason and in respect of the same critically ill adult,
  the maximum number of weeks of benefits payable under this Act in
  respect of that adult is 15 weeks during the period of 52 weeks that
  begins on the first day of the week referred to in paragraph
  152.062(3)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shorter period

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If a shorter period is prescribed for the purposes of
  subsection 152.06(4), then that shorter period applies for the
  purposes of subsection (5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Expiry of shorter period

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} When a shorter period referred to in subsection (6)
  has expired in respect of a family member, no further benefits are
  payable under section 152.06 in respect of that family member until
  the minimum prescribed number of weeks has elapsed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Combined weeks of benefits

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} In a self-employed person's benefit period, the
  self-employed person may, subject to the applicable maximums, combine
  weeks of benefits to which they are entitled because of a reason
  mentioned in subsection (1), but the maximum number of combined weeks
  is 50. However, if the benefit period is extended under subsection
  152.11(14), the maximum number of combined weeks equals the maximum
  number of weeks in the benefit period calculated under subsection
  152.11(16) less two weeks.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
- 2012, c. 27, s. 25
- 2015, c. 36, s. 78
- 2017, c. 20, s. 251
- [2018, c. 27, s. 306](#1039491-1119428){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1039491-1119428.html"}
- [2021, c. 23, s. 323](#1307988-1386904){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307988-1386904.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-152.14-20190317.html "Link to previous version of section 152.14"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiting period

**[[152.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.15 .sectionLabel}** A self-employed
person is not entitled to be paid benefits in a benefit period until,
after the beginning of the benefit period, the person has served a
waiting period of one week of unemployment for which benefits would
otherwise be payable.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
- 2016, c. 7, s. 221
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-152.15-20100101.html "Link to previous version of section 152.15"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presumption

- **[[152.151]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.151
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of determining a
  self-employed person's waiting period, a week of unemployment is
  deemed to be a week of unemployment for which benefits would otherwise
  be payable if --- were the week not the waiting period --- the amount
  of the benefits that would have been payable in that week after
  deducting the following would be greater than zero:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the earnings, if any, that is greater
    than \$50, if the person's rate of weekly benefits is less than
    \$200; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the earnings, if any, that is greater
    than 25% of the person's rate of weekly benefits, if that rate is
    \$200 or more.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}References

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (1),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in determining the amount of the benefits that
    would have been payable, the deduction referred to in subsection
    152.18(2) is to be excluded; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a reference to earnings means earnings as
    determined for the purpose of subsection 152.18(2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 12, s. 290
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rate of weekly benefits

- **[[152.16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.16
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The rate of weekly benefits payable
  to a self-employed person is 55% --- or 33% for the weeks for which
  the self-employed person is paid benefits under section 152.05 in the
  case where the applicable maximum number of weeks is established in
  subparagraph 152.14(1)(b)(ii) --- of the result obtained by dividing
  the aggregate of the amounts referred to in the following
  paragraphs (a) and (b) by 52:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount of their self-employed earnings,
    determined under paragraph 152.01(2)(a), (b) or (c), as the case may
    be, for their qualifying period; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they had insurable earnings from employment,
    including insurable earnings earned as a person to whom regulations
    made under Part VIII apply, for their qualifying period, the amount
    of those insurable earnings for that period, calculated without
    taking into account prescribed insurable earnings.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excess not to be included

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Only the portion of the aggregate of the amounts
  referred to in paragraphs (1)(a) and (b) that does not exceed the
  maximum yearly insurable earnings as calculated under section 4 is to
  be taken into account for the purposes of subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
- 2017, c. 20, s. 252
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-152.16-20100101.html "Link to previous version of section 152.16"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rate increase --- family supplement

- **[[152.17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.17
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The rate of weekly benefits of a
  self-employed person who has one or more dependent children shall be
  increased by the amount of a family supplement determined in
  accordance with the regulations if the person establishes, in the
  manner that the Commission may direct, that the person meets the
  prescribed low-income family eligibility criteria.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criteria

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The criteria for low-income family eligibility may
  include criteria that are the same as or similar to the criteria for
  receiving a Canada child benefit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Canada child benefit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), a Canada child
  benefit is a deemed overpayment under subdivision a.1 of Division E of
  Part I of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum increase

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The amount of the increase in the self-employed
  person's rate of weekly benefits shall not exceed the prescribed
  percentage of their amount of self-employed earnings for the year
  divided by 52 or, if no percentage is prescribed, 25%.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum rate of weekly benefits

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The maximum rate of weekly benefits that may be paid
  under this section is 55% of the maximum yearly insurable earnings, as
  calculated under section 4, divided by 52.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
- 2016, c. 12, s. 86
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-152.17-20100101.html "Link to previous version of section 152.17"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Earnings in waiting period

- **[[152.18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.18
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a self-employed person has
  earnings during their waiting period, an amount not exceeding those
  earnings shall, as prescribed, be deducted from the benefits payable
  for the first three weeks for which benefits would otherwise be
  payable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Earnings in periods of unemployment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3), 152.03(3) and 152.04(4),
  if the self-employed person has earnings during any other week of
  unemployment, there shall be deducted from benefits payable in that
  week the amount equal to the total of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 50% of the earnings that are less than or equal to
    90% of the result obtained by dividing the aggregate of the amounts
    referred to in paragraphs 152.16(1)(a) and (b) by 52, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 100% of the earnings that are greater than 90% of
    the result obtained by dividing the aggregate of the amounts
    referred to in paragraphs 152.16(1)(a) and (b) by 52.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excess not to be included

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Only the portion of the aggregate of the amounts
  referred to in paragraphs 152.16(1)(a) and (b) that does not exceed
  the maximum yearly insurable earnings as calculated under section 4 is
  to be taken into account for the purpose of subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undeclared earnings

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the self-employed person has failed to declare all
  or some of their earnings to the Commission for a period, determined
  under the regulations, for which benefits were claimed,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the following amount shall be deducted from the
    benefits paid to the person for that period:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the undeclared earnings, if, in the
      opinion of the Commission, the person knowingly failed to declare
      the earnings, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the amount of the undeclared
      earnings less the difference between

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} all amounts determined under paragraph (2)(a)
        or (b) for the period,

      and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} all amounts that were applied under those
        paragraphs in respect of the declared earnings for the period;
        and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the deduction shall be made

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} from the benefits paid for a number of weeks that
      begins with the first week for which the earnings were not
      declared in that period, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in such a manner that the amount deducted in
      each consecutive week equals the person's benefits paid for that
      week.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
- 2018, c. 12, s. 291
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-152.18-20100101.html "Link to previous version of section 152.18"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deduction for excluded days in waiting
period

- **[[152.19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.19
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a self-employed person is not
  entitled to receive benefits for a working day in their waiting
  period, an amount equal to 1/5 of their weekly rate of benefits for
  each such working day shall be deducted from the benefits payable for
  the three weeks described in subsection 152.18(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deduction for excluded days not in
  waiting period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a self-employed person is disentitled from
  receiving benefits for a working day in a week of unemployment that is
  not in their waiting period, an amount equal to 1/5 of their weekly
  rate of benefits for each such working day shall be deducted from the
  benefits payable for that week.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prison inmates and persons outside Canada

**[[152.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.2 .sectionLabel}** Except as may
otherwise be prescribed, a self-employed person is not entitled to
receive benefits for any period during which the person

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is an inmate of a prison or similar institution; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not in Canada.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

### [Premium]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218482 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Premium

- **[[152.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.21
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every self-employed person who has
  entered into an agreement referred to in subsection 152.02(1) that has
  not been terminated or that is not deemed to have been terminated
  shall, in respect of every year, pay a self-employment premium equal
  to the amount determined under subsection (2) multiplied by the
  premium rate set under section 66 or 66.32, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount for the purpose of subsection (1) is the
  lesser of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the person's self-employed earnings
    for the year,

  and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the maximum yearly insurable earnings for the year,
    as calculated under section 4, minus the person's insurable
    earnings, if any.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a premium is required to be
  paid

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the year during which the
    self-employed person entered into the agreement, regardless of the
    date on which it was entered into; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the year during which the agreement
    is terminated or deemed to be terminated, regardless of the date on
    which it was terminated or deemed to be terminated.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
- 2013, c. 40, s. 136
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-152.21-20100101.html "Link to previous version of section 152.21"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Self-employed Earnings and Collection of Premiums]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218497 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return to be filed

- **[[152.22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a self-employed person is
  required to pay a premium for a year in respect of their self-employed
  earnings, a return of the person's self-employed earnings for the year
  shall, without notice or demand for it, be filed with the Minister of
  National Revenue in the form and manner, and containing the
  information, specified by that Minister, by that person (or, if the
  person is unable for any reason to file the return, by their
  representative) on or before the day on which the person's return of
  income under Part I of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) is
  required by that Part to be filed or would be required by that Part to
  be filed if tax under that Part were payable for the year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Demand for return

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Whether or not they are liable to pay a premium for a
  year in respect of their earnings from self-employment and whether or
  not a return has been filed under subsection (1), every self-employed
  person shall, on demand from the Minister of National Revenue, served
  personally or by registered letter, file with that Minister, in the
  form and containing information specified by that Minister, within any
  reasonable time that may be specified in the demand, a return of their
  earnings from self-employment for the year designated in the demand.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return by trustee, etc.

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every trustee in bankruptcy, assignee, liquidator,
  curator, receiver, receiver-manager trustee or committee and every
  agent, mandatary or other person administering, managing, winding-up,
  controlling or otherwise dealing with the property, business, estate
  or succession, or income of a self-employed person who has not filed a
  return of the person's self-employed earnings for a year as required
  by this section must file with the Minister of National Revenue a
  return, in the form specified by that Minister, of the person's
  self-employed earnings for the year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Identification of province of residence

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The information to be contained in any such return
  must identify the province in which the self-employed person was
  resident on the last day of that year.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Estimate to be made

**[[152.23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.23 .sectionLabel}** Every
self-employed person required by section 152.22 to file a return of
their self-employed earnings must, in the return, estimate the amount of
the premium to be paid in respect of those earnings.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examination of return and notice of
assessment

**[[152.24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.24 .sectionLabel}** The Minister of
National Revenue must, with all due dispatch, examine each return of
self-employed earnings and assess the premium to be paid for the year in
respect of those earnings and the interest and penalties, if any,
payable, and, after the examination, send a notice of assessment to the
person who filed the return.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of premium

- **[[152.25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.25
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A self-employed person shall, on or
  before their balance-due day for the year, pay to the Receiver General
  the whole amount of the premium if they are not required by section
  155 or 156 of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) to pay instalments
  for that year in respect of their income tax.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Farmers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every self-employed person to whom section 155 of the
  [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) applies, other than a person to whom
  subsection (1) applies, shall pay to the Receiver General on or before
  December 31 in each year, two thirds of the following amounts:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the premium required to be paid by the person for
    the year in respect of the person's self-employed earnings, as
    estimated by the person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the premium required in respect of the person's
    self-employed earnings for the preceding year.

  In addition, the person shall pay to the Receiver General, on or
  before the person's balance-due day for the year, the remainder of the
  premium as estimated under section 152.23. However, paragraphs (a)
  and (b) do not require the payment of any amount in respect of the
  person that would otherwise become due after the death of the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other persons

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every self-employed person, other than a person to
  whom subsection (1) or (2) applies, shall pay to the Receiver General
  in respect of each year

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on or before March 15, June 15, September 15 and
    December 15 in the year, an amount equal to one quarter of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the premium required to be paid by the person for
      the year in respect of the person's self-employed earnings, as
      estimated by the person, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the premium required in respect of the person's
      self-employed earnings for the preceding year; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on or before

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} March 15 and June 15 in the year, an amount equal
      to one quarter of the premium required in respect of the person's
      self-employed earnings for the second preceding year, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} September 15 and December 15 in the year, an
      amount equal to one half of the amount, if any, by which

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the premium required in respect of the person's
        self-employed earnings for the preceding year

      exceeds

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} one half of the premium required in respect of
        the person's self-employed earnings for the second preceding
        year.

  In addition, the person shall pay to the Receiver General, on or
  before the person's balance-due day for the year, the remainder of the
  premium as estimated under section 152.23. However, paragraphs (a)
  and (b) do not require the payment of any amount in respect of the
  person that would otherwise become due after the death of the person.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-152.25-20100101.html "Link to previous version of section 152.25"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest on unpaid premium

- **[[152.26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.26
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the amount paid by a
  self-employed person on or before the person's balance-due day for a
  year on account of the premium required to be paid by the person for
  the year in respect of the person's self-employed earnings is less
  than the amount of the premium so required to be paid, the
  self-employed person shall pay to the Receiver General interest, at a
  rate per year prescribed by regulation made under subsection (5), on
  the difference between those amounts from the balance-due day for the
  year to the day of payment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interest on instalments

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to any interest payable under subsection
  (1), if a self-employed person, being required by section 152.25 to
  pay a part or instalment of a premium, has failed to pay all or any
  part of a premium as required, the person shall, on payment of the
  amount the person failed so to pay, pay to the Receiver General
  interest on the amount, at a rate per year prescribed by regulation
  made under subsection (5), from the day on or before which the person
  was required to make the payment to the day of payment or the
  beginning of the period in respect of which the person is liable to
  pay interest on the amount under subsection (1), whichever is earlier.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Farmers

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), if a
  self-employed person is required by subsection 152.25(2) to pay a part
  or instalment of a premium in respect of their self-employed earnings,
  the person is deemed to have been liable to pay on or before the day
  referred to in that subsection a part or instalment computed by
  reference to whichever of the following that gives rise to the least
  amount required to be paid by the person on or before that day:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the premium required to be paid by them for the
    year in respect of their self-employed earnings;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the premium required in respect of their
    self-employed earnings for the preceding year; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the amount stated to be the amount of the
    instalment payable by them for the year in the notice, if any, sent
    to them by the Minister of National Revenue.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other persons

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), if a
  self-employed person is required by subsection 152.25(3) to pay a part
  or instalment of a premium in respect of their self-employed earnings,
  the person is deemed to have been liable to pay on or before each day
  referred to in that subsection a part or instalment computed by
  reference to whichever of the following that gives rise to the least
  total amount of those parts or instalments required to be paid by them
  by that day:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the premium required to be paid by them for the
    year in respect of their self-employed earnings;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amounts determined under paragraph 152.25(3)(b)
    in respect of them for the year;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the amounts stated to be the amounts of instalment
    payable by them for the year in the notices, if any, sent to them by
    the Minister of National Revenue; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the premium required in respect of their
    self-employed earnings for the preceding year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of National Revenue may, with the
  approval of the Governor in Council, make regulations, prescribing a
  rate for the purposes of each of subsections (1) and (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to file a return

- **[[152.27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.27
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every self-employed person who fails
  to file a return of their self-employed earnings for a year as and
  when required by section 152.22 is liable to a penalty of five per
  cent of the part of the amount of the premium required to be paid by
  them for the year that remained unpaid at the expiry of the time the
  return was required to be filed, except that, if that person is liable
  to a penalty under subsection 162(1) or (2) of the [Income Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) in respect of the year, the Minister of National
  Revenue may reduce the penalty to which the person is liable under
  this section or may remit the penalty in whole or in part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person who fails to file a return as required
  by subsection 152.22(3) is liable to a penalty of \$5 for each day of
  default, to a maximum of \$50.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of [Income Tax
Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3)

- **[[152.28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.28
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part and except as
  otherwise provided by regulation made under subsection (2), the
  provisions of Divisions I and J of Part I of the [Income Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) with respect to payment of tax, assessments,
  objections to assessments, appeals, interest, penalties and excess
  refunds, and the provisions of Part XV of that Act (except
  section 221) and subsections 248(7) and (11) of that Act apply, with
  any modifications that the circumstances require, in relation to any
  amount paid or payable as or on account of the premium for a year in
  respect of self-employed earnings as though that amount were an amount
  paid or payable as or on account of tax under that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister of National Revenue may, with the
  approval of the Governor in Council, make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} exempting any provision of Divisions I and J of
    Part I of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) from the application
    of subsection (1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} specifying that any provisions of either of those
    Divisions apply with the modifications specified in the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Priority in which payment to be applied

**[[152.29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.29 .sectionLabel}** If any payment is
made by a person to the Minister of National Revenue on account of taxes
specified in section 228 of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) and of
a premium under this Part in respect of self-employed earnings, despite
any direction made by the person making the payment with respect to its
application, the part of the payment that would be applied under that
section in payment of tax under the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3)
is, subject to section 37 of the [Canada Pension Plan](/eng/acts/C-8),
to be applied in payment of a premium under this Part and is deemed to
be a payment on account of the premium, and to the extent of the amount
so applied, does not discharge liability for tax under the [Income Tax
Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), and any amount then remaining is to be applied in
payment of tax under the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) and
discharges the liability of the person making the payment for that tax
to the extent of that amount.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of excess premium in respect of
self-employed earnings

- **[[152.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a self-employed person has paid,
  on account of the premium they are required to pay for a year in
  respect of their self-employed earnings, an amount in excess of the
  premium, the Minister of National Revenue

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} may refund that part of the amount so paid in
    excess of the premium on sending the notice of assessment of the
    premium, without any application having been made for the refund;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall make the refund after sending the notice of
    assessment, if an application for the refund is made in writing by
    the self-employed person not later than three years after the end of
    the year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 96(11) to (13) apply in respect of
  refunds under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
- 2010, c. 25, s. 73
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-152.3-20100101.html "Link to previous version of section 152.3"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Application of other Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218588 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of other provisions

- **[[152.31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.31
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3),
  all the provisions of this Act, except sections 5 to 37, 48 and 56 to
  65.2, that are not inconsistent with the provisions of this Part
  apply, with any modifications that the circumstances require, to this
  Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of certain provisions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 86(1) applies as though the reference to
    "an employer" in that subsection were a reference to "a
    self-employed person"; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsection 88(5) applies as though the reference to
    "this Part" were a reference to "Part VII.1".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 102

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Section 102 applies, with any modifications that the
  circumstances require, in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} offences under paragraphs 106(4)(a), (d) and (e)
    committed in relation to any provision of this Part or regulations
    made under sections 152.26 and 152.28;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} offences under section 152.32; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any matter referred to in any of sections 152.21 to
    152.3 or that relates to the application of any of those sections.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 125

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Section 125 applies in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} offences under subsections 135(1) and 136(2)
    committed by self-employed persons and offences committed by them in
    relation to the contravention of any provision of regulations made
    under this Part, except regulations made under sections 152.26 and
    152.28; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any matter referred to in this Part or that relates
    to the application of this Part, other than a matter referred to in
    any of sections 152.21 to 152.3 or in regulations made under
    sections 152.26 and 152.28.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

### [Delegation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218607 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation

**[[152.311]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.311 .sectionLabel}** The Minister of
National Revenue may authorize an officer or a class of officers to
exercise powers or perform duties of that Minister under this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

### [Offences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218612 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence and punishment

**[[152.32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.32 .sectionLabel}** Every person who
contravenes section 152.22 is guilty of an offence and, in addition to
any penalty otherwise provided, is liable on summary conviction to a
fine of not less than \$25 a day for each day the offence continues, to
a maximum of \$1,000.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218617 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[152.33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.33 .sectionLabel}** The Commission
may, with the approval of the Governor in Council, make regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} defining or determining what is a week of
  unemployment for a self-employed person;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} setting out the circumstances under which agreements
  referred to in subsection 152.02(4) are deemed to be terminated;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} fixing, for any year, an amount for the purposes of
  subparagraph 152.07(1)(d)(i), or establishing a manner for determining
  that amount, which amount may not be less than \$6,000;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} for determining the average number of weeks of
  benefits for the purposes of paragraph 152.07(3)(b);

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} specifying, for the purpose of subsection 152.03(4),
  the circumstances when self-employed persons are deemed to be not
  working; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} prescribing anything that by this Part is to be
  prescribed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

### [Review of this Part]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218628 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review of this Part

**[[152.34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152.34 .sectionLabel}** Five years after
the day on which this Part comes into force, the Minister must cause a
review of this Part and its administration and operation to be
conducted.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2009, c. 33, s. 16
:::

## [PART VIII]{.HLabel1}[Self-employed Persons Engaged in Fishing]{.HTitleText1} {#h-218633 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[153]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding anything in this
  Act, the Commission may, with the approval of the Governor in Council,
  make such regulations as it deems necessary respecting the
  establishment and operation of a scheme of employment insurance for
  self-employed persons engaged in fishing, including regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} including as a self-employed person engaged in
    fishing any person engaged in an activity or occupation related to
    or incidental to fishing; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} including as an employer of a self-employed person
    engaged in fishing any person with whom the self-employed person
    enters into a contractual or other commercial relationship in
    respect of their occupation as a self-employed person engaged in
    fishing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Scheme may be different

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The scheme established by the regulations may, with
  respect to any matter, be different from the provisions of this Act
  relating to that matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Tabling of regulation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister shall table the regulations in the House
  of Commons within three sitting days after the day on which they are
  made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Motion to repeal

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The regulations come into force on the 10th sitting
  day after the day on which they are tabled, or on any later day
  specified in the regulations, unless a motion to repeal them, signed
  by not fewer than 30 members of the House of Commons, is filed with
  the Speaker of the House of Commons before the 10th sitting day.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consideration

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a motion to repeal the regulations is filed with
  the Speaker of the House of Commons in accordance with subsection (4),
  it shall be taken up and considered by the House of Commons within
  five sitting days after the day on which it is filed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time for disposition of motion

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The motion shall be taken up after the ordinary hour
  of daily adjournment, for a period of not more than four hours, and at
  the end of the debate the Speaker of the House of Commons shall,
  without delay or further debate or amendment, put every question
  necessary for the disposition of the motion.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adoption or defeat of motion

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If the motion is adopted, the regulations are
  repealed, and if the motion is defeated, the regulations come into
  force on the day after the day on which the motion is defeated or on
  any later day specified in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulation repealed

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The regulations are repealed if Parliament is
  prorogued or dissolved before the motion is disposed of or, if no
  motion has been filed, before the end of the period mentioned in
  subsection (4).

- Definition of [[sitting day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of this section, [[sitting
  day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a day on which the House of Commons is
  sitting.

## [PART VIII.1]{.HLabel1}[Alternate Access to Special Benefits]{.HTitleText1} {#h-218655 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[153.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in this Act, the
  Commission shall, with the approval of the Governor in Council, make
  any regulations it considers necessary respecting the establishment
  and operation of a scheme to ensure that special benefits are provided
  to insured persons who have at least 600 hours of insurable employment
  in their qualifying period but who do not qualify to receive benefits
  under section 7, including regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for establishing requirements to qualify to receive
    the benefits, the duration of entitlement to the benefits, benefit
    rates, disentitlement and disqualification from receiving the
    benefits and benefit repayment requirements; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} varying the application of any other provision of
    this Act in relation to persons who have made claims under this Part
    and who subsequently make claims under Part I or VIII.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Scheme may be different

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The scheme established by the regulations may, with
  respect to any matter, be different from the provisions of this Act
  relating to that matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The scheme established by the regulations may not
  provide special benefits to persons who are subject to an increase
  under section 7.1 in the number of hours of insurable employment
  required to qualify for benefits.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 153.1
- 2000, c. 14, s. 6
:::

## [PART VIII.2]{.HLabel1}[Regulations --- Provincial Plans]{.HTitleText1} {#h-218667 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[153.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite any other provisions of this
  Act, if the Government of Canada has entered into an agreement with a
  province in respect of a provincial law that has the effect of
  reducing or eliminating special benefits, or benefits under Part
  VII.1, payable as described in subsection 69(2), the Commission may,
  with the approval of the Governor in Council, for the purposes of
  implementing the agreement and taking into account the application or
  effect of the provincial law, make any regulations that it considers
  necessary, including regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} respecting the manner in which and the extent to
    which any provision of this Act or the regulations applies; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} adapting any provision of this Act or the
    regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The regulations may provide for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the making of any financial adjustments and for the
    crediting or charging of the amount of any of those adjustments to
    the Employment Insurance Operating Account, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} refunds of overpayments with respect to
      employees' premiums and premiums under Part VII.1,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} adjustment payments between the prov­ince and the
      Government of Canada with respect to premiums, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} reimbursement by the province of benefits paid
      by the Government of Canada in accordance with any administrative
      agreement between the province and the Government of Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the exchange of any information obtained under the
    provincial law or this Act; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the administration of benefits payable under this
    Act to persons employed or residing in the province or who have made
    a claim under the provincial law --- or to self-employed persons,
    within the meaning of subsection 152.01(1), who work or reside in
    the province or who have made a claim under the provincial law ---
    and the increase or decrease in the amount of benefits payable and
    in the number of weeks for which benefits may be paid under this Act
    to and in respect of those persons.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2005, c. 30, s. 131
- 2009, c. 33, s. 17
- 2010, c. 12, s. 2189
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-153.2-20100101.html "Link to previous version of section 153.2"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART VIII.3]{.HLabel1}[Interim Orders]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1241668 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}COVID-19

- **[[153.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Minister may, for the purpose of
  mitigating the economic effects of the coronavirus disease 2019
  (COVID-19), make interim orders that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} add provisions, including provisions that provide
    for new benefits, to this Act or any regulation made under this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} adapt provisions of this Act or any regulation made
    under this Act; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} provide that any provisions of this Act or a
    regulation made under this Act do not apply in whole or in part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An interim order is not to be made under
  subsection (1) in respect of Part IV or VII or in respect of a
  regulation made under this Act for the purposes of those Parts.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Condition precedent --- Minister of
  Finance

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An interim order is not to be made under
  subsection (1) without the consent of the Minister of Finance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Condition precedent --- President of
  the Treasury Board

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An interim order is not to be made under
  subsection (1) in respect of Part III or in respect of a regulation
  made under this Act for the purposes of that Part without the
  additional consent of the President of the Treasury Board.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation with Commission

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the Minister may consult the
  Commission before making an interim order under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retroactive effect

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} An interim order made under subsection (1) may, if it
  so provides, have retroactive effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cessation of effect

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} An interim order made under subsection (1) must
  contain provisions that cause any provision added under paragraph
  (1)(a), any adaptation made under paragraph (1)(b) and any
  non-application provision made under paragraph (1)(c) to cease to
  apply on the earliest of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day, if any, specified in the interim order,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the interim order is repealed, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Saturday after the first anniversary of the day
    on which the interim order takes effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conflict

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If an interim order made under subsection (1) or a
  provision added under paragraph (1)(a) provides that it applies
  despite any provision of this Act or any regulation made under this
  Act, the interim order or added provision prevails to the extent that
  it conflicts with this Act or any regulation made under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} The power to make an interim order under
  subsection (1) is not to be exercised after September 30, 2020.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2020, c. 5, s. 57](#1241011-1241695){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1241011-1241695.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accessory orders

**[[153.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.4 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may
make orders for the purpose of ensuring that interim orders made under
subsection 153.3(1) only have temporary effect, including orders that
repeal any provision added under paragraph 153.3(1)(a).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2020, c. 5, s. 57](#1241011-1241699){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1241011-1241699.html"}
:::

## [PART VIII.4]{.HLabel1}[Employment Insurance Emergency Response Benefit]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1242424 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1242425 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[153.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this Part,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [[COVID-19]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    coronavirus disease 2019;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2020-188, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} [[initial claim for
    benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} has the same meaning as in
    subsection 6(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of Claimant

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part,
  [[claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} who ceases working, whether employed or
    self-employed, for reasons related to COVID-19;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} who could have had a benefit period established on
    or after March 15, 2020 with respect to any of the benefits referred
    to in paragraph (3)(a);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} who is unable to start working for reasons related
    to COVID-19 and to whom, at any time during the period beginning on
    December 29, 2019 and ending on October 3, 2020, at least one
    benefit referred to in paragraph (3)(b) has been paid or was
    payable, if, during that period

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the established benefit period of that person
      with respect to those benefits has ended,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} all of those benefits have been paid to that
      person, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} some of those benefits cannot be paid to that
      person by virtue of subsection 12(6); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} who is unable to start working for reasons related
    to COVID-19 and to whom, at any time during the period beginning on
    December 29, 2019 and ending on October 3, 2020, at least one
    benefit referred to in paragraph (3)(c) has been paid or was
    payable, if, during that period

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the established benefit period of that person
      with respect to those benefits has ended,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} all of those benefits have been paid to that
      person, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} some of those benefits cannot be paid to that
      person by virtue of subsection 8(18) of the [Employment Insurance
      (Fishing) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-445).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefits referred to in paragraphs
  (2)(b) to (d)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The benefits referred to in paragraphs (2)(b) to (d)
  are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to a claimant referred to in paragraph
    (2)(b), benefits provided under section 152.03 or provided under
    Part I, other than those benefits provided under any of sections 22
    to 24;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to a claimant referred to in paragraph
    (2)(c), benefits provided under Part I, other than those benefits
    provided under any of sections 21 to 24; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} with respect to a claimant referred to in paragraph
    (2)(d), benefits provided under the employment insurance scheme
    established under Part VIII.

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1242452 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of other provisions

- **[[153.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a claim made for the
  employment insurance emergency response benefit, the following
  provisions apply in respect of the employment insurance emergency
  response benefit, subject to the adaptations set out in subsection (2)
  and in sections 153.1301 to 153.13091:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsections 42(1) and (2) and sections 43, 44, 47,
    50 and 52;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the provisions of Part II;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the provisions of Part III and IV;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the provisions of Part VI, except subsections
    112(3) and 114(2) and sections 115 and 140; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} section 27 and subsections 92(1) to (5) and (8) of
    the [Employment Insurance Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adaptation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A provision referred to in paragraph (1)(a), (d)
  or (e) is adapted

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to refer, in respect of claimants and the benefits
    to which it refers, to the claimant within the meaning of this Part
    and the employment insurance emergency response benefit provided
    under this Part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} so that any reference, in the English version, to
    "entitled" and other parts of speech and grammatical forms of the
    same word means "eligible" under section 153.9; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} so that any reference to the regulations made under
    the authority of this Act is to be read without that reference.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of other provisions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No other provision of this Act or of any regulations
  made under it applies in respect of a claim for the employment
  insurance emergency response benefit unless a contrary intention
  appears. However, for greater certainty, the definitions set out in
  subsection 2(1) apply in this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
