### [Employment Insurance Emergency Response Benefit]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1242461 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

- **[[153.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An employment insurance emergency
  response benefit is payable to a claimant who makes a claim under
  section 153.8 and who is eligible for the benefit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payments in advance

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may pay the employment insurance
  emergency response benefit in advance of the customary time for paying
  it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other benefits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A claimant who receives, under a provincial Act or
  any other provincial mechanism, a sum of money or financial aid of any
  type and in any form, for reasons related to COVID-19, remains
  eligible to receive the employment insurance emergency response
  benefit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Financial aid under section 63

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A claimant who receives financial aid as a result of
  entering into an agreement referred to in section 63 remains eligible
  to receive the employment insurance emergency response benefit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-61, s. 1](#1241427-1242521){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1241427-1242521.html"}
- [SOR/2020-169, s. 3](#1257732-1257869){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1257732-1257869.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-153.7-20200401.html "Link to previous version of section 153.7"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Claim

- **[[153.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any claimant may, in the form and
  manner established by the Minister, make a claim for the employment
  insurance emergency response benefit for any two-week period starting
  on a Sunday and falling within the period beginning on March 15, 2020
  and ending on October 3, 2020.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- week of September 27,
  2020

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), an employment insurance
  emergency response benefit is not payable to a claimant for the week
  beginning September 27, 2020 unless an employment insurance emergency
  response benefit was paid or payable to the claimant for any week
  beginning before that date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A claim must not be made after December 2, 2020.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A claimant must provide the Minister with any
  information that the Minister may require in respect of the claim.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information --- Employer

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An employer must provide the Minister with any
  information that the Minister may require in respect of the claim.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No benefit period --- March 15 to
  September 26, 2020

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the period beginning on March 15, 2020 and ending
  on September 26, 2020, no benefit period is to be established with
  respect to any of the benefits referred to in paragraph 153.5(3)(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No benefit period --- September 27 to
  October 3, 2020

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the period beginning on September 27, 2020 and
  ending on October 3, 2020, no benefit period is to be established with
  respect to any of the benefits referred to in paragraph 153.5(3)(a) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an employment insurance emergency response benefit
    was paid or payable to the claimant for any week beginning before
    September 27, 2020; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant has not claimed the employment
    insurance emergency response benefit for the maximum number of weeks
    referred to in section 153.11.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} On receiving a claim for the employment insurance
  emergency response benefit, the Commission shall decide whether the
  claimant is eligible for the benefit and whether the benefit is
  payable to the claimant for the two-week period in respect of which
  they claimed the benefit, and shall notify the claimant of its
  decisions in any manner that it considers adequate.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-61, s. 1](#1241427-1242530){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1241427-1242530.html"}
- [SOR/2020-169, s. 4](#1257737-1257874){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1257737-1257874.html"}
- [SOR/2020-188, s. 3](#1259893-1259955){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1259893-1259955.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-153.8-20200722.html "Link to previous version of section 153.8"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligibility

- **[[153.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.9
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A claimant is eligible for the
  employment insurance emergency response benefit

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if they

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} reside in Canada,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} are at least 15 years of age,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} have insurable earnings of at least \$5,000 in
      2019 or in the 52 weeks preceding the day on which they make the
      claim under section 153.8,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} whether employed or self-employed, cease working
      for at least seven consecutive days within the two-week period in
      respect of which they claimed the benefit, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} have no income from employment or self-employment
      in respect of the consecutive days on which they cease working;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they are a claimant referred to in paragraph
    153.5(2)(b) and they have no income from employment or
    self-employment for at least seven consecutive days within the
    two-week period in respect of which they claimed the benefit; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if they are a claimant referred to in paragraph
    153.5(2)(c) or (d) and they

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} reside in Canada,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} are at least 15 years of age,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} have insurable earnings of at least \$5,000 in
      2019 or within the 52 weeks preceding the day on which they make
      the claim under section 153.8, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} have no income from employment or
      self-employment for at least seven consecutive days within the
      two-week period in respect of which they claimed the benefit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ineligibility

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A claimant is not eligible if,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} they receive, under this Act, a benefit other than
    the employment insurance emergency response benefit;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} they receive allowances, money or other benefits
    paid under a provincial plan

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} because of pregnancy, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} because the claimant is caring for one or more
      of their new-born children, or one or more children placed with
      them for the purpose of adoption;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} they receive an income support payment under the
    [Canada Emergency Response Benefit Act](/eng/acts/C-3.7); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} they receive a Canada emergency student benefit
    under the [Canada Emergency Student Benefit Act](/eng/acts/C-3.75).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsequent ineligibility

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} A claimant is not eligible if a benefit period
  established for the claimant --- including a benefit period
  established in respect of benefits payable under Part VIII --- begins
  after the claimant received an employment insurance emergency response
  benefit or an income support payment under the [Canada Emergency
  Response Benefit Act](/eng/acts/C-3.7).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusion

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A claimant does not cease working for the purpose of
  subsection (1) if they leave their employment voluntarily.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- employment,
  self-employment and income

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant receives income, whether from
  employment or self-employment, the total of which does not exceed
  \$1,000 over a period of four weeks that succeed each other in
  chronological order but not necessarily consecutively and in respect
  of which the employment insurance emergency response benefit is paid,
  the claimant is deemed to meet the requirements of subparagraphs
  (1)(a)(iv) and (v), of paragraph (1)(b) or of subparagraph (1)(c)(iv),
  as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Receipt of income support payment

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If, for any week, a claimant received an employment
  insurance emergency response benefit for which they were not eligible
  by reason only of paragraph (2)(c), the claimant, despite that
  paragraph, is deemed to have been eligible for the benefit unless the
  Commission has, under subsection 15(2) of the [Canada Emergency
  Response Benefit Act](/eng/acts/C-3.7), informed the
  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 2 of that
  Act, that subsection 15(1) of that Act should not apply in respect of
  the claimant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Receipt of Canada emergency student
  benefit

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If, for any week, a claimant received an employment
  insurance emergency response benefit for which they were not eligible
  by reason only of paragraph (2)(d), the claimant, despite that
  paragraph, is deemed to have been eligible for the benefit unless the
  Commission has, under subsection 15.1(2) of the [Canada Emergency
  Student Benefit Act](/eng/acts/C-3.75), informed the
  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 2 of that
  Act, that subsection 15.1(1) of that Act should not apply in respect
  of the claimant.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of payment

- **[[153.10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.10
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The amount of the employment
  insurance emergency response benefit for a week is \$500.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provincial assistance --- COVID-19

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, if a claimant receives, under
  a provincial Act or any other provincial mechanism, a sum of money or
  financial aid of any type and in any form, for reasons related to
  COVID-19, the weekly amount of the employment insurance emergency
  response benefit will not be reduced or eliminated.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rate increase --- family supplement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Section 16 and subsections 152.17(1) to (4) apply,
  with the necessary modifications, to the amount of the employment
  insurance emergency response benefit.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-61, s. 1](#1241427-1242558){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1241427-1242558.html"}
- [SOR/2020-88, s. 4](#1244031-1244362){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1244031-1244362.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-153.10-20200401.html "Link to previous version of section 153.10"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum number of weeks

**[[153.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.11 .sectionLabel}** The maximum
number of weeks for which the employment insurance emergency response
benefit may be paid to a claimant is 28 minus the number of weeks, if
any, for which the claimant receives an income support payment under the
[Canada Emergency Response Benefit Act](/eng/acts/C-3.7) or a Canada
emergency student benefit under the [Canada Emergency Student Benefit
Act](/eng/acts/C-3.75).

::: HistoricalNote

### [Employment Insurance Operating Account]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1261609 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefit enhancements under this Act ---
Emergency Response Benefits

**[[153.111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.111 .sectionLabel}** There shall be
credited to the Employment Insurance Operating Account an amount
determined by the Minister of Finance that corresponds to the total cost
of the employment insurance emergency response benefit under this Act,
including all costs related to the benefit and its administration.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-208, s. 1](#1261013-1261617){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1261013-1261617.html"}
:::

### [Social Insurance Number]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1242563 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Social insurance number

**[[153.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.12 .sectionLabel}** The Minister is
authorized to collect and use, for the purposes of the administration
and enforcement of this Part, the Social Insurance Number of a claimant
who makes a claim under this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-61, s. 1](#1241427-1242567){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1241427-1242567.html"}
:::

### [Benefit Period]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1242568 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}End of benefit period

- **[[153.121]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.121
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A benefit period that was
  established for a claimant before March 15, 2020 --- including a
  benefit period established in respect of benefits payable under Part
  VIII --- ends the day before the first week for which the claimant is
  paid an employment insurance emergency response benefit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reconsideration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if, following a
  reconsideration carried out under section 112, a decision that results
  in a benefit period being established that begins before March 15,
  2020 is rendered after the claimant has been paid an employment
  insurance emergency response benefit, the benefit period does not end
  and the claimant is deemed to have not been eligible for the
  employment insurance emergency response benefit by reason of paragraph
  153.9(2)(a).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-173, s. 3](#1258154-1258208){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1258154-1258208.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No reduction

**[[153.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.13 .sectionLabel}** When a benefit
period is being established for a claimant who has received an
employment insurance emergency response benefit, in establishing the
benefit period, the weeks during which the benefit was paid is not to be
taken into account.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-61, s. 1](#1241427-1242572){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1241427-1242572.html"}
:::

### [Adaptations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1257875 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adaptation of English version of section
44

**[[153.1301]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.1301 .sectionLabel}** For the
purposes of applying paragraph 153.6(1)(a), section 44 of the English
version is adapted as follows:

> ::: ReadAsText
> [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability to return overpayment
>
> **44** A person who has received or obtained an employment insurance
> emergency response benefit payment for which the person is not
> eligible, or an employment insurance emergency response benefit
> payment in excess of the amount for which the person is eligible,
> shall without delay return the amount, the excess amount or the
> special warrant for payment of the amount, as the case may be.
> :::

- [SOR/2020-169, s. 5](#1257742-1257882){.wb-lbx

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adaptation of section 50

**[[153.1302]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.1302 .sectionLabel}** For the
purposes of applying paragraph 153.6(1)(a), section 50 is adapted as
follows:

> ::: ReadAsText
> [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligibility --- employment insurance
> emergency response benefit
>
> - **50** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A claimant who does not provide the Minister
>   with the required information in respect of a claim for the
>   employment insurance emergency response benefit or fails to comply
>   with the requirement under subsection (2) is not eligible for the
>   employment insurance emergency response benefit until they provide
>   the information or comply with the requirement.
>
> - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Making claim or providing information
>   in person
>
>   [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may require a claimant or group or
>   class of claimants to be at a suitable place at a suitable time in
>   order to make a claim for the employment insurance emergency
>   response benefit in person or provide additional information about
>   that claim.
> :::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adaptation of subsection 52(2)

- **[[153.1303]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.1303
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of applying
  paragraph 153.6(1)(a), subsection 52(2) is adapted as follows:

  > ::: ReadAsText
  > - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Decision
  >
  >   [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Commission decides that a person has
  >   received money by way of the employment insurance emergency
  >   response benefit for which the person was not eligible, or has not
  >   received money for which the person was eligible, the Commission
  >   must calculate the amount of the money and notify the claimant of

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adaptation of subsection 52(4)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of applying paragraph 153.6(1)(a),

  > ::: ReadAsText
  > - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount payable

  >   emergency response benefit, and the money was not paid, the amount
  >   calculated is payable to the claimant.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adaptation of subsection 112(1)

- **[[153.1305]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.1305
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of applying
  paragraph 153.6(1)(d), subsection 112(1) is adapted as follows:

  > ::: ReadAsText
  > [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reconsideration --- Commission
  >
  > - **112** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A claimant or other person who is the
  >   subject of a decision of the Commission, or the employer of the
  >   claimant, may make a request to the Commission in accordance with
  >   subsection (4) for a reconsideration of that decision at any time
  >   within
  >
  >   - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 30 days after the day on which a decision is
  >     communicated to them; or
  >
  >   - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any further time that the Commission may allow.
  > :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Adaptation of section 112

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of applying paragraph 153.6(1)(d),
  section 112 is adapted by adding the following after subsection (3):

  > ::: ReadAsText
  > - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reconsideration of a decision
  >
  >   [(4)]{.lawlabel} A request for reconsideration of the decision
  >   must be in writing and contain the following information:
  >
  >   - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person making the request and
  >     their
  >
  >     - [(i)]{.lawlabel} social insurance number or the business
  >       number assigned to them by the Minister of National Revenue,
  >       as the case may be,
  >
  >     - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} address and telephone number, and
  >
  >     - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} facsimile number and email address, if any;
  >
  >   - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the decision was communicated
  >     to the person;
  >
  >   - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the reasons why the person is requesting a
  >     reconsideration of the decision; and
  >
  >   - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any relevant information that was not
  >     previously provided to the Commission.
  >
  > - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing of request for
  >   reconsideration
  >
  >   [(5)]{.lawlabel} A request for reconsideration must be filed with
  >   the Commission at the address, facsimile number or email address
  >   --- or in accordance with the electronic filing procedure ---
  >   provided by the Commission on the website of the Department of
  >   Employment and Social Development.
  > :::

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-169, s. 5](#1257742-1257939){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1257742-1257939.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Write-off of amounts wrongly paid

- **[[153.1306]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.1306
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An amount payable under section 43
  may be written off by the Commission if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount does not exceed \$100;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the debtor is deceased;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the debtor is a discharged bankrupt;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the debtor is an undischarged bankrupt in respect
    of whom the final dividend has been paid and the trustee has been
    discharged;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the overpayment does not arise from an error made
    by the debtor or as a result of a false or misleading declaration or
    representation made by the debtor, whether the debtor knew it to be
    false or misleading or not, but arises from

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a retrospective decision or ruling made under
      Part IV, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} provision by the Commission of a weekly
      employment insurance emergency response benefit amount in excess
      of the amount referred to in subsection 153.10(1); or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the Commission considers that, having regard to all
    the circumstances,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount is uncollectable,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the repayment of the amount would result in
      undue hardship to the debtor, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the administrative costs of collecting the
      amount would likely equal or exceed the amount to be collected.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The portion of an amount owing under section 47 in
  respect of the employment insurance emergency response benefit
  received before the Commission notifies the debtor of the overpayment
  may be written off by the Commission if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the overpayment does not arise from an error made
    by the debtor or as a result of a false or misleading declaration or
    representation made by the debtor, whether the debtor knew it to be
    false or misleading or not; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the overpayment arises as a result of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a delay or error made by the Commission in
      processing a claim for the employment insurance emergency response
      benefit,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} retrospective control procedures or a
      retrospective review initiated by the Commission,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an error made on the record of employment by
      the employer,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} an incorrect calculation by the employer of the
      debtor's insurable earnings, or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} an error in insuring the employment or other
      activity of the debtor.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-169, s. 5](#1257742-1257963){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1257742-1257963.html"}
:::

### [Special Rules]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1257992 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefits referred to in paragraph
153.5(3)(a)

**[[153.1310]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.1310 .sectionLabel}** The following
rules apply to a person who made an initial claim for benefits in
respect of any of the benefits referred to in paragraph 153.5(3)(a) and
who either had, before the coming into force retroactively of this Part,
a benefit period established on or after March 15, 2020 with respect to
the benefits for which the claim was made, or who would have had, but
for the coming into force of this Part on March 15, 2020, a benefit
period established on or after that same date with respect to the
benefits for which the claim was made:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is deemed to have made a claim for the
  employment insurance emergency response benefit under section 153.8;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the rules set out in Part VIII.4 apply to a claim for
  the employment insurance emergency response benefit, with the
  exception of the rule requiring that a claim be made in accordance
  with subsection 153.8(1); and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the benefits referred to in paragraph 153.5(3)(a)
  received on or after March 15, 2020 are deemed to be the employment
  insurance emergency response benefit and, as the case may be,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person is to be paid, for each week for which
    they receive the benefits referred to in paragraph 153.5(3)(a), an
    amount of employment insurance emergency response benefit that is
    equal to the difference between the amount set out in subsection
    153.10(1) and the amount of benefits that they received for the week
    in question, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} each amount of benefits received for a week that
    exceeds the amount set out in subsection 153.10(1) is deemed to be
    equal to the amount set out in that subsection and section 44 does
    not apply to any amount of benefits received for a week that exceeds
    the amount set out in subsection 153.10(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-169, s. 5](#1257742-1258001){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1257742-1258001.html"}
:::

### [Cessation of Effect]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1252700 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}June 2, 2027 or repeal

**[[153.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.14 .sectionLabel}** A provision of
this Part that is added by any of the following Interim Orders or a
provision that, under any of those Interim Orders, is an adaptation of a
provision of this Act or provides for the non-application of a provision
of this Act ceases to apply on the earlier of June 2, 2027 and the day
on which the Interim Order that enacted the provision is repealed:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Interim Order Amending the Employment Insurance
  Act (Employment Insurance Emergency Response Benefit);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} Interim Order No. 2 Amending the Employment Insurance
  Act (Employment Insurance Emergency Response Benefit);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} Interim Order No. 3 Amending the Employment Insurance
  Act (Employment Insurance Emergency Response Benefit);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} Interim Order No. 4 Amending the Employment Insurance
  Act (Employment Insurance Emergency Response Benefit);

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} Interim Order No. 5 Amending the Employment Insurance
  Act (Employment Insurance Emergency Response Benefit);

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} Interim Order No. 6 Amending the Employment Insurance
  Act (Employment Insurance Emergency Response Benefit);

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} Interim Order No. 7 Amending the Employment Insurance
  Act (Employment Insurance Emergency Response Benefit);

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} Interim Order No. 9 Amending the Employment Insurance
  Act (Employment Insurance Emergency Response Benefit); and

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} Interim Order No. 10 Amending the Employment
  Insurance Act (Employment Insurance Emergency Response Benefit).

## [PART VIII.5]{.HLabel1}[Temporary Measures to Facilitate Access to Benefits]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1258220 .Part}

### [Definitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1258221 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[153.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.15 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[initial claim for benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[initial claim for benefits]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
    meaning as in subsection 6(1). ([demande initiale de
    prestations]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[qualifying period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[qualifying period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of benefits
    under Part I, has the same meaning as in subsection 6(1). ([période
    de référence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[waiting period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[waiting period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of benefits under Part I or VIII, the
      same meaning as in subsection 6(1); and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of benefits under Part VII.1, the same
      meaning as in subsection 152.01(1). ([délai de
      carence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

### [Rate of Unemployment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1258228 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rate of 13.1%

**[[153.16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.16 .sectionLabel}** Despite section
17 of the [Employment Insurance
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332), if the later of the weeks
referred to in subsection 10(1) begins during the period beginning on
September 27, 2020 and ending on September 25, 2021, the regional rate
of unemployment that applies to the claimant is 13.1%, if that rate is
greater than the rate that would otherwise apply to them.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-173, s. 6](#1258163-1258232){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1258163-1258232.html"}

  ajax-after="1259815-1261587.html"}

  ajax-after="1307993-1316790.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-153.16-20200927.html "Link to previous version of section 153.16"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Availability]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1261618 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Course, program of instruction or
non-referred training

- **[[153.161]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.161
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of applying
  paragraph 18(1)(a), a claimant who attends a course, program of
  instruction or training to which the claimant is not referred under
  paragraphs 25(1)(a) or (b) is not entitled to be paid benefits for any
  working day in a benefit period for which the claimant is unable to
  prove that on that day they were capable of and available for work.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may, at any point after benefits are
  paid to a claimant, verify that the claimant referred to in
  subsection (1) is entitled to those benefits by requiring proof that
  they were capable of and available for work on any working day of
  their benefit period.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-208, s. 3](#1261017-1261625){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1261017-1261625.html"}
:::

### [Increase in Hours of Insurable Employment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1258233 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefits under Part I

- **[[153.17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.17
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A claimant who makes an initial
  claim for benefits under Part I on or after September 27, 2020 or in
  relation to an interruption of earnings that occurs on or after that
  date is deemed to have in their qualifying period

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the initial claim is in respect of benefits
    referred to in any of sections 21 to 23.3, an additional 480 hours
    of insurable employment; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, an additional 300 hours of
    insurable employment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a claimant who has
  already had the number of insurable hours in their qualifying period
  increased under that subsection or under this section as it read on
  September 26, 2020, if a benefit period was established in relation to
  that qualifying period.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-173, s. 6](#1258163-1258242){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1258163-1258242.html"}
- [SOR/2020-187, s. 1](#1259815-1261588){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1259815-1261588.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-153.17-20200810.html "Link to previous version of section 153.17"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefits

**[[153.171]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.171 .sectionLabel}** A claimant who
qualifies to receive benefits under section 7 and who received the
additional 300 hours of insurable employment under paragraph
153.17(1)(b) may make a claim for benefits under sections 21 to 23.3,
even if they are not a [[major attachment
claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 6(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-208, s. 4](#1261019-1261629){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1261019-1261629.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fishing

**[[153.172]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.172 .sectionLabel}** A claimant for
whom, before the coming into force of section 153.17, a benefit period
could have been established under section 8 of the [Employment Insurance
(Fishing) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-445) may make a request
under section 112 in order to receive benefits under those Regulations
rather than under section 7.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-208, s. 4](#1261019-1261633){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1261019-1261633.html"}
:::

### [Extension of Qualifying Period]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1258243 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recipients of emergency response benefit

- **[[153.18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.18
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A qualifying period mentioned in
  paragraph 8(1)(a) is extended by 28 weeks if the claimant

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} makes the initial claim for benefits on or after
    September 27, 2020 or in relation to an interruption of earnings
    that occurs on or after that date; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} received, during the period mentioned in that
    paragraph, an employment insurance emergency response benefit
    referred to in subsection 153.7(1) or an income support payment
    under the [Canada Emergency Response Benefit Act](/eng/acts/C-3.7).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a claimant who has
  already had a qualifying period extended under that subsection or
  under this section as it read on August 29, 2020 or September 26,
  2020.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-173, s. 6](#1258163-1258249){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1258163-1258249.html"}
- [SOR/2020-187, s. 1](#1259815-1261546){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1259815-1261546.html"}
- [SOR/2020-187, s. 2](#1259817-1259983){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1259817-1259983.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-153.18-20200830.html "Link to previous version of section 153.18"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Benefit Period]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1258250 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Late claims

**[[153.19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.19 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes
of subsections 10(4) and (5), the fact that, but for sections 153.17 and
153.18 as they read on August 29, 2020, the claimant would not have
qualified to receive benefits referred to in any of sections 22 to 24 is
good cause for a delay in making a claim for those benefits.

::: HistoricalNote

### [Waiting Period]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1258269 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiver

- **[[153.191]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.191
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite sections 13 and 152.15, a
  claimant need not serve a waiting period if the claim is in respect of
  benefits under Part I, VII.1 or VIII and the benefit period begins on
  or before October 25, 2020.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of waiting period

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite sections 13 and 152.15, a claimant who
  receives benefits under section 21 or 152.03 need not serve a waiting
  period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The claimant may nonetheless serve the waiting period
  if they are entitled to receive a payment referred to in subsection
  37(1) or 38(1) of the [Employment Insurance
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332) during that period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 40(6) and 40.1(2) of the [Employment
  Insurance Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332) do not apply to
  any waiting period to which subsection (1) applies.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-173, s. 6](#1258163-1258278){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1258163-1258278.html"}
- [SOR/2020-187, s. 1](#1259815-1261557){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1259815-1261557.html"}
- [SOR/2020-208, s. 5](#1261021-1261636){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1261021-1261636.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-153.191-20200810.html "Link to previous version of section 153.191"){.PITLink}

### [Rate of Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1258279 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Weekly insurable earnings

- **[[153.192]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.192
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 14(2), the weekly
  insurable earnings of a claimant whose benefit period begins on or
  after September 27, 2020 are deemed to be the greater of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant's insurable earnings in the
    calculation period referred to in subsection 14(4), divided by the
    number of weeks in that period in which they had insurable earnings,
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} \$909.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Self-employed persons

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 152.16(1), if a self-employed
  person's benefit period begins on or after September 27, 2020 and the
  result obtained by dividing the aggregate of the amounts referred to
  in paragraphs 152.16(1)(a) and (b) by 52 is less than \$909, the
  result is deemed to be \$909.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fishers

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph 8.1(a) of the [Employment Insurance
  (Fishing) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-445), the weekly
  insurable earnings of a fisher whose benefit period begins on or after
  September 27, 2020 are deemed to be the greater of the amount
  determined under that paragraph and \$909.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-173, s. 6](#1258163-1258283){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1258163-1258283.html"}
- [SOR/2020-187, s. 1](#1259815-1261585){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1259815-1261585.html"}
- [SOR/2020-187, s. 3](#1259819-1259993){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1259819-1259993.html"}
- [SOR/2020-208, s. 6](#1261023-1261642){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1261023-1261642.html"}
:::

Version](section-153.192-20200906.html "Link to previous version of section 153.192"){.PITLink}

### [Medical Certificate]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1261643 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of medical certificate
requirement --- [Employment Insurance
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332)

- **[[153.1921]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.1921
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of applying
  subsection 40(1) of the [Employment Insurance
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332), the requirement for a
  claimant to provide a medical certificate to the Commission completed
  by a medical doctor or other medical professional is suspended, unless
  otherwise indicated by the Commission.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension of medical certificate
  requirement --- special benefits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of applying any of sections 23.1,
  23.2, 23.3 and 152.06 to 152.062, the Commission may suspend the
  requirement for a claimant to provide a medical certificate completed
  by a medical doctor or other medical professional.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-208, s. 7](#1261028-1261650){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1261028-1261650.html"}
:::

### [Employment Insurance (Fishing) Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1261651 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Eligibility

**[[153.1922]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.1922 .sectionLabel}** A fisher who
does not meet the conditions under paragraph 8(2)(b) or (7)(b) of the
[Employment Insurance (Fishing)
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-445) may receive benefits under
section 8.1 of those Regulations if the fisher has received such
benefits during any of the periods referred to in subparagraph
153.1923(1)(a)(ii) or (iii) or (b)(ii) or (iii).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-208, s. 7](#1261028-1261655){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1261028-1261655.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determined earnings

- **[[153.1923]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.1923
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The rate of weekly benefits under
  section 8.1 of the [Employment Insurance (Fishing)
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-445) shall be calculated using
  the highest of the following earnings:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an initial claim for benefits under
    subsection 8(1) of those Regulations,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the earnings that would be used to
      calculate the fisher's rate of weekly benefits,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the earnings that were used to calculate the
      fisher's rate of weekly benefits for the benefit period that was
      established for the fisher under subsection 8(1) of those
      Regulations during the period beginning on September 29, 2019 and
      ending on June 20, 2020, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the earnings that were used to calculate the
      fisher's rate of weekly benefits for the benefit period that was
      established for the fisher under subsection 8(1) of those
      Regulations during the period beginning on September 30, 2018 and
      ending on June 15, 2019; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an initial claim for benefits made
    on or after March 28, 2021 under subsection 8(6) of those
    Regulations,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the earnings that would be used to
      calculate the fisher's rate of weekly benefits,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the earnings that were used to calculate the
      fisher's rate of weekly benefits for the benefit period that was
      established for the fisher under subsection 8(6) of those
      Regulations during the period beginning on March 29, 2020 and
      ending on December 19, 2020, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the earnings that were used to calculate the
      fisher's rate of weekly benefits for the benefit period that was
      established for the fisher under subsection 8(6) of those
      Regulations during the period beginning on March 31, 2019 and
      ending on December 21, 2019.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefit period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A fisher may have a benefit period established once
  under paragraph (1)(a) and once under paragraph (1)(b).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-208, s. 7](#1261028-1261670){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1261028-1261670.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying period

**[[153.1924]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.1924 .sectionLabel}** For the
purposes of the [Employment Insurance (Fishing)
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-445), the earnings determined under
subsection 153.1923(1) are the insurable earnings for the qualifying
period.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-208, s. 7](#1261028-1261674){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1261028-1261674.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to special benefits

**[[153.1925]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.1925 .sectionLabel}** Despite
subsection 12(1) of the [Employment Insurance (Fishing)
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-445), an insured person who is not
qualified to receive benefits under section 7 and who is claiming
special benefits qualifies to receive the special benefits if the person
has at least \$2,500 of insurable earnings from employment as a fisher
in their qualifying period.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-208, s. 7](#1261028-1261678){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1261028-1261678.html"}
:::

### [Deductions from Benefits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1258284 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusions from earnings

**[[153.193]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.193 .sectionLabel}** The following
are to be excluded from the earnings referred to in section 35 of the
[Employment Insurance Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332):

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} any pay or earnings referred to in subsection
  36(8), (9) or (19) of those Regulations if

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the claimant's benefit period begins on or after
    September 27, 2020, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the pay or earnings are declared to the Commission
    on or after September 27, 2020 and would otherwise have been
    allocated under section 36 of those Regulations to a week beginning
    on or after September 27, 2020; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any of the following portions of payments that are
  paid to a claimant as an insured person for the first week for which
  benefits are payable, if their waiting period has been waived under
  subsection 153.191(1):

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the portion of any payments referred to in
    subsection 37(1) of those Regulations that does not exceed 95% of
    the claimant's normal weekly earnings from the employment to which
    the payments relate, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} despite paragraph 38(1)(a) of those Regulations,
    the portion of any payments referred to in subsection 38(1) of those
    Regulations that does not exceed the claimant's normal weekly
    earnings from the employment to which the payments relate.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-173, s. 6](#1258163-1258292){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1258163-1258292.html"}
- [SOR/2020-187, s. 1](#1259815-1261596){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1259815-1261596.html"}
- [SOR/2020-187, s. 4](#1259821-1259992){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1259821-1259992.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-153.193-20200906.html "Link to previous version of section 153.193"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Disqualification and Disentitlement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1261577 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[employment]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[153.194]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.194 .sectionLabel}** Despite
paragraph 29(a), for the purposes of sections 30 to 33,
[[employment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} refers to any employment of the
claimant

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} within their qualifying period, but no earlier than
  12 weeks before the Sunday referred to in paragraph 10(1)(a); or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} within their benefit period.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-187, s. 1](#1259815-1261594){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1259815-1261594.html"}
:::

### [Claim Procedure]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1261595 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

**[[153.195]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.195 .sectionLabel}** Despite
paragraph 51(a), the Commission may, but is not required to, give the
claimant and the employer the opportunity referred to in that paragraph.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-187, s. 1](#1259815-1261600){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1259815-1261600.html"}
:::

### [Cessation of Effect]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1261601 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}September 25, 2021 or repeal

- **[[153.196]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.196
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and (3),
  this Part ceases to apply on the earlier of September 25, 2021 and the
  day on which Interim Order No. 8 Amending the Employment Insurance Act
  (Facilitated Access to Benefits) is repealed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 153.19 ceases to apply on the earlier of
  October 31, 2020 and the day on which Interim Order No. 8 Amending the
  Employment Insurance Act (Facilitated Access to Benefits) is repealed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Sections 153.1922 to 153.1924 cease to apply on
  December 18, 2021.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-187, s. 1](#1259815-1261608){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1259815-1261608.html"}
- [2021, c. 23, s. 327](#1307995-1327345){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1307995-1327345.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-153.196-20200927.html "Link to previous version of section 153.196"){.PITLink}

## [PART VIII.6]{.HLabel1}[Temporary Measures]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1327346 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Weekly insurable earnings

- **[[153.197]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153.197
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 14(2), the weekly
  insurable earnings of a claimant whose benefit period begins during
  the period beginning on September 26, 2021 and ending on November 20,
  2021 are deemed to be the greater of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant's insurable earnings in the
    calculation period referred to in subsection 14(4), divided by the
    number of weeks in that period in which they had insurable earnings,
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} \$545.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Self-employed persons

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 152.16(1), if a self-employed
  person's benefit period begins during the period beginning on
  September 26, 2021 and ending on November 20, 2021 and the result
  obtained by dividing the aggregate of the amounts referred to in
  paragraphs 152.16(1)(a) and (b) by 52 is less than \$545, the result
  is deemed to be \$545.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fishers

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph 8.1(a) of the [Employment Insurance
  (Fishing) Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-445), the weekly
  insurable earnings of a fisher whose benefit period begins during the
  period beginning on September 26, 2021 and ending on November 20, 2021
  are deemed to be the greater of the amount determined under that
  paragraph and \$545.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2021, c. 23, s. 328](#1308000-1327357){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1308000-1327357.html"}
:::

## [PART IX]{.HLabel1}[Repeals, Transitional Provisions, Related and Conditional Amendments and Coming into Force]{.HTitleText1} {#h-218683 .Part}

### [Repeals]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218684 .Subheading}

**[[154 and 155]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154_and_155
.sectionLabel}** [\[Repeals\]]{.Repealed}

### [Transitional Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218686 .Subheading}

#### [National Training Act]{.HTitleText3} {#h-218687 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Allowances

**[[156]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-156 .sectionLabel}** A training allowance
being paid under section 5 of the National Training Act when it is
repealed may continue to be paid in accordance with that Act and the
regulations made under it, as they read immediately before the repeal of
that Act, until the conclusion of the course to which the allowance
relates.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreements

**[[157]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-157 .sectionLabel}** An agreement under
section 7 of the National Training Act in effect when it is repealed
continues in effect according to the terms of the agreement.

**[[158]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2010, c.
12, s. 2190\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-158-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 158"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Unemployment Insurance Act]{.HTitleText3} {#h-218693 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Benefit periods beginning before this
section comes into force

- **[[159]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-159
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided in this
  section, all matters relating to a claim for benefits during a benefit
  period beginning before the Unemployment Insurance Act (the "former
  Act") is repealed shall be dealt with under that Act, including any
  amendments that may be made by Bill C-31, introduced in the second
  session of the 35th Parliament and entitled An Act to implement
  certain provisions of the budget tabled in Parliament on March 6,
  1996.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeals - written reasons not required

  [(1.01)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 70(2) of the former Act applies in
  respect of appeals under that Act except that the Tax Court of Canada
  need not give reasons in writing for its decision but may give reasons
  in writing where, in a particular case, the Court deems it advisable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deductions for undeclared earnings

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 19(3) of this Act applies to claimants
  who fail to declare all or some of their earnings in a period
  determined under the regulations beginning on or after January 5,
  1997, but the Commission may make deductions under subparagraph
  19(3)(a)(i) on or after that date in respect of any failure to declare
  earnings for such a period beginning after June 30, 1996.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deductions under subsection 19(4)

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 19(4) of this Act applies to claimants
  who begin attending a course or program after the former Act is
  repealed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parental benefits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 23 of this Act applies in place of section 20
  of the former Act to claimants claiming benefits for the care of
  children born or placed for adoption after that Act is repealed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Job creation projects

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Section 25 of the former Act applies only to
  claimants employed on job creation projects under that section when
  that Act is repealed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Training

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Section 26 of the former Act applies only to
  claimants in a course or program to which they are referred under that
  section before that Act is repealed.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2010, c. 12, s. 2191\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disentitlement and disqualification

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Sections 27 to 33 of this Act apply in place of
  sections 27 to 28.3 of the former Act in respect of events occurring
  after that Act is repealed that give rise to a disentitlement or
  disqualification under those sections and, for the purpose of applying
  those sections, a reference in the former Act to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} section 27 shall be read as a reference to section
    27 of this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} section 28 shall be read as a reference to section
    29 of this Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} section 28.1 shall be read as a reference to
    section 31 of this Act;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} section 28.2 shall be read as a reference to
    section 32 of this Act; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} section 28.3 shall be read as a reference to
    section 33 of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 145

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Section 145 of this Act applies in place of section
  123 of the former Act in respect of benefits paid after December 31,
  1995.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1996, c. 23, s. 159
- 1998, c. 19, s. 274
- 1999, c. 31, s. 82(F)
- 2010, c. 12, s. 2191
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-159-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 159"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hours of insurable employment and
earnings before 1997

**[[160]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160 .sectionLabel}** For the purpose of
calculating after 1996 how many hours of insurable employment and the
amount of insurable earnings a claimant has under this Act, other than
Part VIII, insurable employment and insurable earnings occurring

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} before June 30, 1996 shall be determined in
  accordance with the former Act; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} on or after June 30, 1996 but before January 5, 1997
  shall be determined in accordance with this Act, as it applies on June
  30, 1996.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Premiums

**[[161]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-161 .sectionLabel}** All matters relating to
the payment of premiums under the former Act shall be dealt with under
that Act.

**[[162]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-162 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2010, c.
12, s. 2192\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-162-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 162"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Estimated insurable earnings for 1996-97

- **[[163]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-163 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of section 78, the Commission's estimate of the insurable
  earnings of all insured persons in the fiscal year 1996-97 shall be
  published in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) if it is
  not set out in the Main Estimates tabled in Parliament for that year.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Plan for 1996-97

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The plan mentioned in section 79 shall be published
  in the [Canada Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/) for the fiscal year
  1996-97 if it is not included in the Main Estimates tabled in
  Parliament for that year.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers and functions

- **[[164]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-164 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  powers or functions of any person under the former Act shall be
  exercised or performed by the person who exercises the corresponding
  powers or performs the corresponding functions under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Boards of referees, etc.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Boards of referees, panels, chairpersons, umpires and
  the chief umpire established, appointed or designated under the former
  Act continue as if they had been established, appointed or designated
  under this Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waivers and agreements

**[[165]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-165 .sectionLabel}** Waivers and agreements
made under paragraph 4(1)(d) of the former Act and in effect when that
Act is repealed continue in effect as though they had been made under
paragraph 5(4)(d) of this Act.

**[[166]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-166 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 2010, c.
12, s. 2193\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-166-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 166"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Transitional Regulations]{.HTitleText3} {#h-218741 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[167]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-167 .sectionLabel}** The Commission may,
with the approval of the Governor in Council, make regulations providing
for any other transitional matters, including regulations

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} providing for the transition from weeks of insurable
  employment to hours of insurable employment, or from any other basis
  to another under Part VIII; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} for establishing requirements to qualify to receive
  benefits, the duration of entitlement to benefits, benefit rates and
  disentitlement or disqualification from receiving benefits.

### [Consequential and Related Amendments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218746 .Subheading}

**[[168 to 186]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-168_to_186
.sectionLabel}** \[Amendments\]

### [Changes in Terminology]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218748 .Subheading}

**[[187]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-187 .sectionLabel}** \[Amendments\]

### [Conditional Amendments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218750 .Subheading}

**[[188 and 189]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-188_and_189
.sectionLabel}** \[Amendments\]

### [Coming into Force]{.HTitleText2} {#h-218752 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coming into force

- **[[190]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-190
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except as otherwise provided in this
  section, this Act comes into force on June 30, 1996.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certain provisions in force on January
  1, 1997

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 4, subsection 5(6), sections 66 and 67,
  subsections 82(1) and (2), paragraphs 90(1)(d), (h) and (i), section
  95 and subsections 96(4) and (5) come into force on January 1, 1997.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certain provisions in force on January
  5, 1997

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The following provisions come into force on January
  5, 1997:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the definitions [[major attachment
    claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and [[minor attachment
    claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 6(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} section 7;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subsection 12(2);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} sections 14 to 17;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} subsection 19(2);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} subsection 28(4);

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 30(1)(a) and subsections 30(5) and (6);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 31(c);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 32(2)(c);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} subsection 38(3);

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} section 55;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 108(1)(h);

  - [(l.1)]{.lawlabel} subsection 153.1(3); and

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} Schedule I.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certain provisions in force on January
  5, 1997

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 7.1(1) to (3) come into force on
  January 5, 1997, but in applying them the Commission may take into
  account notices issued under subsection 7.1(4) on or after June 30,
  1996.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsection 19(3) in force on January 5,
  1997

  [(3.2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 19(3) comes into force on January 5,
  1997, but the Commission may make deductions under subparagraph
  19(3)(a)(i) on and after that date in respect of any failure to
  declare earnings for a period beginning on or after June 30, 1996.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interim provisions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The provisions set out in Schedule II apply in place
  of the provisions listed in subsections (2) and (3) from June 30, 1996
  until the coming into force of those provisions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuing application of benefit rate
  provision

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The provisions enacted by section 6 of Schedule II
  continue to apply in place of sections 14, 16 and 17 of this Act to
  claimants whose benefit periods begin on or after June 30, 1996 and
  before January 5, 1997.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certain provisions in force in January
  1, 1998

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Sections 172 to 175 come into force on January 1,
  1998.
