::::: section
:::: {#218794 .Schedule}
::: {}
## [SCHEDULE II]{.scheduleLabel}[(Section 190)]{.OriginatingRef}[Interim Provisions]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-218795 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** The following applies in place of section 4:

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum yearly insurable earnings

    - **4** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 17, subsection
      82(2) and sections 95 and 145, the maximum yearly insurable
      earnings is \$39,000 for 1996.

    - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum weekly insurable earnings

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, the maximum weekly
      insurable earnings is \$750.
  :::
  ::::

- **2** The following applies in place of subsection 5(6):

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations to exclude employment

    [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may, with the approval of the
    Governor in Council, make regulations for excluding from insurable
    employment

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any employment if it appears to the Commission
      that because of the laws of a country other than Canada a
      duplication of contributions or benefits will result;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the entire employment of a person who is employed
      by one employer partly in insurable employment and partly in other
      employment;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any employment if it appears to the Commission
      that the nature of the work performed by persons employed in that
      employment is similar to the nature of the work performed by
      persons employed in employment that is not insurable employment;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the employment of a member of a religious order
      who has taken a vow of poverty and whose remuneration is paid
      directly or by the member to the order;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any employment in which persons are employed to
      an inconsiderable extent or for an inconsiderable consideration;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any employment provided under regulations made
      under section 24 or employment measures authorized by subsection
      58(1); and

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any employment with an employer in which persons
      are employed for fewer than 20 hours in a week or in which the
      earnings of persons are less than 30% of the maximum weekly
      insurable earnings.
  :::
  ::::

- **3** The following apply in place of the definitions [[major
  attachment claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} and [[minor attachment
  claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 6(1):

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText

  [[major attachment claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[major attachment claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a claimant
      who qualifies to receive benefits and has been employed in
      insurable employment for 20 or more weeks in the claimant's
      qualifying period; ([prestataire de la première
      catégorie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[minor attachment claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[minor attachment claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a claimant
      who qualifies to receive benefits and has been employed in
      insurable employment for fewer than 20 weeks in the claimant's
      qualifying period; ([prestataire de la deuxième
      catégorie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})
  :::
  ::::

- **4** The following applies in place of section 7:

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When benefits payable

    - **7** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unemployment benefits are payable as
      provided in this Part to an insured person who qualifies to
      receive them.

    - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualification requirement

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} An insured person, other than a new entrant or a
      re-entrant to the labour force, qualifies if the person

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has had an interruption of earnings from
        employment; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has had during their qualifying period at least
        the number of weeks of insurable employment set out in the
        following table in relation to the regional rate of unemployment
        that applies to the person.

        <figure>
        <table
        class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize9 topdouble topbot">
        <thead>
        <tr class="topdouble">
        <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom alignleft"
        scope="col">Regional Rate of Unemployment</th>
        <th class="borderBottom bottom alignleft" scope="col">Required Number of
        Weeks of Insurable Employment</th>
        </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">6% and under</td>
        <td class="noBorderBottom top aligncenter">20</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 6% but not
        more than 7%</td>
        <td class="noBorderBottom top aligncenter">19</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 7% but not
        more than 8%</td>
        <td class="noBorderBottom top aligncenter">18</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 8% but not
        more than 9%</td>
        <td class="noBorderBottom top aligncenter">17</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 9% but not
        more than 10%</td>
        <td class="noBorderBottom top aligncenter">16</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 10% but not
        more than 11%</td>
        <td class="noBorderBottom top aligncenter">15</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 11% but not
        more than 12%</td>
        <td class="noBorderBottom top aligncenter">14</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 12% but not
        more than 13%</td>
        <td class="noBorderBottom top aligncenter">13</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight top">more than 13%</td>
        <td class="top aligncenter">12</td>
        </tr>
        </tbody>
        </table>
        <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
        </figure>

    - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualification requirement for new
      entrants and re-entrants

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} An insured person who is a new entrant or a
      re-entrant to the labour force qualifies if the person

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has had an interruption of earnings from
        employment; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has had 26 or more weeks of insurable
        employment during their qualifying period.

    - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New entrants and re-entrants

      [(4)]{.lawlabel} An insured person is a new entrant or a
      re-entrant to the labour force if, during the last 52 weeks before
      their qualifying period, the person has had fewer than 14

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} weeks of insurable employment;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} weeks for which benefits have been paid or were
        payable to the person;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribed weeks that relate to employment in
        the labour force; or

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} weeks comprised of any combination of those
        weeks.

    - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Computation of weeks

      [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (4), a week that
      is taken into account under any of paragraphs (4)(a), (b) or (c)
      may not be taken into account under the other.

    - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exhaustion or termination of
      entitlement to U.S. benefits

      [(6)]{.lawlabel} A claimant is not qualified to receive benefits
      if it is jointly determined that the claimant must first exhaust
      or terminate benefit rights under the laws of another
      jurisdiction, as provided by Article VI of the Agreement Between
      Canada and the United States Respecting Unemployment Insurance,
      signed on March 6 and 12, 1942.
  :::
  ::::

- **5** The following applies in place of subsection 12(2):

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General maximum

    [(2)]{.lawlabel} The maximum number of weeks for which benefits may
    be paid in a benefit period because of any reasons other than those
    referred to in subsection (3) shall be determined in accordance with
    the table in Schedule I by reference to the regional rate of
    unemployment that applies to the claimant and the number of weeks of
    insurable employment of the claimant in their qualifying period.
  :::
  ::::

- **6** The following applies in place of section 14:

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rate of weekly benefits

    - **14** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The rate of weekly benefits payable to a
      claimant is an amount calculated as follows, to a maximum of
      \$413:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 55% of the claimant's average weekly insurable
        earnings, in the case of a claimant in respect of whom
        paragraph (b) does not apply; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any case where it is established, in such
        manner as the Commission may direct, that the prescribed
        circumstances exist in relation to one or more persons who are
        dependants of the claimant or their spouse or that, in the
        opinion of the Commission, even though the prescribed
        circumstances do not exist, the claimant or their spouse
        supports one or more persons who are dependants of the claimant
        or of their spouse,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 60% of the claimant's average weekly
          insurable earnings, if those earnings do not exceed 50% of the
          maximum weekly insurable earnings for the year in which the
          benefit period is established, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the greater of 55% of the claimant's average
          weekly insurable earnings and \$225, if the claimant's average
          weekly insurable earnings exceed 50% of the maximum weekly
          insurable earnings for that year.

    - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Average weekly insurable earnings
      --- major attachment claimant

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} The average weekly insurable earnings of a major
      attachment claimant are the insurable earnings in the last 20
      weeks of insurable employment in their qualifying period divided
      by 20.

    - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Average weekly insurable earnings
      --- minor attachment claimant

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} The average weekly insurable earnings of a minor
      attachment claimant are the insurable earnings in their qualifying
      period divided by the larger of the following divisors:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the divisor that equals the number of weeks of
        insurable employment in their qualifying period, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the divisor determined in accordance with the
        following table by reference to the applicable regional rate of
        unemployment.

        <figure>
        <table
        class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
        <thead>
        <tr class="topdouble">
        <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom top" scope="col">Regional Rate of
        Unemployment</th>
        <th class="borderBottom top aligncenter" scope="col">Divisor</th>
        </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">not more than 8%</td>
        <td class="noBorderBottom aligncenter">20</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 8% but not more
        than 9%</td>
        <td class="noBorderBottom top aligncenter">19</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 9% but not more
        than 10%</td>
        <td class="noBorderBottom top aligncenter">18</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 10% but not more
        than 11%</td>
        <td class="noBorderBottom top aligncenter">17</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 11% but not more
        than 12%</td>
        <td class="noBorderBottom top aligncenter">16</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">more than 12% but not more
        than 13%</td>
        <td class="noBorderBottom top aligncenter">15</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td class="noBorderRight top">more than 13%</td>
        <td class="top aligncenter">14</td>
        </tr>
        </tbody>
        </table>
        <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
        </figure>

    - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

      [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Commission may, with the approval of the
      Governor in Council, make regulations

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} defining and determining who is a dependant of
        a claimant or a spouse of a claimant, or who is a spouse of a
        claimant;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} determining the weekly insurable earnings of
        claimants; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} where earnings are paid or payable in the
        qualifying period for a period, whether on a weekly basis or
        otherwise, for calculating and establishing for the purposes of
        this Part

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the weeks or number of weeks that are to be
          taken as weeks of insurable employment in that period, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount to be taken as the insurable
          earnings or average weekly insurable earnings for any weeks or
          number of weeks in that period.
  :::
  ::::

- **7** The following applies in place of subsection 19(2):

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Earnings in unemployed periods

    [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (3) and (4), if a claimant
    has earnings during any other week of unemployment, there shall be
    deducted from the benefits payable to the claimant in that week the
    amount, if any, of the earnings that exceeds 25% of their weekly
    rate of benefit.
  :::
  ::::

- **8** The following applies in place of subsection 28(4):

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

    [(4)]{.lawlabel} No weeks of disqualification shall be carried
    forward against a claimant who has had 20 or more weeks of insurable
    employment since the event giving rise to the disqualification.
  :::
  ::::

- - **9** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following applies in place of paragraph
    30(1)(a):

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant has, since losing or leaving the
      employment, been employed in insurable employment for the number
      of weeks required by section 7 to qualify for benefits; or
    :::
    ::::

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following applies in place of subsections 30(5)
    and (6):

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on qualifying for
      benefits

      [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where a claimant who has lost or left an
      employment as described in subsection (1) makes an initial claim
      for benefits, the following weeks of insurable employment may not
      be used for the purposes of subsection 7(2) or (3):

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} weeks of insurable employment from that or any
        other employment before the day on which that employment was
        lost or left; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} weeks of insurable employment in any employment
        that the claimant subsequently loses or leaves, as described in
        subsection (1).

    - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on number of weeks and
      rate of benefits

      [(6)]{.lawlabel} No weeks of insurable employment in any
      employment that a claimant loses or leaves as described in
      subsection (1) may be used for the purposes of subsection 12(2) or
      section 14.
    :::
    ::::

- **10** The following applies in place of paragraph 31(c):

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claimant, after the beginning of the period of
    suspension, accumulates with another employer the number of weeks of
    insurable employment required under section 7 in order to qualify to
    receive benefits under this Act.
  :::
  ::::

- **11** The following applies in place of paragraph 32(2)(c):

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claimant, after the beginning of the period of
    leave, accumulates with another employer the number of weeks of
    insurable employment required under section 7 in order to qualify to
    receive benefits under this Act.
  :::
  ::::

- **12** The following applies in place of section 66:

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Premium rate

    **66** The premium rate for 1996 is the rate established for that
    year under section 48.1 of the Unemployment Insurance Act
    immediately before its repeal.
  :::
  ::::

- **13** The following applies in place of section 67:

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Employee's premium

    - **67** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For every week during which a person is
      employed in insurable employment, the person shall pay, by
      deduction as provided in Part IV, an amount equal to such
      percentage of their insurable earnings as is fixed by the
      Commission as the employee's premium for the year in which that
      week occurs.

    - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of employer's premium

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} For every week during which an employer employs a
      person in insurable employment, the employer shall pay, in respect
      of that person and in the manner provided in Part IV, an amount
      equal to such percentage of that person's insurable earnings as is
      fixed by the Commission as the employer's premium payable by
      employers or a class of employers of which the employer is a
      member, as the case may be, for the year in which that week
      occurs.

    - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Overlapping pay periods

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsections (1) and (2), where
      insurable earnings are paid to a person in a year after the year
      in which their insurable employment occurred, all that insurable
      employment is, for the purposes of calculating insurable earnings
      and premiums payable in respect of them, deemed to have occurred
      in the year in which the insurable earnings are paid.
  :::
  ::::

- **14** The following applies in place of subsection 82(1):

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deduction and payment of premiums

    - **82** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every employer paying remuneration to a
      person employed by the employer in insurable employment shall

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} deduct the prescribed amount from that
        remuneration as or on account of the employee's premium payable
        by that insured person under section 67 for any weeks in respect
        of which that remuneration is paid; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} remit that amount, together with the employer's
        premium payable by the employer under that section for those
        weeks, to the Receiver General at the prescribed time and in the
        prescribed manner.
  :::
  ::::

- **15** The following applies in place of section 95:

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Employee overpayment

    - **95** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where the aggregate of all amounts
      deducted as required from the insurable earnings of an insured
      person for a year, whether by one or more employers, on account of
      that person's employee's premiums for that year under this Act
      exceeds an amount equal to such percentage of the person's maximum
      yearly insurable earnings for the year as is fixed by the
      Commission, the excess is an overpayment made by the person.

    - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation --- remuneration for
      more than 52 weeks

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (1) and section 3, an
      overpayment is not created when amounts are deducted and remitted
      as required from the insurable earnings of an insured person in
      excess of the percentage of the maximum yearly insurable earnings
      for the year, if the excess occurs by reason only that the
      remuneration paid to the person in that year is in respect of more
      than 52 calendar weeks.
  :::
  ::::

- **16** The following applies in place of paragraph 108(1)(h):

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} for the allocation of the hours a person is
    employed with an employer and earnings to weeks or to pay periods;

  - [(h.1)]{.lawlabel} for calculating and determining the hours a
    person is employed with an employer, the amount of insurable
    earnings of insured persons and the amount of premiums payable;

  - [(h.2)]{.lawlabel} where earnings are paid or payable for a period
    otherwise than in respect of weeks, for calculating and establishing

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the weeks or number of weeks to be taken as weeks
      of insurable employment in that period, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the amount to be taken as insurable earnings in
      any of those weeks or number of weeks in that period;
  :::
  ::::

- **17** The following applies in place of subsection 153.1(3):

  :::: section
  ::: AmendedText
  - [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

    [(3)]{.lawlabel} The scheme established by the regulations may not
    provide special benefits to persons who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} have less than 20 weeks of insurable employment in
    their qualifying period; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} are subject to an increase under section 7.1 in the
    number of hours of insurable employment required to qualify for
    benefits.
  :::
  ::::

- **18** The following applies in place of Schedule I:

  ::::::: section
  :::::: AmendedText
  ::::: section
  :::: Schedule
  ::: {}
  ## [SCHEDULE I]{.scheduleLabel}[(Subsection 12(2))]{.OriginatingRef} {#schedule-isubsection-122 .scheduleLabel}
  :::

<figure id="1376686">

<thead>

<th class="borderRight borderBottom bottom"></th>
<th colspan="12" class="borderBottom bottom aligncenter"

</tr>
<tr>
<th class="borderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Number of hours
of insurable employment in qualifying period</th>
<th class="borderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">6% and

:::::: section
::::: {#1327377 .Schedule}
::: {}
## [SCHEDULE VI]{.scheduleLabel}[(Subparagraph 12(2.3)(a)(ii))]{.OriginatingRef}[Regions for the Purpose of Benefits for Seasonal Workers]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-1327378 .scheduleLabel}
:::

#### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#interpretation .SchedHeadL2}

- **1** The definitions in this section apply in this Schedule.

  [[Census Agglomeration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Census Agglomeration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} for the purposes of
      section 4 means a census agglomeration within the meaning of the
      Statistics Canada document entitled Standard Geographical
      Classification (SGC) 1996 and for the purposes of sections 5 and 7
      means a census agglomeration within the meaning of the Statistics
      Canada document entitled Standard Geographical Classification
      (SGC) 2011. ([agglomération de recensement]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Census Division]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Census Division]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a census division
      within the meaning of the Statistics Canada document entitled
      Standard Geographical Classification (SGC) 1996. ([division de
      recensement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Census Metropolitan Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Census Metropolitan Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a census
      metropolitan area within the meaning of the Statistics Canada
      document entitled Standard Geographical Classification (SGC)
      1996. ([région métropolitaine de recensement]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Census Subdivision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Census Subdivision]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} for the purposes of
      sections 4 and 6 means a census subdivision within the meaning of
      the Statistics Canada document entitled Standard Geographical
      Classification (SGC) 1996 and for the purposes of sections 5 and 7
      means a census subdivision within the meaning of the Statistics
      Canada document entitled Standard Geographical Classification
      (SGC) 2011. ([subdivision de recensement]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

#### [Regions]{.HTitleText2} {#regions .SchedHeadL2}

##### [Quebec]{.HTitleText3} {#quebec .SchedHeadL3}

- - **2** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The region of Gaspésie ---
    Îles-de-la-Madeleine, consisting of Census Division Nos. 1 to 8 and
    98.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The region of Central Quebec, consisting of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} those portions of Census Division Nos. 21 and 22
      that are not part of the Census Metropolitan Area of Quebec City;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} those portions of Census Division Nos. 37 and 38
      that are not part of the Census Metropolitan Area of
      Trois-Rivières;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} those portions of Census Division Nos. 41, 42, 44
      and 45 that are not part of the Census Metropolitan Area of
      Sherbrooke;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} those portions of Census Division Nos. 52, 60, 75
      and 76 that are not part of the Census Metropolitan Area of
      Montréal;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the portion of Census Division No. 82 that is not
      part of the Census Metropolitan Area of Ottawa --- Hull; and

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} Census Division Nos. 31 to 36, 39, 40, 48, 49,
      50, 51, 53, 61, 62, 63, 77, 78, 80 and 90.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} The region of North Western Quebec, consisting of
    Census Division Nos. 79, 83 to 89 and 99.

  - [(4)]{.lawlabel} The region of Lower Saint Lawrence and North Shore,
    consisting of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the portion of Census Division No. 94 that is not
      part of the Census Metropolitan Area of Chicoutimi --- Jonquière;
      and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Census Division Nos. 9 to 18, 28, 91, 92, 93, 95,
      96 and 97.

  - [(5)]{.lawlabel} The region of Chicoutimi --- Jonquière, consisting
    of the Census Metropolitan Area of Chicoutimi --- Jonquière.

##### [Nova Scotia]{.HTitleText3} {#nova-scotia .SchedHeadL3}

- - **3** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The region of Eastern Nova Scotia, consisting
    of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Census Division Nos. 13 to 18; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the portion of Census Division No. 9 that is not
      part of the Census Metropolitan Area of Halifax.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The region of Western Nova Scotia, consisting of
    Census Division Nos. 1 to 8, 10, 11 and 12.

##### [New Brunswick]{.HTitleText3} {#new-brunswick .SchedHeadL3}

- - **4** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The region of Madawaska --- Charlotte,
    consisting of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the portion of Census Division No. 2 that is not
      part of the Census Metropolitan Area of Saint John;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Census Division Nos. 11, 12 and 13; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} Census Subdivision Nos. 1310004, 1310005,
      1310006, 1310007, 1310008, 1310011, 1310012, 1310013, 1310014,
      1310016, 1310021, 1310024, 1310025 and 1310054.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The region of Restigouche --- Albert, consisting of

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Census Division Nos. 8, 9, 14 and 15;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Census Subdivision Nos. 1303014 and 1303018;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the portion of Census Division No. 4 that is not
      part of the Census Metropolitan Area of Saint John;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the portion of Census Division No. 6 that is not
      part of the Census Agglomeration of Moncton;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} Census Subdivision Nos. 1307001, 1307002,
      1307004, 1307005, 1307007, 1307008, 1307009, 1307011, 1307012,
      1307013, 1307014, 1307016, 1307024, 1307029 and 1307052; and

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} Census Subdivision Nos. 1310036 and 1310037.

##### [Prince Edward Island]{.HTitleText3} {#prince-edward-island .SchedHeadL3}

- - **5** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The region of Charlottetown, consisting of
    the Census Agglomeration of Charlottetown.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The region of Prince Edward Island, consisting of
    all Census Subdivisions that are not part of the Census
    Agglomeration of Charlottetown.

##### [Newfoundland/Labrador]{.HTitleText3} {#newfoundlandlabrador .SchedHeadL3}

- **6** The region of Newfoundland/Labrador, consisting of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Census Subdivision Nos. 01557 and 01559 and the
    portion of Census Division No. 1 that is not part of the Census
    Metropolitan Area of St. John's; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Census Division Nos. 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9 and 10.

##### [Yukon]{.HTitleText3} {#yukon .SchedHeadL3}

- **7** The region of Yukon, consisting of all Census Subdivisions that
  are not part of the Census Agglomeration of Whitehorse.

## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- 2003, c. 15, s. 25(1)

  - - [25]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Sections 15 to 20 and 22 apply to
      a claimant for any benefit period

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that begins on or after January 4, 2004; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that has not ended before January 4, 2004, but
        only for weeks of benefits that begin on or after that date.

- --- 2005, c. 34, s. 56

  - ###### Repeal of sections 127 and 128 of the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6) {#repeal-of-sections-127-and-128-of-the-employment-insurance-act .MarginalNote}

    [56]{.lawlabel} The repeal of sections 127 and 128 of the
    [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6) does not affect the
    validity of a disclosure of information, or an agreement for the
    purpose of making information available, made under those sections
    by the Minister of Human Resources Development or the Minister of
    State to be styled Minister of Human Resources and Skills
    Development.

- --- 2008, c. 28, ss. 39(3), (4)

  - - [39]{.lawlabel} [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies in respect
      of remittances by a prescribed person that are first due after
      February 25, 2008.

    - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) applies in respect of payments and
      remittances that are required to be first made after February 25,
      2008.

- --- 2008, c. 28, s. 122

  - ###### Application {#application .MarginalNote}

    [122]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph 4(a) of the [Canada
    Employment Insurance Financing Board Act](/eng/acts/C-4.4), the
    Board sets the premium rate under section 66 of the [Employment
    Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as enacted by section 127.

- --- 2009, c. 2, s. 226(2)

  - ###### Transitional {#transitional .MarginalNote}

    - [226]{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} The maximum number of weeks for
      which benefits may be paid in a benefit period that is established
      for a claimant who is included in Pilot Project No. 10 and whose
      benefit period has not ended before the second Sunday before the
      day on which this Act receives royal assent is to be determined in
      accordance with Schedule I to the [Employment Insurance
      Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as enacted by subsection 224(1).

- --- 2009, c. 2, s. 227

  - ###### Deeming provision {#deeming-provision .MarginalNote}

    [227]{.lawlabel} Section 66.1 of the [Employment Insurance
    Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as enacted by section 9 of chapter 5 of the
    Statutes of Canada, 2001, is deemed to have read as follows:

    > ::: ReadAsText
    > - ###### Premium rates for 2002 and 2003 {#premium-rates-for-2002-and-2003 .MarginalNote}
    >
    >   [66.1]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding section 66, the premium rates
    >   for the years 2002 and 2003 are 2.2% and 2.1%, respectively.
    > :::

- --- 2009, c. 2, s. 228

  - ###### Deeming provision {#deeming-provision-1 .MarginalNote}

    [228]{.lawlabel} Section 66.3 of the [Employment Insurance
    Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as enacted by section 25 of chapter 22 of the
    Statutes of Canada, 2004, is deemed to have read as follows:

    > ::: ReadAsText
    > - ###### Premium rate for 2005 {#premium-rate-for-2005 .MarginalNote}
    >
    >   [66.3]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding section 66, the premium rate
    >   for the year 2005 is 1.95%.
    > :::

- --- 2009, c. 2, s. 229

  - ###### Application {#application-1 .MarginalNote}

    [229]{.lawlabel} Subsection 224(1) applies with respect to every
    claimant whose benefit period has not ended before the day on which
    that subsection comes into force and whose benefit period does not
    begin after September 11, 2010.

- --- 2009, c. 30, s. 7

  - ###### Subsection 55(10) of regulations {#subsection-5510-of-regulations .MarginalNote}

    [7]{.lawlabel} With respect to a claimant to whom any of sections 3
    to 6 applies whose benefit period has not been extended under any of
    subsections 10(13) to (13.3) of the [Employment Insurance
    Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), subsection 55(10) of the [Employment
    Insurance Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332) is deemed to
    read as follows:

    > ::: ReadAsText
    > - [(10)]{.lawlabel} In a claimant's benefit period, a claimant who
    >   is not in Canada or a claimant referred to in subsection (8),
    >   subject to the applicable maximums set out in paragraphs (7)(a)
    >   and (b), may combine the weeks of benefits to which the claimant
    >   is entitled, but the total number of weeks of benefits shall not
    >   exceed 50 or, if the maximum number of weeks during which
    >   benefits may be paid to a claimant under section 225 of the
    >   [Budget Implementation Act, 2009](/eng/acts/B-9.858) is equal to
    >   or greater than 50 weeks as a result of the application of any
    >   provision of any Act of Parliament, other than the [Employment
    >   Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), the number that corresponds to
    >   that maximum number of weeks.
    > :::

- --- 2009, c. 33, s. 18

  - ###### Agreements {#agreements .MarginalNote}

    [18]{.lawlabel} Agreements referred to in subsection 152.02(1) of
    the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6) may be entered into
    at any time during the period that begins on the day on which this
    Act receives royal assent and ends on the day on which section 16
    comes into force, but any such agreement that is entered into during
    that period is deemed to have been entered into on the day on which
    that section comes into force.

- --- 2009, c. 33, s. 19

  - ###### Benefit periods during 2011 {#benefit-periods-during-2011 .MarginalNote}

    [19]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph 152.07(1)(a) of the [Employment
    Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), a benefit period under Part VII.1
    of that Act may begin on or after January 1, 2011 if the
    self-employed person to whom the benefit period relates has, during
    the period that begins on the later of the day on which this Act
    receives royal assent and January 1, 2010 and ends on April 1, 2010,
    entered into an agreement referred to in subsection 152.02(1) of
    that Act.

- --- 2009, c. 33, s. 20

  - ###### Violations {#violations .MarginalNote}

    [20]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection 152.07(6) of the
    [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), a violation accumulated
    by an individual under section 7.1 of that Act before the coming
    into force of that subsection is deemed to be a violation
    accumulated by the individual under section 152.07 on the day on
    which the notice of violation was given to the individual even
    though that section was not in force on that day.

- --- 2010, c. 9, s. 4

  - ###### Transitional {#transitional-1 .MarginalNote}

    - [4]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Sections 2 and 3 apply to a
      claimant whose benefit period began less than 104 weeks before the
      day on which this Act comes into force if the period referred to
      in subsection 23(2) of the [Employment Insurance
      Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6)

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has not ended before that day; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ended before that day and, within the period
        referred to in that subsection, the claimant's parental leave is
        deferred or the claimant is directed to return to duty from
        parental leave in accordance with regulations made under the
        [National Defence Act](/eng/acts/N-5), and the claimant's
        deferral or return to duty, as the case may be, has not ended
        before that day.

    - ###### Special case {#special-case .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 2 applies to a claimant and the period
      referred to in subsection 23(2) of the [Employment Insurance
      Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6) is extended to 104 weeks if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within the period referred to in that
        subsection, in accordance with regulations made under the
        [National Defence Act](/eng/acts/N-5), the claimant's parental
        leave is deferred or the claimant is directed to return to duty
        from parental leave, as the case may be;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant's deferral or return to duty ended
        before the day on which this Act comes into force;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claimant's benefit period has not ended
        before that day; and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the period referred to in that subsection ended
        before that day.

    - ###### Other special case {#other-special-case .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} A claimant's benefit period and the period
      referred to in subsection 23(2) of the [Employment Insurance
      Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6) are each extended to 104 weeks if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} within the period referred to in that
        subsection, in accordance with regulations made under the
        [National Defence Act](/eng/acts/N-5), the claimant's parental
        leave is deferred or the claimant is directed to return to duty
        from parental leave, as the case may be;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant's deferral or return to duty ended
        before the day on which this Act comes into force; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claimant's benefit period and the period
        referred to in that subsection each began less than 104 weeks
        before that day and both ended before that day.

- --- 2010, c. 12, s. 2195

  - ###### Account closed {#account-closed .MarginalNote}

    [2195]{.lawlabel} The account in the accounts of Canada known as the
    Employment Insurance Account is deemed to have been closed at the
    beginning of January 1, 2009 and removed from the accounts of Canada
    at that time.

- --- 2010, c. 12, s. 2196

  - ###### Section 76 {#section-76 .MarginalNote}

    [2196]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, any amount purporting to
    have been credited to the Employment Insurance Account on or after
    January 1, 2009 and charged to the Consolidated Revenue Fund under
    section 76 of the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6) is
    deemed to have never been credited to that Account and charged to
    that Fund.

- --- 2010, c. 12, s. 2197

  - ###### Amounts credited and charged {#amounts-credited-and-charged .MarginalNote}

    [2197]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, all amounts credited or
    charged to the Employment Insurance Account on or after January 1,
    2009 are deemed to have been credited or charged, as the case may
    be, to the Employment Insurance Operating Account established by
    section 70.2 of the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as
    enacted by section 2185.

- --- 2010, c. 12, s. 2198

  - ###### Appropriation Acts {#appropriation-acts .MarginalNote}

    [2198]{.lawlabel} Every authority in any appropriation Act to make
    recoverable expenditures on behalf of the Employment Insurance
    Account in respect of the fiscal year 2010--2011 is deemed to be an
    authority to make recoverable expenditures on behalf of the
    Employment Insurance Operating Account established by section 70.2
    of the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as enacted by
    section 2185.

- --- 2010, c. 12, s. 2199

  - ###### Employment Insurance Operating Account --- audit for fiscal year 2008--2009 {#employment-insurance-operating-account-audit-for-fiscal-year-20082009 .MarginalNote}

    [2199]{.lawlabel} The Auditor General of Canada shall audit the
    Employment Insurance Operating Account, established by section 70.2
    of the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as enacted by
    section 2185, in respect of the fiscal year 2008--2009, and a report
    of that audit shall be made to the Minister of Human Resources and
    Skills Development.

- --- 2010, c. 12, s. 2200

  - ###### Employment Insurance Account --- re-audit {#employment-insurance-account-re-audit .MarginalNote}

    [2200]{.lawlabel} Despite sections 2186 and 2195, and as a
    consequence of the establishment of the Employment Insurance
    Operating Account by section 70.2 of the [Employment Insurance
    Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as enacted by section 2185, the Auditor
    General of Canada may re-audit the Employment Insurance Account in
    respect of any fiscal year if he or she considers it necessary to do
    so, and a report of that audit is to be made to the Minister of
    Human Resources and Skills Development.

- --- 2012, c. 19, s. 251

  - ###### Definitions {#definitions .MarginalNote}

    [251]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in sections 252 to
    270.

    [[board of referees]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[board of referees]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a board of
        referees established under Part VI of the [Employment Insurance
        Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as it read immediately before the coming
        into force of section 247. ([conseil arbitral]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

    [[Pension Appeals Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[Pension Appeals Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Pension
        Appeals Board established under section 83 of the [Canada
        Pension Plan](/eng/acts/C-8), as it read immediately before the
        coming into force of section 229. ([Commission d'appel des
        pensions]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[Review Tribunal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[Review Tribunal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Review Tribunal
        established under section 82 of the [Canada Pension
        Plan](/eng/acts/C-8), as it read immediately before the coming
        into force of section 229. ([tribunal de
        révision]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[Social Security Tribunal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[Social Security Tribunal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
        Social Security Tribunal established under section 44 of the
        Department of Human Resources and Skills Development
        Act. ([Tribunal de la sécurité sociale]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

    [[umpire]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[umpire]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an umpire appointed under
        Part VI of the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as
        it read immediately before the coming into force of section
        247. ([juge-arbitre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- --- 2012, c. 19, s. 252

  - ###### Information {#information .MarginalNote}

    [252]{.lawlabel} The Pension Appeals Board, a Review Tribunal, a
    board of referees or an umpire must transfer to the Social Security
    Tribunal any information under their control that either relates to
    an application of which that Tribunal is seized or that the Governor
    in Council has, by regulation, prescribed.

- --- 2012, c. 19, s. 263

  - ###### Board of referees --- chairpersons {#board-of-referees-chairpersons .MarginalNote}

    - [263]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The chairpersons of a board of
      referees referred to in subsection 265(1) continue to hold office
      until the earlier of the end of the term for which they were
      appointed and November 1, 2013.

    - ###### Board of referees --- members {#board-of-referees-members .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Persons on panels referred to in subsection
      111(3) of the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as it
      read immediately before the coming into force of section 247,
      continue to hold office until the earlier of the end of the term
      for which they were appointed and November 1, 2013.

    - ###### No compensation {#no-compensation .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite the provisions of any contract, agreement
      or order, no person either appointed to hold office as chairperson
      of a board of referees or referred to in subsection (2) has any
      right to claim or receive any compensation, damages, indemnity or
      other form of relief from Her Majesty in right of Canada or from
      any employee or agent of Her Majesty for ceasing to hold that
      office or for the abolition of that office by the operation of
      this Division.

    - ###### Earlier date {#earlier-date .MarginalNote}

      [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections (1) and (2), the
      Governor in Council may, by order, fix a day earlier than November
      1, 2013.

- --- 2012, c. 19, s. 264

  - ###### Umpires {#umpires .MarginalNote}

    - [264]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Umpires continue to hold office
      until April 1, 2014.

    - ###### No compensation {#no-compensation-1 .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite the provisions of any contract, agreement
      or order, no person appointed to hold office as umpire has any
      right to claim or receive any compensation, damages, indemnity or
      other form of relief from Her Majesty in right of Canada or from
      any employee or agent of Her Majesty for ceasing to hold that
      office or for the abolition of that office by the operation of
      this Division.

- --- 2012, c. 19, s. 265

  - ###### Appeals --- board of referees {#appeals-board-of-referees .MarginalNote}

    - [265]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The board of referees remains
      seized of any appeal filed and not decided before April 1, 2013
      under subsection 114(1) of the [Employment Insurance
      Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as it read immediately before the coming
      into force of section 247.

    - ###### Time limit {#time-limit .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} The board of referees must make its decision no
      later than October 31, 2013 or, if an order is made under
      subsection 263(4), the day before the day fixed by that order.

    - ###### Failure to decide {#failure-to-decide .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} The General Division of the Social Security
      Tribunal becomes seized of any appeal referred to in
      subsection (1) if no decision has been made by the day referred to
      in subsection (2). The appeal is deemed to be an appeal filed with
      the General Division of the Social Security Tribunal on November
      1, 2013 or, if an order is made under subsection 263(4), the day
      fixed by that order.

    - ###### Appeals --- Social Security Tribunal {#appeals-social-security-tribunal .MarginalNote}

      [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person who is dissatisfied with a decision made
      under subsection (1) may appeal the decision to the Appeal
      Division of the Social Security Tribunal.

- --- 2012, c. 19, s. 266

  - ###### Appeals --- umpire {#appeals-umpire .MarginalNote}

    [266]{.lawlabel} An appeal from a decision of a board of referees
    that could have been appealed to an umpire, but for the repeal of
    subsection 115(1) of the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6)
    by section 247, may be appealed to the Appeal Division of the Social
    Security Tribunal.

- --- 2012, c. 19, s. 267

  - ###### Appeals --- umpire {#appeals-umpire-1 .MarginalNote}

    - [267]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} An umpire remains seized of any
      appeal filed and heard before April 1, 2013 under subsection
      115(1) of the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as it
      read immediately before the coming into force of section 247.

    - ###### Time limit {#time-limit-1 .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} An umpire must make his or her decision no later
      than March 31, 2014.

    - ###### Failure to decide {#failure-to-decide-1 .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Appeal Division of the Social Security
      Tribunal becomes seized of any appeal referred to in
      subsection (1) if no decision has been made by the day referred to
      in subsection (2). The Appeal Division of the Social Security
      Tribunal is deemed to have granted leave to appeal on April 1,
      2014.

- --- 2012, c. 19, s. 268

  - ###### Appeals --- Social Security Tribunal {#appeals-social-security-tribunal-1 .MarginalNote}

    [268]{.lawlabel} The Appeal Division of the Social Security Tribunal
    is deemed to have granted leave to appeal on April 1, 2013 with
    respect to any appeal filed and not heard before April 1, 2013 under
    subsection 115(1) of the [Employment Insurance
    Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as it read immediately before the coming into
    force of section 247.

- --- 2012, c. 19, s. 269

  - ###### Request for reconsideration {#request-for-reconsideration .MarginalNote}

    - [269]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} If no decision has been made
      before April 1, 2013 in respect of a request made under section
      120 of the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6) as it read
      immediately before the coming into force of section 247, it is
      deemed to be an application made on April 1, 2013 under section 66
      of the Department of Human Resources and Skills Development Act
      and is deemed to relate to a decision made, as the case may be, by

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the General Division of the Social Security
        Tribunal, in the case of a decision made by a board of referees;
        or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Appeal Division of the Social Security
        Tribunal, in the case of a decision made by an umpire.

    - ###### Deeming {#deeming .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application made under section 66 of the
      Department of Human Resources and Skills Development Act after
      March 31, 2013 is deemed to relate to a decision made, as the case
      may be, by

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the General Division of the Social Security
        Tribunal, in the case of a decision made by a board of referees;
        or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Appeal Division of the Social Security
        Tribunal, in the case of a decision made by an umpire.

- --- 2012, c. 19, s. 270

  - ###### Continued application {#continued-application .MarginalNote}

    [270]{.lawlabel} The provisions of the [Employment Insurance
    Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6) repealed by this Act, and their related
    regulations, continue to apply to appeals of which the board of
    referees or an umpire remains seized under this Act, with any
    necessary adaptations.

- --- 2012, c. 19, s. 616

  - ###### Transitional {#transitional-2 .MarginalNote}

    [616]{.lawlabel} Section 14 of the [Employment Insurance
    Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as amended by section 604, applies only to
    claimants whose benefit period is established on or after April 7,
    2013.

- --- 2012, c. 27, s. 32

  - ###### Illness, injury or quarantine {#illness-injury-or-quarantine .MarginalNote}

    [32]{.lawlabel} Sections 18, 21 and 152.03 of the [Employment
    Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as amended by sections 15, 16 and
    21, apply only to claims for benefits because of illness, injury or
    quarantine that are made for weeks that begin on or after the day on
    which sections 15, 16 and 21 come into force.

- --- 2012, c. 27, s. 33

  - ###### Child's critical illness {#childs-critical-illness .MarginalNote}

    [33]{.lawlabel} Sections 23.2 and 152.061 of the [Employment
    Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as enacted by sections 18 and 23,
    apply to a claimant for any benefit period

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that begins on or after the day on which sections
      18 and 23 come into force; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that has not ended before that day, but only for
      weeks of benefits that begin on or after that day.

- --- 2012, c. 31, s. 456

  - ###### Surplus {#surplus .MarginalNote}

    [456]{.lawlabel} Any surplus of financial assets that remains after
    the satisfaction of the debts and liabilities of the Board on the
    day on which section 454 comes into force is to be paid into the
    Consolidated Revenue Fund and credited to the Employment Insurance
    Operating Account established under section 70.2 of the [Employment
    Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6).

- --- 2013, c. 35, s. 4

  - ###### Transitional {#transitional-3 .MarginalNote}

    [4]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs 10(10)(a) and 152.11(11)(a) of the
    [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as enacted by sections
    2 and 3, respectively, do not apply to the benefit period of a
    claimant or a self-employed person, as the case may be, in respect
    of any week for which the claimant or the self-employed person was
    confined in a jail, penitentiary or other similar institution before
    the coming into force of this Act.

- --- 2014, c. 20, s. 250

  - ###### Illness, injury or quarantine {#illness-injury-or-quarantine-1 .MarginalNote}

    [250]{.lawlabel} Subsections 18(2) and 152.03(1.1) of the
    [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as enacted by sections
    247 and 248, apply only to claims for benefits because of illness,
    injury or quarantine that are made for weeks that begin on or after
    the day on which sections 247 and 248 come into force.

- --- 2015, c. 36, s. 79

  - ###### Compassionate care benefits {#compassionate-care-benefits .MarginalNote}

    - [79]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a period referred to in
      subsection 23.1(4) of the [Employment Insurance
      Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as it read immediately before January 3,
      2016, has begun in respect of a claimant --- as defined in
      subsection 2(1) of that Act --- before that day but has not ended,
      then sections 12 and 23.1 of that Act, as they read on that day,
      apply to that claimant beginning on that day.

    - ###### Compassionate care benefits --- self-employed persons {#compassionate-care-benefits-self-employed-persons .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a period referred to in subsection 152.06(3)
      of the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as it read
      immediately before January 3, 2016, has begun in respect of a
      self-employed person --- as defined in subsection 152.01(1) of
      that Act --- before that day but has not ended, then sections
      152.06 and 152.14 of that Act, as they read on that day, apply to
      that person beginning on that day.

- --- 2016, c. 7, s. 225

  - ###### Provisions continue to apply {#provisions-continue-to-apply .MarginalNote}

    [225]{.lawlabel} The following provisions of the [Employment
    Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as those provisions read
    immediately before July 9, 2017, continue to apply in respect of a
    [[claimant]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection 2(1) of

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subsections 10(13.1) to (14.1); and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subsections 12(2) to (2.8) and (6).

- --- 2016, c. 7, s. 228

  - ###### Retroactive regulations {#retroactive-regulations .MarginalNote}

    [228]{.lawlabel} Regulations made by the Canada Employment Insurance
    Commission under the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6)
    that, in the opinion of the Commission, are necessary as a result of
    the amendments made by sections 208, 213, 214, 219 and 221 may, if
    the regulations so provide, be retroactive and have effect with
    respect to any period before they are made that begins on or after
    the day that is fixed by order of the Governor in Council made under
    subsection 231(4).

- --- 2016, c. 7, s. 229

  - ###### Non-application of subsections 153(3) and (4) {#non-application-of-subsections-1533-and-4 .MarginalNote}

    [229]{.lawlabel} Subsections 153(3) and (4) of the [Employment
    Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6) do not apply in respect of any
    regulations made by the Canada Employment Insurance Commission under
    that Act that, in the opinion of the Commission, are necessary as a
    result of the amendments made by subsection 207(3) and sections 209,
    210, 216, 220 and 230.

- --- 2017, c. 20, s. 254

  - ###### Birth or placement for adoption {#birth-or-placement-for-adoption .MarginalNote}

    [254]{.lawlabel} The [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as
    it read immediately before the day on which sections 235 and 245
    come into force, continues to apply to a claimant for the purpose of
    paying benefits under section 23 or 152.05 of that Act in respect of
    a child or children who are, before that day, born or placed with
    the claimant for the purpose of adoption.

- --- 2017, c. 20, s. 255

  - ###### Critically ill child {#critically-ill-child .MarginalNote}

    [255]{.lawlabel} The [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as
    it read immediately before the day on which sections 237 and 247
    come into force, continues to apply to a claimant for the purpose of

- --- 2017, c. 20, s. 256

  - ###### Critically ill adult {#critically-ill-adult .MarginalNote}

    [256]{.lawlabel} Sections 23.3 and 152.062 of the [Employment
    Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as enacted by sections 238 and 248,

- --- 2018, c. 12, s. 292

  - ###### Words and expressions {#words-and-expressions .MarginalNote}

    - [292]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Words and expressions used in
      sections 293 to 295 have the same meaning as in the [Employment
      Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6).

    - ###### Definition of [[Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} {#definition-of-regulations .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} In sections 293 to 295,
      [[Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Employment
      Insurance Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-332) as they read
      on August 11, 2018.

- --- 2018, c. 12, s. 293

  - ###### Claimants eligible to make election {#claimants-eligible-to-make-election .MarginalNote}

    - [293]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} A claimant who was eligible to
      make an election under subsection 77.991(2) of the Regulations in
      respect of a benefit period and who did not, before August 12,
      2018, make such an election, may, after August 11, 2018, make an
      election under subsection (2) in respect of that benefit period if

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the last notification of payment or non-payment
        of benefits in respect of that benefit period is issued to the
        claimant after July 13, 2018; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the claimant receives earnings during one or
        more weeks of unemployment included in that benefit period and
        makes a claim for benefits for at least one of those weeks.

    - ###### Election {#election .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} A claimant to whom this section applies may
      elect, in respect of earnings received during all weeks of
      unemployment that fall in the benefit period referred to in
      subsection (1), to have deducted from benefits the amount, if any,
      of the earnings that exceeds the following instead of the amount
      described in subsection 19(2) of the [Employment Insurance
      Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6):

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$75, if the claimant's rate of weekly benefits
        is less than \$188; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 40% of the claimant's rate of weekly benefits,
        if that rate is \$188 or more.

    - ###### Requirement to inform Commission {#requirement-to-inform-commission .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} A claimant must inform the Commission of their
      election, if any, in respect of earnings received during all weeks
      of unemployment included in the benefit period not later than the
      30th day after the day on which the last notification of payment
      or non-payment of benefits in respect of one or more weeks of
      unemployment included in that benefit period is issued to the
      claimant. The election is irrevocable.

    - ###### Late election {#late-election .MarginalNote}

      [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant informs the Commission of their
      election after the day on which the deadline expires, the election
      is considered to have been made by that deadline if the claimant
      shows that there was good cause for the delay throughout the
      period that begins on the day on which the deadline expired and
      that ends on the day on which the claimant informs the Commission
      of their election.

    - ###### No reconsideration of decision {#no-reconsideration-of-decision .MarginalNote}

      [(5)]{.lawlabel} A decision of the Commission in respect of any
      matter related to an election, including the failure to make an
      election, is not subject to reconsideration under section 112 of
      the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6).

- --- 2018, c. 12, s. 294

  - ###### Election made before August 12, 2018 {#election-made-before-august-12-2018 .MarginalNote}

    [294]{.lawlabel} An election that is made before August 12, 2018
    under subsection 77.991(2) of the Regulations in respect of a
    benefit period is deemed to apply to earnings received during all
    weeks of unemployment that are included in the portion of the
    benefit period that falls in the period that begins on August 12,
    2018 and that ends on August 14, 2021.

- --- 2018, c. 12, s. 295

  - ###### Claimants eligible to make election {#claimants-eligible-to-make-election-1 .MarginalNote}

    - [295]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} This section applies to

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a claimant who made an election under section
        77.98 of the Regulations in respect of a benefit period, if the
        last notification of payment or non-payment of benefits in
        respect of that benefit period was issued to them after August
        6, 2016;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a claimant who made an election under
        subsection 77.991(2) of the Regulations; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a claimant who makes an election under section
        293.

    - ###### Election {#election-1 .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} A claimant to whom this section applies may
      elect, in respect of earnings received during all weeks of
      unemployment before August 15, 2021 that fall in a benefit period
      established after August 11, 2018 but before August 15, 2021, to
      have deducted from benefits the amount, if any, of the earnings
      that exceeds the following instead of the amount described in
      subsection 19(2) or 152.18(2), as the case may be, of the
      [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6):

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} \$75, if the claimant's rate of weekly benefits
        is less than \$188; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 40% of the claimant's rate of weekly benefits,
        if that rate is \$188 or more.

    - ###### Requirement to inform Commission {#requirement-to-inform-commission-1 .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} A claimant must inform the Commission of their
      election, if any, in respect of earnings received during all weeks
      of unemployment included in a given benefit period not later than
      the 30th day after the day on which the last notification of
      payment or non-payment of benefits in respect of one or more weeks
      of unemployment included in that benefit period is issued to the
      claimant or February 19, 2022, whichever is earlier. The election
      is irrevocable.

    - ###### Late election {#late-election-1 .MarginalNote}

      [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a claimant informs the Commission of their
      election after the day on which the deadline expires, the election
      is considered to have been made by that deadline if the claimant
      shows that there was good cause for the delay throughout the
      period that begins on the day on which the deadline expired and
      that ends on the day on which the claimant informs the Commission
      of their election.

    - ###### No reconsideration of decision {#no-reconsideration-of-decision-1 .MarginalNote}

      [(5)]{.lawlabel} A decision of the Commission in respect of any
      matter related to an election, including the failure to make an
      election, is not subject to reconsideration under section 112 of
      the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6).

- --- 2023, c. 26, s. 664

  - ###### Definitions {#definitions-1 .MarginalNote}

    [664]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in this section and
    sections 665 to 678.

    [[Appeal Division]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[Appeal Division]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Appeal
        Division of the Tribunal. ([division d'appel]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

    [[Board of Appeal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[Board of Appeal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Employment
        Insurance Board of Appeal established under section 43.01 of the
        [Department of Employment and Social Development
        Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7). ([Conseil d'appel]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

    [[Employment Insurance Section]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[Employment Insurance Section]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
        Employment Insurance Section of the General Division. ([section
        de l'assurance-emploi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[General Division]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[General Division]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except for the
        purposes of subsections 665(2) and 667(2), the General Division
        referred to in section 44 of the [Department of Employment and
        Social Development Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7), as it reads on the day
        on which this section comes into force. ([division
        générale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[Tribunal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[Tribunal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Social Security
        Tribunal established under section 44 of the [Department of
        Employment and Social Development
        Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7). ([Tribunal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

## AMENDMENTS NOT IN FORCE {#amendments-not-in-force .scheduleTitleText}

- --- 2000, c. 12, s. 107(1.1), as amended by 2017, c. 20, s. 257(2)

  - - [107]{.lawlabel} [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 23(2) of the Act is
      replaced by the following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - ###### Weeks for which benefits may be paid {#weeks-for-which-benefits-may-be-paid .MarginalNote}

        [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 12, benefits under this
        section are payable for each week of unemployment in the period
        that begins with the week in which

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the child or children of the claimant are
          born,

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the child or children are actually placed
          with the claimant for the purpose of adoption, or

        - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the claimant first meets the requirements set
          out in the regulations made under paragraph 54(f.1)

        and ends 52 weeks after that week.
      :::
      ::::

- --- 2009, c. 33, s. 34

  - ###### [2000, c. 14]{.HistoricalNote} {#c.-14 .MarginalNote}

    [34]{.lawlabel} On the first day on which both section 10 of the
    [Budget Implementation Act, 2000](/eng/acts/B-9.84) has produced its
    effects and subsection 7(2) of this Act is in force, subsection
    23(6) of the [Employment Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as enacted
    by that subsection 7(2), is renumbered as subsection (7) and is
    repositioned accordingly if required.

- --- 2023, c. 26, s. 662

  - [662]{.lawlabel} Section 113 of the [Employment Insurance
    Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6) is replaced by the following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Appeal to Employment Insurance Board of Appeal {#appeal-to-employment-insurance-board-of-appeal .MarginalNote}

      [113]{.lawlabel} A party who is dissatisfied with a decision of
      the Commission made under section 112, including a decision in
      relation to further time to make a request, may appeal the
      decision to the Employment Insurance Board of Appeal established
      under section 43.01 of the [Department of Employment and Social
      Development Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7).
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2023, c. 26, s. 663

  - - [663]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 114(1) of the Act is
      replaced by the following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - ###### Payment of benefit pending appeal {#payment-of-benefit-pending-appeal .MarginalNote}

        - [114]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a claim for benefits is
          allowed by the General Division of the Social Security
          Tribunal established under section 44 of the [Department of
          Employment and Social Development Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7) or the
          Employment Insurance Board of Appeal established under section
          43.01 of that Act, benefits are payable in accordance with the
          decision of the Tribunal or the Employment Insurance Board of
          Appeal, as the case may be, even though an appeal is pending,
          and any benefits paid under this section after the decision of
          the Tribunal or the Employment Insurance Board of Appeal, as
          the case may be, are to be treated as having been duly paid
          and are not recoverable from the claimant, even if the final
          determination of the question is adverse to the claimant.
      :::
      ::::

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 114(1) of the Act is replaced by the
      following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - ###### Payment of benefit pending appeal {#payment-of-benefit-pending-appeal-1 .MarginalNote}

        - [114]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a claim for benefits is
          allowed by the Employment Insurance Board of Appeal
          established under section 43.01 of the [Department of
          Employment and Social Development Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7),
          benefits are payable in accordance with the decision of the
          Employment Insurance Board of Appeal even though an appeal is
          pending, and any benefits paid under this section after the
          decision of the Employment Insurance Board of Appeal are to be
          treated as having been duly paid and are not recoverable from
          the claimant, even if the final determination of the question
          is adverse to the claimant.
      :::
      ::::

    - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph 114(2)(a) of the Act is replaced by the
      following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the appeal to the Appeal Division of the
        Social Security Tribunal was brought within 21 days after the
        day on which the decision of the General Division of the Social
        Security Tribunal or the Employment Insurance Board of Appeal,
        as the case may be, was given and on the ground that the
        claimant ought to be disentitled under section 36; and
      :::
      ::::

    - [(4)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph 114(2)(a) of the Act is replaced by the
      following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the appeal to the Appeal Division of the

- --- 2023, c. 26, s. 676

    - [676]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Section 241 of the [Budget

    - ###### Section 66 of [Department of Employment and Social Development Act](/eng/acts/H-5.7) {#section-66-of-department-of-employment-and-social-development-act .MarginalNote}

- --- 2023, c. 26, s. 677

    Insurance Act](/eng/acts/E-5.6), as those subsections read on the

- --- 2024, c. 15, s. 345

  - [345]{.lawlabel} The Act is amended by adding the following after
    section 22:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Benefit for responsibilities related to child's placement or arrival {#benefit-for-responsibilities-related-to-childs-placement-or-arrival .MarginalNote}

      - [22.1]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 18, but
        subject to this section, benefits are payable to a major
        attachment claimant for carrying out responsibilities related to

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the placement with the claimant of one or
          more children for the purpose of adoption under the laws
          governing adoption in the province in which the claimant
          resides; or

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the arrival of one or more new-born children
          of the claimant into the claimant's care, in the case where
          the person who will be giving or gave birth to the child or
          children is not, or is not intended to be, a parent of the
          child or children.

      - ###### Weeks for which benefits may be paid {#weeks-for-which-benefits-may-be-paid-1 .MarginalNote}

        [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 12, benefits under this
        section are payable for each week of unemployment in the period

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that begins the earlier of

          - [(i)]{.lawlabel} five weeks before the week in which the
            placement of the child or children with the claimant for the
            purpose of adoption is expected or the new-born child or
            children of the claimant are expected to arrive into the
            claimant's care, and

          - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the week in which the child or children
            are actually placed with the claimant for the purpose of
            adoption or the new-born child or children of the claimant
            actually arrive into the claimant's care; and

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that ends 17 weeks after the week in which
          the child or children are actually placed with the claimant
          for the purpose of adoption or the new-born child or children
          of the claimant actually arrive into the claimant's care.

      - ###### Limitation --- delay of placement or arrival {#limitation-delay-of-placement-or-arrival .MarginalNote}

        [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the placement or arrival of the child or
        children referred to in subsection (1) is delayed, the period
        referred to in subsection (2) must not, subject to any extension
        under subsection (4), exceed 52 weeks after the week in which
        the placement or arrival was expected.

      - ###### Extension of period --- children in hospital {#extension-of-period-children-in-hospital .MarginalNote}

        [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the child or children referred to in
        subsection (1) are hospitalized during the period that begins
        the week referred to in subparagraph (2)(a)(ii) and that ends 17
        weeks after that week, the period referred to in subsection (2)
        is extended by the number of weeks during which the child or
        children are hospitalized.

      - ###### Limitation --- children in hospital {#limitation-children-in-hospital .MarginalNote}

        [(5)]{.lawlabel} The extended period shall end no later than 52
        weeks after the week referred to in subparagraph (2)(a)(ii).

      - ###### Limitation {#limitation .MarginalNote}

        [(6)]{.lawlabel} If benefits are payable to a claimant for the
        reasons set out in this section and any allowances, money or
        other benefits are payable to the claimant for the same reasons
        under a provincial law, the benefits payable to the claimant
        under this Act are to be reduced or eliminated as prescribed.

      - ###### Application of section 18 {#application-of-section-18 .MarginalNote}

        [(7)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 13, the provisions
        of section 18 do not apply to the week that immediately precedes
        the period described in subsection (2).

      - ###### Division of weeks of benefits {#division-of-weeks-of-benefits .MarginalNote}

        [(8)]{.lawlabel} If two major attachment claimants each make a
        claim for benefits under this section --- or if one major
        attachment claimant makes a claim for benefits under this
        section and an individual makes a claim for benefits under
        section 152.041 --- in respect of the same child or children,
        the weeks of benefits payable under this section, under section
        152.041 or under both those sections may be divided between them
        up to a maximum of 15. If they cannot agree, the weeks of
        benefits are to be divided in accordance with the prescribed
        rules.

      - ###### Maximum number of weeks that can be divided {#maximum-number-of-weeks-that-can-be-divided .MarginalNote}

        [(9)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, if, in respect of the
        same child or children, a major attachment claimant makes a
        claim for benefits under this section and an individual makes a
        claim for benefits under section 152.041, the total number of
        weeks of benefits payable under this section and section 152.041
        that may be divided between them may not exceed 15.

      - ###### Deferral of waiting period {#deferral-of-waiting-period .MarginalNote}

        [(10)]{.lawlabel} A major attachment claimant who makes a claim
        for benefits under this section may have their waiting period
        deferred until they make another claim for benefits in the same
        benefit period, otherwise than under this section, if

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the claimant has already made a claim for
          benefits under this section in respect of the same child or
          children and has served the waiting period;

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} another major attachment claimant has made a
          claim for benefits under this section in respect of the same
          child or children and that other claimant has served or is
          serving their waiting period;

        - [(c)]{.lawlabel} another major attachment claimant is making a
          claim for benefits under this section in respect of the same
          child or children at the same time as the claimant and that
          other claimant elects to serve the waiting period; or

        - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the claimant or another major attachment
          claimant meets the prescribed requirements.

      - ###### Exception {#exception-1 .MarginalNote}

        [(11)]{.lawlabel} If a major attachment claimant makes a claim
        under this section and an individual makes a claim under section
        152.041 in respect of the same child or children and one of them
        has served or elected to serve their waiting period, then

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the major attachment claimant is not the
          one who served or elected to serve the waiting period, that
          claimant is not required to serve a waiting period; or

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the individual is not the one who served
          or elected to serve the waiting period, that claimant may have
          their waiting period deferred in accordance with section
          152.041.
    :::
    ::::
